# QuantEdge — Whole-Platform Design Blueprint

> **Identity:** Fortress-style institutional trading terminal (dense, data-first, dark) **+ QuantEdge cyan accent** (`--brand-cyan #22d3ee`) **+ a premium 3D / motion layer** (signature: a React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface; animated product shots; framer-motion micro-interactions).

**Status:** assembled from the design workflow's completed output — **5/5 design-law pillars** and **58 surface audits** (556 violations cataloged). Component-group audits (16) + shells (6) + the auto-synthesis were not reached before the run stalled; they're listed under *Pending coverage* and filled next. Nothing here is invented — every spec came from an agent that read the actual file.

---

## 0. The Law (one-screen summary)

# DESIGN LAW SUMMARY

**Foundation.** All color/surface/border/text/accent/focus values come from CSS tokens in `client/src/index.css` (`:root` light, `.dark` dark). Never hardcode hex/rgb in components; consume `hsl(var(--token))` or named tokens. Dark-first: design in dark, light is a derived inverse.

**Color.** Surfaces are neutral grayscale with a strict 4-step elevation ladder (dark L0 `--background` #0a0a0a → L1 `--card` #121212 → L2 `--secondary` 11% → L3 `--surface-overlay-solid` 13%; depth via bg-step + `--border`/`--border-strong`, not glow). Light = inverse, depth via shadow. Exactly ONE chromatic accent: brand cyan `--brand-cyan #22d3ee` for focus/active/selection/links/brand/primary CTA. Semantic `--trade-bullish #10b981` / `--trade-bearish #ef4444` / `--trade-neutral #f59e0b` encode ONLY money/direction — never chrome, decoration, or generic success/error. Teal `#14b8a6` is demoted to the logo stripe + GEX surface only.

**Typography.** Two families only: **IBM Plex Sans** (`--font-sans`, weights 400/500/600/700) for all words; **IBM Plex Mono** (`--font-mono`, 400/500/600) for EVERY number (price, %, ticker, strike, GEX, Greeks, time) with `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums` + slashed zero. Fixed scale: micro 9px / data-xs 11px / data-sm 12px / data-base 13px / data-lg 15px; price-sm/md/lg for hero numbers. Micro-labels = UPPERCASE sans 500, tracking 0.05–0.08em, muted. Tickers = uppercase mono 600, +0.04em.

**Density.** Compact is the default (Bloomberg-dense: card pad 8–10px, row 28px, base 12px), driven by one `data-density` attribute (compact/comfortable/spacious). Density changes spacing/size ONLY — never color, accent, or elevation.

**3D/Motion signature.** Micro-interactions: framer-motion 150–300ms on `--ease-out-smooth` cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1); default hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98). NO ambient/decorative glow. Springs and Aceternity/Magic effects are landing-only, banned in-terminal. The ONE signature 3D moment is the React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface (lazy-loaded, cyan-lit peaks/purple valleys, OrbitControls clamped, 2D fallback). All motion respects `prefers-reduced-motion`. Icons: lucide only, no emoji.

## 1. The Five Pillars

### COLOR, ELEVATION & THEME

QuantEdge is a DARK-FIRST, FORTRESS-style institutional trading terminal: dense, data-first, near-black surfaces with a strict 4-step elevation ladder, exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan #22d3ee), and semantic green/red/amber reserved EXCLUSIVELY for P&L and directional meaning. A premium 3D + motion layer (GEX gamma surface, Framer-motion micro-interactions, subtle inner-light depth) sits ON TOP of this disciplined palette and must never recolor it.

THE CORE LAW IN ONE SENTENCE: surfaces are neutral grayscale, the only chromatic UI color is cyan (selection/focus/active/links/brand), and green/red/amber may appear ONLY where they encode money or direction — never as decoration, never as the app accent.

The existing index.css foundation is sound (semantic --trade-* tokens, --brand-cyan, radius/spacing/density scales, light+dark) but is INTERNALLY INCONSISTENT: the declared accent is cyan #22d3ee yet nearly every interactive effect (focus rings, --ring, --accent, --ai-primary, selection, card-hover glow, grids, magnetic buttons) actually paints TEAL #14b8a6 / EMERALD #10b981. Emerald is also --trade-bullish, so green is illegally doing double duty as both brand accent AND P&L meaning. This law resolves that conflict by making cyan the sole accent and demoting teal/emerald to: teal = a discouraged decorative-gradient partner only inside the brand mark and the GEX 3D surface; emerald = P&L-bullish ONLY. Light mode is a faithful inverse, not a separate identity.

| Rule | Spec |
|---|---|
| **Dark-first authoring. Dark is the canonical theme; light is a derived inverse. Every component must be designed and reviewed in dark mode first. Both themes are driven by the same CSS variables on :root (light) and .dark (dark) in client/src/index.css — never hardcode a hex/rgb for a surface, border, text, accent, or focus color in a component. Reference HSL tokens via hsl(var(--token)) or the named hex tokens.** | Source of truth = client/src/index.css :root + .dark blocks. Components consume tokens only. Any raw color literal in a .tsx/.css component that is not inside an approved 3D/GEX visualization is a violation. |
| **Dark elevation ladder — exactly 4 steps, neutral grayscale only, no chroma in surfaces. Backgrounds get LIGHTER as they come forward; borders are the separator, shadow is secondary. Lock these dark values: L0 page = --background 0 0% 4% (#0a0a0a); L1 card/panel = --card 0 0% 7% (#121212); L2 raised/header/popover/hover = --popover 0 0% 7% bumped to 0 0% 9% for hover, --secondary 0 0% 11%; L3 overlay/modal/cmdk = a new --surface-overlay-solid 0 0% 13%. Borders: hairline --border 0 0% 13%, stronger --border-strong (NEW) 0 0% 18%. Never tint a surface with cyan/teal/green.** | Add --surface-overlay-solid: 0 0% 13% and --border-strong: 0 0% 18% to .dark. Elevation is expressed primarily by background lightness step (+3 to +4 L per level) and border, NOT by colored glow. Keep existing --surface-base #0a0a0b / --surface-raised #111113 as aliases mapping into this ladder. |
| **Light elevation ladder is the inverse and equally neutral. L0 page = 0 0% 100%, L1 card = 0 0% 100% with --card-border 0 0% 92%, L2 raised = --surface-raised #f8fafc, L3 overlay 0 0% 100% with --border-strong-light 0 0% 85%. Shadows carry depth in light (existing --shadow-* flat scale); borders carry depth in dark.** | Light keeps existing values; add --border-strong: 0 0% 85% override inside :root. Depth cue priority: light = shadow-first, dark = border+bg-step-first. |
| **ONE accent only = brand cyan --brand-cyan #22d3ee. Cyan is the sole chromatic UI color and is used for: focus rings, keyboard-focus/active nav state, primary interactive emphasis (selected tab/row marker, active filter pill, links, cmd+K match highlight), brand mark, and the single primary CTA accent. It is NOT a status color and NEVER means up/down/profit. Re-point every currently teal/emerald INTERFACE effect to cyan.** | Override in .dark: --ring: 187 92% 53% (cyan, replaces 160 84% 39% teal); --accent: 187 92% 53%; --accent-foreground: 0 0% 4%; --ai-primary: #22d3ee (replaces #10b981); --sidebar-ring: 187 92% 53%. selection background -> rgba(34,211,238,0.22) (replaces rgba(20,184,166,0.25)). terminal-input:focus border-color -> var(--brand-cyan), box-shadow -> 0 0 0 2px rgba(34,211,238,0.35) (replaces teal). Sidebar active gradient/glow -> cyan rgba(34,211,238,*) (replaces rgba(20,184,166,*)). |
| **Semantic trade colors are LOCKED to meaning and forbidden elsewhere. --trade-bullish #10b981 (up / profit / long / buy), --trade-bearish #ef4444 (down / loss / short / sell), --trade-neutral #f59e0b (flat / caution / pending / partial). These three may color ONLY: P&L numbers, % change, price-tick flashes, direction badges (LONG/SHORT/CALL/PUT), buy/sell controls, and confidence-as-direction. They must NEVER be used for generic UI chrome, decorative gradients, section accents, grids, or 'success/error' toasts that aren't about money/direction (use neutral + cyan for those instead).** | Keep --trade-* hex values as-is. Remove green/emerald from non-semantic decoration: graph-grid / emerald-grid / dot-grid / graph-grid-subtle must switch from rgba(16,185,129,*) to neutral rgba(255,255,255,0.04) (dark) / rgba(0,0,0,0.04) (light) or cyan-at-very-low-alpha for brand pages only. data-badge/terminal-data/quant-card emerald borders -> neutral border + optional cyan accent. confidence-bar fills keep green/blue ONLY when bar literally encodes bullish confidence. |
| **Focus rings are cyan, visible, and uniform. Every focusable element shows a 2px cyan ring on :focus-visible: outline none + box-shadow 0 0 0 2px hsl(var(--background)), 0 0 0 4px var(--brand-cyan) (offset ring) OR ring-2 ring-[--brand-cyan] via Tailwind. Same ring in light and dark. Never remove focus for aesthetics; never use a green or teal focus ring.** | Standardize on --ring = cyan (187 92% 53%). Default :focus-visible ring = 2px cyan with 2px background-colored offset for separation on dense rows. Mouse :focus (non-keyboard) may suppress the ring; :focus-visible must not. |
| **Glow is RARE, cyan-or-semantic, and signal-bearing — never ambient default chrome. Allowed glow: (a) the 3D GEX gamma surface and its markers; (b) live/active real-time status (cyan pulse) and price-tick flashes (semantic green/red, already defined); (c) the single primary CTA / active brand element on hover. Forbidden: global card-hover teal glow, magnetic-button colored glow on every button, badge-pro glow, generic 'premium' glows. Default card hover = border-color brighten to --border-strong + 1px translateY, NO colored box-shadow.** | Remove/neutralize: .dark .shadcn-card:hover glow rgba(20,184,166,0.06) -> use border-color shift to var(--border-strong) (no glow); .terminal-card:hover glow rgba(20,184,166,0.1) -> cyan rgba(34,211,238,0.08) ONLY if card is a live/brand surface, else border-only; .btn-magnetic colored shadow restricted to primary CTA class only. Glow color, when present, is cyan or a locked semantic — never teal. |
| **Teal #14b8a6 is demoted: brand-mark + GEX-surface gradient partner only. Teal/emerald gradients are permitted ONLY inside (a) the QuantEdge logo/wordmark and the 3px top body stripe brand gradient, and (b) the 3D GEX gamma surface palette (which has its own --gex-* and --projection-glow tokens and is exempt from the one-accent rule as a data visualization). Teal must not appear as a UI accent, focus, hover, or nav color anywhere else.** | Keep --brand-teal #14b8a6 and the body::before stripe (teal->cyan->teal) as the ONLY teal UI usage. GEX uses its dedicated --gex-positive/negative/mid/neutral + --projection-glow tokens. Everywhere else teal -> cyan. |
| **Numbers are monospace + tabular; words are sans. Every numeric value (price, %, P&L, strike, volume, GEX, confidence) uses --font-mono (JetBrains Mono) with font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums. All labels/prose use --font-sans (DM Sans). This is a color-adjacent identity rule because semantic color sits on these mono numbers.** | Use existing .data-cell / .price-display / .change-indicator / .ticker-font helpers for all numerics. Sans is the body default; mono is opt-in per number. Tabular alignment is mandatory in any column/grid of numbers. |
| **Compact density is the default; color/elevation tokens are density-invariant. The terminal ships in .density-compact by default with .density-comfortable / .density-spacious opt-ins. Density changes spacing/padding/font-size ONLY — it must never change colors, borders, elevation steps, or accent usage.** | Wrap app shell in density-compact by default. Density classes (already defined) tune --density-* spacing vars; they must not redefine --background/--card/--border/--brand-cyan/--trade-*. |
| **No emoji as icons; lucide-react only, sized + colored by token. Icons are line icons from lucide, default stroke = currentColor inheriting muted-foreground; only become cyan when active/selected or a locked semantic when they label direction/P&L. No emoji glyphs anywhere in UI chrome.** | Icon default color = hsl(var(--muted-foreground)); active/hover = var(--brand-cyan); directional icons (TrendingUp/Down) = --trade-bullish/bearish. Standard sizes 14/16/18px to match compact density. |

**Tokens:** `--background (dark 0 0% 4% / light 0 0% 100%) — L0 page` · `--card (dark 0 0% 7% / light 0 0% 100%) — L1 surface` · `--secondary (dark 0 0% 11%) — L2 raised` · `--surface-overlay-solid: 0 0% 13% (NEW, dark) — L3 modal/overlay/cmdk` · `--border (dark 0 0% 13% / light 0 0% 90%) — hairline separator` · `--border-strong: 0 0% 18% dark / 0 0% 85% light (NEW) — stronger separator + default hover border` · `--brand-cyan #22d3ee — THE single accent` · `--ring: 187 92% 53% (cyan, replaces dark teal 160 84% 39%) — focus` · `--accent: 187 92% 53% (cyan, replaces teal)` · `--accent-foreground: 0 0% 4% (on-cyan text)` · `--ai-primary: #22d3ee (replaces #10b981)` · `--brand-teal #14b8a6 — DEMOTED: brand mark + body stripe + GEX gradient only` · `--trade-bullish #10b981 — up/profit/long ONLY` · `--trade-bearish #ef4444 — down/loss/short ONLY` · `--trade-neutral #f59e0b — flat/caution/pending ONLY` · `--trade-bullish-bg / --trade-bearish-bg / --trade-neutral-bg — semantic backgrounds` · `--gex-positive / --gex-negative / --gex-mid / --gex-neutral — GEX 3D surface sub-palette (exempt)` · `--projection-glow / --projection-glow-soft — GEX Skylit arc glow` · `--grade-s/a/b/c/d/f — grade sub-palette (s=cyan), non-UI-accent` · `--glow-cyan rgba(34,211,238,0.08 dark) — the only sanctioned ambient glow color` · `--font-sans DM Sans — words` · `--font-mono JetBrains Mono — all numbers (tabular-nums)` · `--radius-card 0.625rem / --radius-button 0.5rem / --radius-badge 0.25rem` · `--density-compact (default) / comfortable / spacious — spacing only, color-invariant` · `--ease-out-smooth cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1) — single motion curve`

**Avoid:** Using teal #14b8a6 or emerald #10b981 as a UI accent, focus ring, hover state, or nav-active color — the file currently does this via --ring/--accent/--ai-primary/selection/terminal-input focus; all must move to cyan #22d3ee. · Letting green do double duty: emerald as both the brand/success color AND --trade-bullish. Green means PROFIT/UP only. · Painting any surface (page/card/panel/modal) with a chromatic tint instead of neutral grayscale elevation steps. · Ambient colored glow as default chrome — global card-hover teal glow, magnetic-button glow on every button, badge-pro glow, decorative 'premium' glows. Glow is reserved for GEX surface, live status, and price ticks. · Green/red/amber used for non-financial 'success/error/warning' UI (generic toasts, validation, decorative section accents) — use neutral + cyan instead; reserve semantics for money/direction. · Emerald-tinted grids/dot-grids/backgrounds (graph-grid, emerald-grid, dot-grid) — backgrounds must be neutral or ultra-low-alpha cyan on brand pages only. · Removing focus outlines for looks, or using a non-cyan focus ring; dense keyboard-driven terminal requires a uniform visible cyan :focus-visible ring. · Hardcoding hex/rgb surface, border, text, accent, or focus colors in components instead of consuming index.css tokens. · Introducing a second accent hue (blue, purple, gold) as interactive UI color — there is exactly one accent, cyan. Gold/blue/purple exist only in locked sub-palettes (GEX, grade system). · Proportional/non-tabular fonts for numbers, or monospace for prose — numbers are tabular mono, words are sans. · Density modes that alter colors, borders, or elevation rather than spacing/typography only. · Emoji used as icons or status indicators instead of lucide line icons.


### Typography & Number Formatting

QuantEdge's typographic identity is FORTRESS-style institutional density with a strict two-axis system: a humanist-neutral SANS carries every word, a tabular MONOSPACE carries every number. Recommendation: adopt IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono as the canonical pairing (matching Fortress's proven institutional read, while the cyan accent and 3D/motion layer differentiate the brand) — they are designed as a single superfamily so weights, metrics, and the slashed-zero align perfectly, and IBM Plex Mono's tabular figures are purpose-built for dense financial tables. This supersedes the current ad-hoc state (DM Sans + a JetBrains-Mono var that isn't even loaded — index.html ships Space Mono, so mono silently falls back). The non-negotiable law: EVERY number, price, percent, ticker, strike, expiry, GEX value, Greek, and timestamp renders in mono with font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums (slashed-zero on) so digits column-align and never reflow on tick. Prose, labels, buttons, and nav render in sans. One accent only (--brand-cyan #22d3ee); lucide icons only, never emoji. Compact density is the default. The existing index.css already encodes most of this (.data-cell, .price-display, .ticker-font, micro/data-* scale, uppercase .stat-label) — the work is to consolidate fonts to IBM Plex, wire the @font-face/Google load, point --font-mono at the font that's actually loaded, and codify the rules below as enforced law rather than convention.

Key files: /Users/abdulmalik/UnTitld/QuantEdgeee/client/src/index.css (tokens --font-sans/--font-mono at lines 272-274 and 431-433, dual light/dark; .data-cell L838, .price-display L989, .ticker-font L848, .stat-label L873); /Users/abdulmalik/UnTitld/QuantEdgeee/tailwind.config.ts (fontSize scale L38-48, fontFamily L124-128); /Users/abdulmalik/UnTitld/QuantEdgeee/client/index.html (font <link> L42-49 — currently DM Sans + Space Mono).

| Rule | Spec |
|---|---|
| **Adopt IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono as the canonical font pairing** | Set --font-sans: 'IBM Plex Sans', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif and --font-mono: 'IBM Plex Mono', ui-monospace, 'SF Mono', Menlo, monospace in BOTH :root blocks of client/src/index.css (light L272-274 and dark L431-433). Load both from Google Fonts in client/index.html with display=swap and a preconnect, weights Sans 400;500;600;700 and Mono 400;500;600. Delete the stale DM Sans / Space Mono / JetBrains references. Tailwind already maps font-sans/font-mono to these vars (tailwind.config.ts L124-128), so no Tailwind change is needed. |
| **EVERY number renders in mono with tabular, lining figures** | Any numeric glyph — price, %, $, ticker, strike, expiry, DTE, GEX, Greeks (delta/gamma/theta/vega), IV, volume, OI, P&L, timestamps, counts — MUST use font-family: var(--font-mono) with font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums and font-feature-settings: 'tnum' 1, 'lnum' 1, 'zero' 1 (slashed zero). Enforce via the existing .data-cell utility (index.css L838) and a <Num>/<Price> QE primitive that applies it; sans is forbidden for numerals. Prose, labels, buttons, nav use var(--font-sans). |
| **Type scale is fixed and data-dense (compact default)** | Use only the defined steps from tailwind.config.ts L38-48: micro 9px (metric captions), data-xs 11px (table cells), data-sm 12px (stat values), data-base 13px (body data — default), data-lg 15px (emphasized). Numeric hero sizes use price-sm 16px / price-md 20px / price-lg 24px with negative letter-spacing (-0.02 to -0.03em) per the existing .price-display tiers (index.css L989-1010). Section/page headings step up from data-lg via sans semibold. No arbitrary font sizes outside this scale. |
| **Weights are constrained to a four-stop ramp** | Sans: 400 (body/prose), 500 (labels, table headers, secondary emphasis), 600 (values, card titles, active nav), 700 (page titles, hero prices only). Mono: 400 (default numbers), 500 (emphasized values/stat rows), 600 (tickers, large prices). Never use 300 (too thin at 11-13px on dark) or 800/900. Bullish/bearish/neutral coloring conveys direction, not extra weight. |
| **Micro-labels are UPPERCASE sans with positive tracking** | All field labels, column headers, stat-row keys, and section eyebrows render UPPERCASE in var(--font-sans), 500 weight, font-size micro (9px) or 11px, letter-spacing 0.05-0.08em, color hsl(var(--muted-foreground)). This is codified by the existing .stat-label (index.css L873-878) and section-label utilities; reuse them rather than re-styling. Values paired with these labels are mono. Do NOT uppercase prose, sentences, or button labels with mixed-case words. |
| **Tickers are uppercase mono, tracked +0.04em** | Symbols (NVDA, AMD, SPY) and option roots use .ticker-font (index.css L848): var(--font-mono), 600, text-transform: uppercase, letter-spacing 0.04em, tabular-nums. They are never set in sans and never lowercased. |
| **One accent, lucide icons, no emoji** | --brand-cyan (#22d3ee) is the sole brand accent for active states, focus rings, the Cmd+K palette highlight, primary CTAs, and the 3D GEX surface signature color. Semantic direction uses only --trade-bullish/bearish/neutral (green/red/amber). All iconography is lucide-react at 16px (compact) / 20px; emoji are never used as icons or status glyphs. Color encodes meaning sparingly so cyan stays a signal, not decoration. |

**Tokens:** `--font-sans: 'IBM Plex Sans', -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif` · `--font-mono: 'IBM Plex Mono', ui-monospace, 'SF Mono', Menlo, Monaco, monospace` · `--brand-cyan: #22d3ee (sole accent)` · `--trade-bullish: #10b981 / --trade-bearish: #ef4444 / --trade-neutral: #f59e0b` · `fontSize.micro 9px / data-xs 11px / data-sm 12px / data-base 13px / data-lg 15px` · `fontSize.price-sm 16px / price-md 20px / price-lg 24px (negative tracking)` · `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums + feature-settings 'tnum' 1,'lnum' 1,'zero' 1` · `Sans weights 400/500/600/700; Mono weights 400/500/600` · `Micro-label: uppercase, sans 500, 9-11px, letter-spacing 0.05-0.08em, muted-foreground` · `Ticker: mono 600, uppercase, letter-spacing 0.04em`

**Avoid:** Rendering any number, price, percent, or ticker in the sans font — numerals MUST be mono. A sans price that reflows on every tick is the #1 violation. · Omitting font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums on numeric cells, causing column widths to jitter as live data updates. · Leaving --font-mono pointed at a font (JetBrains Mono) that index.html does not actually load — the current silent fallback to Space Mono. The declared font and the loaded font must match. · Introducing arbitrary font sizes (e.g. text-[13.5px], text-sm) instead of the closed micro/data-*/price-* scale. · Using 300-weight or 800/900-weight text; thin weights are illegible at 11-13px on dark, heavy weights break the institutional read. · Adding a second accent color or using --brand-cyan for decoration — cyan must stay a signal reserved for active/focus/CTA/GEX. · Using emoji as icons or status indicators instead of lucide-react glyphs. · Uppercasing prose, sentences, or mixed-case button labels — uppercase is reserved for tracked micro-labels, eyebrows, and tickers only. · Setting labels and values in the same font/weight/case so the label-vs-data hierarchy collapses; labels are quiet muted sans, values are bright mono. · Mixing a third font family (DM Sans, Inter, etc.) alongside IBM Plex — the system is exactly two families.


### NAVIGATION / IA / LAYOUT

QuantEdge ships a FORTRESS-style institutional terminal shell — dense, data-first, dark — wired in client/src/App.tsx as a persistent three-zone frame: a collapsible icon RAIL (left, --sidebar-width 14rem / --sidebar-width-icon 3rem), a thin TOPBAR (AuthHeader, h-10, market status + Cmd+K search + user/theme), and a SCROLLABLE WORKSPACE that hosts one of six SHELLS. The six shells are the entire authenticated IA: Home (/p), Hunt (/h), GEX (/g), Research (/r/:symbol), Positions (/pos), Journal (/j), each reached by sidebar click or ⌘1-6. Inside every shell the pattern is identical and already proven in client/src/pages/shells/*: a mono section-header, a QETabs strip (prefixLabel="VIEW") for sub-navigation, a one-line tab hint, then PageErrorBoundary + Suspense-loaded content. The accent is QuantEdge cyan (--brand-cyan #22d3ee) — never steel-blue, never a second accent — and every number renders in --font-mono while words use --font-sans. Cmd+K (client/src/components/command-palette.tsx) is the universal jump: tickers → /r/SYMBOL, pages, tabs, actions. The premium 3D + motion layer (gamma surface, Framer micro-interactions, ambient orbs/grid) sits BEHIND content via the zIndex scale (behind:-1, base:0) and never competes with data. The law below locks this so the six shells stay consistent and the next ten do too. CRITICAL DEBT to resolve: there are four competing page wrappers (page-container.tsx, page-header.tsx, qe-page-shell.tsx, plus shell-local headers) — the shell-local pattern wins; the breadcrumb primitive exists but is unused; density defaults to 'standard' not 'compact'; and 1440 is not a Tailwind breakpoint (closest is xl 1280).

| Rule | Spec |
|---|---|
| **Shell frame is fixed and singular — rail + topbar + workspace** | Every authenticated route renders inside the App.tsx frame: <SidebarProvider style={{--sidebar-width:14rem,--sidebar-width-icon:3rem}}> wrapping <div className="flex h-screen w-full"><AppSidebar/><MainContentWrapper/></div>. MainContentWrapper = <AuthHeader/> (topbar) over a single scroll container (flex-1 overflow-auto) holding <main> + <Footer>. Never add a second sidebar, a second topbar, or a nested scroll-root at the page level. Public/marketing/admin routes (the publicPages list in App.tsx) opt OUT of the frame and render bare. |
| **Sidebar rail = exactly 6 primary destinations, flat, mono, cyan-active** | PRIMARY in client/src/components/app-sidebar.tsx is the canonical nav set: Home /p (Home icon, ⌘1), Hunt /h (Crosshair, ⌘2), GEX /g (Zap, ⌘3), Research /r/SPY (Microscope, ⌘4), Positions /pos (Wallet, ⌘5), Journal /j (BookOpen, ⌘6). Labels render font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]; active row uses bg-[var(--brand-cyan)]/10 text-[var(--brand-cyan)] border-[var(--brand-cyan)]/20; inactive uses sidebar-foreground/60. Icons are lucide only, w-4 h-4 — never emoji. Below PRIMARY, only the RADARS and UTILITY groups (small, low-emphasis, captioned text-[8px] uppercase tracking). Sidebar is collapsible="icon". Do not add a 7th primary; new top-level features become a tab inside an existing shell. |
| **Every shell uses the identical container + header + tabs rhythm** | A shell file (client/src/pages/shells/*-shell.tsx) is structured: <div className="space-y-3 px-4 py-3"> → <header> with <h1 className="text-lg font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-foreground"> (accent word wrapped in text-[var(--brand-cyan)]) + optional <p className="text-[11px] font-mono text-muted-foreground/70"> subtitle → optional brief widget → <QETabs items={TABS} active={tab} onChange={setTab} prefixLabel="VIEW"/> → one-line tab hint text-[9px] font-mono text-muted-foreground/60 → <PageErrorBoundary label={`Shell · ${tab}`}><Suspense fallback={<Loader2 .. text-[var(--brand-cyan)]/>}>{tab content}</Suspense></PageErrorBoundary>. Tab state comes from useTabState<Tab>(default, VALID_TABS) so ?tab= deep-links and ⌘K targets work. |
| **QETabs is the ONLY sub-navigation primitive** | All in-shell section switching uses QETabs from @/components/ui/qe (variant 'cyan' default for primary sub-nav, 'gold' for secondary selectors, 'subtle' for tertiary in-card tabs). Tabs are font-mono uppercase; active tab is the filled cyan chip with soft glow defined in tabs.tsx. Use the optional group field to cluster long tab bars rather than nesting a second tab row. Never hand-roll a button row, never use Radix Tabs or premium-tabs.tsx for shell sub-nav. |
| **Cmd+K is the universal command palette and the only global jump surface** | client/src/components/command-palette.tsx is mounted once in App.tsx (both public and authed trees). ⌘K / Ctrl+K toggles; ⌘1-6 jump to the 6 primaries; typing a ticker (regex ^[A-Z]{1,6}(\.[A-Z])?$) + Enter routes to /r/SYMBOL. It exposes four groups in order: Go to (primaries), Tabs & sub-pages (NESTED_TABS), Tickers → Research, Quick actions. Any header/button opens it by dispatching window event 'qe:open-command-palette' — never by faking a keystroke. When you add a shell tab, register its /x?tab=y entry in NESTED_TABS so it is reachable from the palette. |
| **Topbar is a thin h-10 utility strip, not a content header** | AuthHeader (App.tsx) stays h-10, border-b border-border/40, bg-card/50 backdrop-blur-sm, shrink-0: left = SidebarTrigger + market OPEN/CLOSED dot (sm:+); right = Cmd+K Search button (with ⌘K kbd), user name (md:+), logout, ThemeToggle. The page title/identity belongs to the shell <header>, never the topbar. Do not grow the topbar with per-page actions — those go in the shell header or QETabs rightSlot. |
| **Numbers are monospace, words are sans, one accent only** | Apply font-mono (--font-mono: JetBrains Mono) to EVERY numeric value — prices, %, P&L, counts, strikes, DTE, tickers, shortcuts — and font-sans (--font-sans: DM Sans) to prose/labels. The single accent is --brand-cyan #22d3ee; trading semantics use --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish/--trade-neutral and grade tokens, but no second brand accent (no steel-blue, no purple chrome) is introduced for navigation, active states, or focus rings. |
| **Responsive law: mobile-first at 375/768/1024 with a 1440 reading-width cap** | Design and test at four widths: 375 (xs — single column, rail collapses to off-canvas via SidebarTrigger), 768 (md — rail icon-collapsible, 2-col grids), 1024 (lg — rail expanded, 3-col grids), 1440 (desktop target — content centered, capped). Tailwind screens are xs:375 sm:640 md:768 lg:1024 xl:1280 2xl:1536 — there is no 1440 breakpoint, so cap reading width with the container constraints (default max-w-7xl 1280, wide max-w-[1600px]) rather than inventing a screen. Use QEGridLayout cols for responsive grids; never set fixed pixel widths on layout regions. |
| **Premium 3D + motion lives behind content on the z-index scale** | All depth effects — the 3D GEX gamma surface, ambient glow orbs, terminal grid overlay, Framer-motion entrances — render at zIndex.behind (-1) or as pointer-events-none fixed overlays, with content at zIndex.base+ (scale in client/src/lib/design-tokens.ts: behind -1, base 0, dropdown 10, sticky 20, fixed 30, modalBackdrop 40, modal 50, popover 60, tooltip 70, toast 80). Motion uses the one global easing var(--ease-out-smooth) cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1); micro-interactions are subtle (translateY(-1px) hover, scale(0.98) active) and must respect prefers-reduced-motion. Effects never intercept clicks or obscure numbers. |
| **Use the shell-local header + QESectionDivider as the section-header pattern; promote breadcrumbs only where depth > shell/tab** | The canonical section header is the shell <h1> (mono, uppercase, cyan accent word) plus QETabs for the second level. For a THIRD level (e.g. Research → ticker → contract), surface context inline in the shell header (TickerSwitcher + live price pill, as research-shell.tsx does) and use QESectionDivider (qe-page-shell.tsx) with a numbered marker ('02 // …') between in-page sections. The breadcrumb primitive (client/src/components/ui/breadcrumb.tsx) is reserved for genuinely nested detail routes only — do not scatter breadcrumbs across shells whose depth is just shell+tab. |
| **Density: compact is the platform default; expose the toggle, gate detail by density** | Spacing rhythm inside shells stays tight (space-y-3, px-4 py-3, card-gap 10px, section-gap-sm 24px, section-gap 40px from index.css). Density is governed by ContentDensityProvider (client/src/hooks/use-content-density.tsx, modes minimal/standard/detailed) — set the platform default to the compact ('minimal') end per the FORTRESS dense identity, surface DensityControl in Settings/topbar, and let components read useContentDensity() to add/strip secondary detail. Do not hardcode loose paddings (p-6, space-y-6) in shells; use the design-tokens compact override map when collapsing. |

**Tokens:** `--brand-cyan (#22d3ee, the single nav/active/focus accent)` · `--font-mono (JetBrains Mono — all numbers, tickers, tabs, shortcuts)` · `--font-sans (DM Sans — all prose/labels)` · `--sidebar-width (14rem) / --sidebar-width-icon (3rem)` · `--section-gap (2.5rem) / --section-gap-sm (1.5rem) / --card-gap (0.625rem)` · `--radius-card (0.625rem) / --radius-button (0.5rem) / --radius-badge (0.25rem)` · `--ease-out-smooth (cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)) + --transition-fast/base/slow` · `--surface-base / --surface-raised / --surface-glass (shell + panel layers)` · `--glow-cyan / --glow-blue (ambient orb tints, behind content)` · `zIndex scale (behind:-1, base:0, dropdown:10, sticky:20, fixed:30, modalBackdrop:40, modal:50, popover:60, tooltip:70, toast:80)` · `Tailwind screens xs:375 sm:640 md:768 lg:1024 xl:1280 2xl:1536` · `Tailwind data-density font sizes (micro:9px, data-xs:11px, data-sm:12px, data-base:13px, data-lg:15px)` · `--trade-bullish / --trade-bearish / --trade-neutral (semantic, not accents)`

**Avoid:** Adding a 7th primary sidebar destination — new top-level features must become a tab inside one of the six shells (Home/Hunt/GEX/Research/Positions/Journal), not a new rail entry. · Creating a new page wrapper. There are already four (page-container.tsx, page-header.tsx, qe-page-shell.tsx, and the shell-local pattern) — the shell-local div.space-y-3.px-4.py-3 + mono header + QETabs is the law; do not introduce a fifth. · Hand-rolling sub-navigation with a custom button row, Radix Tabs, or premium-tabs.tsx instead of QETabs from @/components/ui/qe. · Using a second brand accent (steel-blue, purple, etc.) for active states, focus rings, or chrome — only --brand-cyan #22d3ee plus the trade-semantic tokens are allowed. · Setting numbers in a sans font or labels in mono — every numeral (price, %, P&L, ticker, strike, DTE, shortcut) must be --font-mono. · Emoji used as an icon anywhere in nav, tabs, or headers — lucide icons only. · Putting the page title or per-page action buttons into the h-10 AuthHeader topbar; identity and actions belong to the shell header / QETabs rightSlot. · Letting 3D/motion/orbs/grid render at or above content z-index, intercept pointer events, or obscure data — they must sit at zIndex.behind with pointer-events-none. · Inventing a 1440 Tailwind breakpoint — it does not exist (screens stop at xl:1280, 2xl:1536); cap reading width with max-w containers instead. · Adding a shell tab without registering its /x?tab=y entry in command-palette.tsx NESTED_TABS, leaving it unreachable from Cmd+K. · Faking a keyboard event to open the command palette instead of dispatching the window 'qe:open-command-palette' event. · Hardcoding loose paddings (p-6, space-y-6, gap-6) in shells instead of the compact spacing tokens / density system; the platform identity is dense-first. · Introducing a nested scroll container or second SidebarProvider at the page level — there is exactly one scroll-root (MainContentWrapper) and one sidebar instance. · Scattering breadcrumbs across shells whose depth is only shell+tab; breadcrumbs are reserved for genuinely nested detail routes.


### MOTION & 3D

QuantEdge motion is FORTRESS-institutional: fast, restrained, data-first, with ONE premium signature 3D moment and zero decorative bloat. Three tiers govern everything. (1) MICRO-INTERACTIONS — framer-motion at 150-300ms on the existing --ease-out-smooth curve (cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)); hovers/press/reveals are functional, never bouncy in the terminal; every motion gated by prefers-reduced-motion. (2) THE SIGNATURE 3D MOMENT — a single React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface (QEGammaSurface) rendering the EXISTING strikeExpiryMatrix (StrikeExpiryCell[]) as terrain: strike axis x expiry/DTE axis x netGEX height, call walls as cyan-lit peaks, put walls as purple valleys, the gamma flip plane (snapshot.gammaFlipPrice) as a glowing horizontal section line. This is the ONE place we spend the depth budget. (3) LANDING PRODUCT SHOTS — animated terminal captures via Screen Studio / Rotato (rendered .mp4/.webm), not live-DOM recreations. Stack already ships framer-motion ^11, three ^0.160, @react-three/fiber ^8.15, @react-three/drei ^9.92 — no new heavy deps. Aceternity/Magic UI effects are allowed ONLY on the marketing landing surface and BANNED everywhere inside the authed terminal. One accent (--brand-cyan #22d3ee); numbers stay monospace even inside 3D HTML labels; lucide for any glyph. All grounded in existing tokens in client/src/index.css and existing data in shared/gex-types.ts.

| Rule | Spec |
|---|---|
| **Micro-interaction duration band is 150-300ms, mapped to existing tokens** | Use the three existing CSS tokens, do not invent new durations: --transition-fast (0.15s) for press/active + toggle state, --transition-base (0.2s) for hover lift/glow/color, --transition-slow (0.3s) for reveals/expands/drawer/layout. In framer-motion: transition={{duration: 0.2, ease: [0.4,0,0.2,1]}} for hover/color, 0.3 for reveals, 0.15 for taps. Anything <120ms reads as a glitch; anything >300ms reads as sluggish in a dense terminal and is BANNED for in-terminal UI (the only >300ms exception is the deliberate 3D surface intro at ~600-800ms and landing-only effects). |
| **One easing curve for terminal motion; springs are landing-only** | In-terminal framer-motion MUST use ease: [0.4, 0, 0.2, 1] (the --ease-out-smooth curve). The bouncy spring curve cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1) — already present as .transition-spring / animate-tab-bounce — is RESTRICTED to the marketing landing page and tab-indicator only; it is BANNED on data rows, cards, tables, drawers, and the 3D surface controls. Press feedback stays the existing global active:scale(0.98) at 0.05s. |
| **Standard reveal patterns — fade-up for content, fade for chrome, stagger capped** | Reuse the existing keyframes/utilities: .animate-fade-up (12px translateY + opacity), .animate-fade-in, .animate-scale-in, .animate-page-in. Content blocks/cards enter with fade-up (y:8-12px, opacity 0->1, 0.3s). Overlays/tooltips/popovers use fade only (no translate). Lists/grids stagger at 30-50ms per item, capped at the first ~8 items then render the rest immediately (never stagger a 50-row table). Page-level mount uses animate-page-in once, not per-child. |
| **Reduced-motion is a hard requirement, not the existing partial block** | Expand the @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) block at index.css:1745 (currently only .transition-spring/.transition-smooth/.btn-magnetic) to a GLOBAL kill switch: *,*::before,*::after { animation-duration:0.01ms!important; animation-iteration-count:1!important; transition-duration:0.01ms!important; scroll-behavior:auto!important; } and disable the body::before brand stripe shimmer, .film-grain animation, and ambient orb/grain loops. In React, gate framer-motion via useReducedMotion() and the R3F surface via the same check — see the 3D fallback rule. |
| **THE signature 3D moment: exactly ONE GEX gamma surface, fed by existing matrix data** | Build client/src/components/gex/qe-gamma-surface.tsx as a React-Three-Fiber <Canvas>. Input = the EXISTING data: strikeExpiryMatrix: StrikeExpiryCell[] (strike, expiryLabel, dte, netGEX, netVEX) plus snapshot (gammaFlipPrice, spotPrice) and levels: GEXLevel[] (role: call_wall\|put_wall\|flip\|max_gamma). Build a grid geometry where X=strike, Z=dte/expiry, Y=netGEX (height). Positive netGEX = peaks, negative = valleys. Do NOT refetch or reshape server data — consume GEXTerminalData.strikeExpiryMatrix exactly as GEXExpiryMatrix already does. This is the single approved 3D set-piece; no second 3D hero may be added without explicit design sign-off. |
| **Gamma-surface color + lighting semantics map to existing GEX tokens** | Vertex/material colors come from existing tokens, not new ones: call walls / positive gamma peaks tinted with --gex-positive (#eab308 yellow) rising toward the brand --brand-cyan (#22d3ee) emissive highlight at the tallest call wall / max_gamma star; put walls / valleys use --gex-negative (#9333ea purple); the gamma flip plane is a semi-transparent horizontal section at y=0 (or at gammaFlipPrice mapped height) glowing --gex-flip (#f97316). Spot price marked by a thin vertical --brand-cyan line. Cyan is the ONLY accent/branded light; everything else is the data-semantic GEX palette. HTML labels via drei <Html> use --font-mono for every number. |
| **Surface interaction + camera are restrained and labeled** | Use drei <OrbitControls> with enablePan=false, damping on, polar angle clamped (no flipping under the terrain), autoRotate OFF by default (optional ~0.3 speed slow idle drift only until first user interaction, then stop). Intro animation: camera eases to resting angle once over ~600-800ms with --ease-out-smooth, then idle. Hover a cell raises a drei <Html> tooltip showing strike / expiryLabel / netGEX (monospace, 1-2 decimals, $Bn). Keep DPR clamped (dpr={[1,2]}) and use frameloop="demand" when idle to stop the RAF when nothing moves. No continuous spinning, no parallax-on-scroll for the surface. |
| **3D performance + graceful fallback are mandatory** | Lazy-load the surface: const QEGammaSurface = React.lazy(...) behind <Suspense> with a skeleton, so three/R3F never enter the main terminal bundle. Render the 3D view ONLY when strikeExpiryMatrix?.length is sufficient AND not reduced-motion AND WebGL is available; otherwise fall back to the existing 2D <GEXExpiryMatrix> (which already takes the same matrix prop). Provide an explicit 2D/3D toggle in ticker-gex-tab.tsx alongside the existing bars/table/matrix toggle — 2D table view stays the default for compact density. Cap geometry (decimate to ~30 strikes centered on spot, matching the 2D component's default window) to keep vertex count sane. |
| **Landing-page animated product shots use a recorded-video pipeline, not live DOM** | Hero/feature 'product shots' on client/src/pages/landing.tsx are pre-recorded with Screen Studio or Rotato, exported as optimized .mp4 + .webm (with a poster .png first frame), <video autoPlay muted loop playsInline preload="metadata">. Do NOT rebuild the live terminal in the hero with real queries/effects. Respect reduced-motion: when useReducedMotion() / the media query is set, show the poster image instead of the looping video. Keep clips short (<=10s), device-framed, showing real QuantEdge screens (GEX board, signal cards, gamma surface). |
| **Aceternity / Magic UI effects: landing-allowed, terminal-banned** | ALLOWED on the marketing landing surface only (pages/landing.tsx and its sections): at most ONE ambient effect per viewport section, drawn from a small allowlist — Spotlight, subtle BackgroundBeams/grid, and the already-present watchlist-spotlight usage — all recolored to --brand-cyan and dimmed (opacity in the 0.03-0.08 range like existing --glow-cyan / page-atmosphere). BANNED everywhere inside the authed terminal (any page under the app shell): no Meteors, no Sparkles, no card-spotlight on data cards, no animated gradient borders on tables, no text-generate/typewriter on real data, no infinite-marquee except the existing dedicated ticker tape. The signature 3D surface is the terminal's ONLY 'wow' effect. |
| **Number-bearing motion must never compromise legibility or monospace** | Price/value flashes use the existing animate price-flash-up/price-flash-down + price-pulse keyframes (brief background tint, <=300ms, fading to transparent) — never translate or scale digits. Numbers stay --font-mono at all times including inside drei <Html> 3D labels and animated counters. Animated count-up (if used) increments a monospace, fixed-width field so width never jitters. No layout-shifting animation on any cell containing a number. |

**Tokens:** `--brand-cyan (#22d3ee) — the ONE branded accent; only branded emissive light in the 3D surface and the only recolor target for landing ambient effects` · `--ease-out-smooth (cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)) — the single easing for all terminal motion and the framer-motion ease array` · `--transition-fast (0.15s) — press/active + toggle state` · `--transition-base (0.2s) — hover lift/glow/color` · `--transition-slow (0.3s) — reveals/expands/drawers` · `--gex-positive (#eab308) — call walls / positive-gamma peaks on the surface` · `--gex-negative (#9333ea) — put walls / negative-gamma valleys on the surface` · `--gex-flip (#f97316) — the gamma flip section plane` · `--gex-star (#eab308) — max_gamma peak highlight marker` · `--projection-glow (#06b6d4) — cyan arc/glow accent reused for surface spot line` · `--glow-cyan (rgba(34,211,238,0.06)) — dim ambient ceiling for landing effects + surface ambient light` · `--font-mono ('JetBrains Mono'…) — every number, including 3D <Html> labels and animated counters` · `--trade-bullish/-bearish/-neutral (#10b981/#ef4444/#f59e0b) — semantic value-flash tints (price-flash-up/down)`

**Avoid:** Adding a second 3D hero/set-piece anywhere — the gamma surface is the ONLY signature 3D moment; more depth dilutes it. · Bouncy/overshoot spring easing (cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)) on terminal data rows, cards, tables, or drawers — that curve is landing/tab-indicator only. · Durations outside 150-300ms for in-terminal UI (sub-120ms glitchy taps or 400ms+ sluggish reveals). Only the 3D intro (~600-800ms) and landing effects may exceed it. · Staggering every row of a long data table — cap stagger at ~8 items, then render instantly. · Aceternity/Magic UI effects (Meteors, Sparkles, card-spotlight, animated gradient borders, typewriter) inside the authed terminal. · Recreating the live terminal in the landing hero with real queries instead of a recorded Screen Studio/Rotato product video. · Continuous autorotate or unclamped OrbitControls on the gamma surface — reads as a tech demo, not a tool. · Letting three/R3F into the main bundle — the surface MUST be React.lazy + Suspense and only mount when data + WebGL + motion-allowed. · Shipping the 3D surface with no 2D fallback for reduced-motion / weak-GPU / missing-WebGL users (the existing GEXExpiryMatrix is the fallback). · A second, more-than-one accent color in motion or 3D — only --brand-cyan #22d3ee is branded; everything else is the GEX data palette. · Translating, scaling, or non-monospacing digits during value-change animation — use the existing price-flash tint keyframes on fixed-width monospace. · Refetching or reshaping GEX data for the 3D view — consume the existing strikeExpiryMatrix (StrikeExpiryCell[]) exactly as the 2D matrix does. · Leaving the prefers-reduced-motion block as the current 3-class partial instead of a global kill switch covering grain, shimmer, gradients, price-flash, and framer-motion. · Emojis as motion accents or icons — use lucide glyphs only.


### COMPONENT PRIMITIVES

QuantEdge already has a half-built canonical layer (client/src/components/ui/qe/: QECard, QEStat, QEPill, QEBar, QESection, QETabs, QEDrawer — 30 importers) sitting on a strong token foundation (client/src/index.css + client/src/lib/design-tokens.ts), but it is drowning under 80 ui/ primitives plus parallel hand-rolled variants. The audit shows three structural problems. (1) Two heavy, competing systems: base shadcn (button 123, card 146, badge 152, input 41, tabs 27, table 13 importers) AND the qe layer — pages mix both, plus a third tier of bespoke composites. (2) Dead premium composites: trade-idea-card, premium-card, premium-tabs, glow-card, stat-card, animated-stat, confidence-meter, mini-sparkline all have 0 importers; trade-card/screener-card/strategy-card/earnings-card have 1-2. (3) No single SignalCard — there are 5+ rival implementations (signal-card/SignalCard.tsx, trade-desk/SignalCard.tsx, research-signal-card.tsx, inline ones in deep-analysis-panel.tsx and dashboard/trading-signals-feed.tsx), and the supposed-canonical one hand-rolls `bg-zinc-900/40 border border-zinc-800` with raw emerald/red/cyan — the exact anti-pattern qe/index.ts forbids. The LAW: the QE primitive set is the ONLY public building-block layer; shadcn primitives become private internals QE wraps; one accent (--brand-cyan), sans for words / mono for every number, lucide-only icons, three density modes wired through a single data-density attribute. The 3D gamma surface + framer-motion (both already in deps: three/@react-three/fiber/drei + framer-motion) live in a separate, opt-in motion/3D layer that NEVER leaks into the dense data primitives. Two density systems already exist and conflict (componentStyles.density compact/comfortable Tailwind-override hack, and use-content-density.tsx minimal/standard/detailed which is about content verbosity not spacing) — both must be replaced by one spacing-scale density model defaulting to compact.

| Rule | Spec |
|---|---|
| **The QE namespace is the only public primitive layer; shadcn becomes private** | Pages and feature components may import ONLY from @/components/ui/qe (QEButton, QECard, QEPill/QEBadge, QETable*, QESignalCard, QEStat, QETabs, QEInput, QEEmptyState) plus the small set of unwrapped Radix wrappers QE re-exports (dialog, popover, dropdown, tooltip, select, sheet, command). Direct imports of the raw shadcn files (ui/button, ui/card, ui/badge, ui/input, ui/tabs, ui/table) from outside ui/qe are forbidden going forward. Each QE primitive is a thin wrapper that maps a typed `variant`/`tone`/`size` prop to componentStyles.* class strings (lib/design-tokens.ts) which resolve to CSS vars in index.css. Add an ESLint no-restricted-imports rule blocking ui/{button,card,badge,input,tabs,table} outside the ui/qe folder. |
| **Button = QEButton, 6 variants x 3 sizes, accent is cyan only** | Canonical: QEButton with variant ∈ {primary, secondary, outline, ghost, danger, glass} and size ∈ {sm(h-7 px-2.5 text-xs), md(h-9 px-4 text-sm DEFAULT), lg(h-10 px-6 text-base)} plus icon (square h-9/h-7/h-10). primary = solid --brand-cyan fill on near-black bg with the existing inset-highlight + drop shadow (depth layer), NOT a teal→cyan gradient. danger = --trade-bearish. There is exactly ONE accent: cyan. Remove the success(green) and the teal→cyan gradient primary from current ui/button.tsx and componentStyles.button.primary. Words in font-sans semibold; any number rendered inside a button (counts, prices) uses font-mono tabular-nums. Icons are lucide at [&_svg]:size-4 (md) — no emoji. Press feedback: scale(0.98) (already global). Magnetic/translateY hover is opt-in via a `motion` prop only, never default on dense toolbars. |
| **Card = QECard; it is the only surface primitive** | Keep QECard as the sole card. Canonical variants: default, glass, elevated, interactive, dense, inset, and the 5 accent-left variants (accent-cyan/teal/gold/bull/bear). Drop the 'feature' variant (rounded-xl breaks the one-radius rule — all cards use --radius-card 10px). Padding prop {none,sm,md,lg} is the ONLY way to set card padding — no ad-hoc p-* on cards. QECard renders the shadcn-card class so it inherits the global hover-lift (translateY -1px) + faint cyan glow in dark. glass variant uses --surface-glass tokens, never raw rgba. Deprecate ui/card.tsx as a public export (QECard wraps it internally) and deprecate premium-card, glow-card, terminal-card CSS as separate surfaces. |
| **Pill/Badge = QEPill; mono uppercase micro-label, one shape** | Keep QEPill as the canonical inline status chip; standardize on the rounded (4px, --radius-badge) mono variants from componentStyles.badge (cyan/bull/bear/gold/warn/ai/live/delayed/closed/accent/success/error). Retire the rounded-full pill shape (componentStyles.badge.default/accent/success/warning/error use rounded-full — convert to rounded to match badge radius token). All pills: 10px font-mono font-semibold uppercase, colored text + 10-15% tinted bg + 25% border, semantic via --trade-*/--brand-* vars. Status dots (live/online) keep their glow. Deprecate ui/badge.tsx (shadcn) as a public export and fold freshness-badge into a QEPill variant. |
| **Table row = QETable* density-aware data grid** | Promote a QETable family (QETable, QETableHeader, QETableRow, QETableCell, QETableHead) wrapping the shadcn table. Defaults to Bloomberg density (the existing .data-table-compact: th 10px uppercase mono muted, td 11px mono tabular-nums, row hover bg-muted/50, 1px border-border rows). Numeric cells are font-mono tabular-nums RIGHT-aligned; label/text cells font-sans left-aligned — enforced via a `numeric` prop on QETableCell, not ad-hoc classes. Row height follows the active density mode (compact 28px / comfortable 36px / spacious 44px). Replace the shadcn defaults (h-12 header, p-4 cells) which are far too airy for a data-first terminal. |
| **SignalCard = QESignalCard, the single signature component, token-pure** | Collapse the 5+ rival SignalCards into ONE QESignalCard with size ∈ {mini(dashboard tile), standard(desk row), full(Oracle terminal)} rendering from one SignalData shape. It MUST be built on QECard + QEStat + QEPill + QEBar — zero hand-rolled bg-zinc-900/40 / border-zinc-800, zero raw emerald/red/cyan. Direction → QEPill variant bull/bear/default; confidence → QEBar tone cyan; every price/target/P&L/% → QEStat or font-mono tabular-nums via --trade-*/--brand-cyan. Optional premium layer: framer-motion entrance + new-signal flash and the 3D GEX gamma surface are mounted ONLY in size='full', behind a `motion`/`surface3d` prop, and never on mini/standard (dense lists stay static for scan speed). Deprecate trade-desk/SignalCard.tsx, research-signal-card.tsx, and the inline SignalCards in deep-analysis-panel.tsx + dashboard/trading-signals-feed.tsx. |
| **Stat/Metric = QEStat / QEStatStrip; one metric tile** | Keep QEStat (label + big mono tabular value + optional delta with ▲/▼ + tone) and QEStatStrip as the ONLY metric display. tone drives color via --trade-*/--brand-* vars; size ∈ {sm,md,lg,xl}. Every number on the platform that isn't in a table renders through QEStat or the .price-display/.data-cell mono utilities. Deprecate ui/stat-card.tsx, ui/animated-stat.tsx, and componentStyles-based bespoke metric blocks; if animation is wanted, add an optional `animate` count-up prop to QEStat rather than a separate AnimatedStat. |
| **Tabs = QETabs (sub-nav) with one Radix Tabs escape hatch** | QETabs (the mono uppercase pill strip, variants cyan/gold/subtle, sizes sm/md, grouped-cluster mode) is the canonical workflow/sub-nav primitive — used for PULSE/HUNT/RESEARCH/POSITIONS/JOURNAL and in-card tabs. Active = filled cyan chip with soft glow (gold reserved for secondary sort selectors only — still one PRIMARY accent). For panels that genuinely need mounted/unmounted tab PANELS, expose QETabsPanel wrapping Radix Tabs styled with componentStyles.tab.underline. Deprecate ui/premium-tabs.tsx (0 importers) and the raw ui/tabs.tsx default styling as a public surface. |
| **Input = QEInput; one field, mono for numeric entry** | Promote a single QEInput consolidating ui/input.tsx + glass-input.tsx: props variant {solid(default),glass}, size {sm(h-8),md(h-9),lg(h-10)}, font {sans(default),mono}, iconLeft/iconRight (lucide), error, hint. Focus ring is --brand-cyan (NOT the current teal in glass-input or the default ring). Numeric/ticker/price inputs set font='mono' tabular-nums. Deprecate glass-input.tsx and the bespoke .terminal-input CSS; textarea/select follow the same token + cyan-focus treatment. |
| **EmptyState = QEEmptyState; lucide icon, no emoji, optional cyan action** | Promote ui/empty-state.tsx to QEEmptyState (currently 0 importers despite empty states existing inline everywhere). Keep its variant presets (no-data/no-signals/no-results/error/offline/coming-soon) each with a lucide icon (no emoji), title (sans), message (sans muted), and optional QEButton actions (primary action uses --brand-cyan, not bg-cyan-500 raw). Provide QEEmptyState.Inline for the terminal '> No data' one-liner. Every list/grid/panel renders QEEmptyState on empty data instead of bespoke 'No results' divs. |
| **Three density modes, one mechanism, compact default** | Define exactly three modes: compact (DEFAULT — Bloomberg density: card padding 8-10px, table row 28px, stat label 9px, base text 12px, gaps 6-8px), comfortable (card padding 12-14px, row 36px, base 13px, gaps 12px), spacious (card padding 16-20px, row 44px, base 14px, gaps 16px). Drive ALL of it from a single `data-density` attribute on a root provider; primitives read CSS-var spacing tokens (--space-card-pad, --row-h, --stat-label-size) that switch on [data-density='...']. RETIRE the two existing conflicting systems: componentStyles.density (compact/comfortable Tailwind child-override hack `[&_.p-6]:p-3 ...`) and use-content-density.tsx (minimal/standard/detailed — that hook is about CONTENT VERBOSITY, keep it renamed as useContentVerbosity, but it is NOT the visual density system). Density never changes color, accent, or which elements show — only spacing/size. |
| **Number/word/icon/accent invariants are non-negotiable across all primitives** | Hard rules baked into every QE primitive: (1) EVERY number — price, %, count, P&L, GEX, time — renders font-mono with tabular-nums (use .data-cell/.price-display utils or QEStat); words render font-sans (DM Sans). (2) Exactly ONE accent: --brand-cyan #22d3ee for primary/active/focus. Gold (--brand-gold) is the single permitted SECONDARY accent for sort/secondary toggles; trade semantics (--trade-bullish/bearish/neutral) and GEX palette are DATA colors, not UI accents. No teal-as-accent, no green success buttons, no violet AI accent in chrome. (3) Icons are lucide only — no emoji as icons anywhere. (4) One radius scale (--radius-card 10 / --radius-button 8 / --radius-badge 4), one easing (--ease-out-smooth), one shadow ramp (--shadow-*). (5) The 3D gamma surface + framer-motion micro-interactions live in a dedicated motion/3D layer (e.g. ui/fx or signal-card full mode) and are opt-in; dense data primitives stay static and instant. |

**Tokens:** `--brand-cyan (#22d3ee) — THE single primary accent: primary buttons, active tabs, focus rings, interactive hover; replace every raw cyan-400/500 and teal-as-accent usage` · `--brand-gold (#f59e0b) — the ONLY permitted secondary accent (sort/secondary toggles); never co-equal with cyan` · `--trade-bullish / --trade-bearish / --trade-neutral (+ -bg / -bg-subtle) — data semantics for direction/P&L/change; replace raw emerald/red/amber Tailwind` · `--surface-base / --surface-raised / --surface-glass / --surface-glass-border — the only panel/glass backgrounds; kill raw bg-zinc-900/40 and rgba literals` · `--radius-card (10px) / --radius-button (8px) / --radius-badge (4px) — single radius scale; drop rounded-xl 'feature' cards and rounded-full pills` · `--font-sans (DM Sans) for words / --font-mono (JetBrains Mono) for EVERY number, paired with tabular-nums + .data-cell/.price-display utilities` · `--ease-out-smooth + --transition-fast/base/slow — one easing curve and three durations for all interactive transitions` · `--shadow-2xs … --shadow-2xl — single shadow ramp for depth; no ad-hoc box-shadow literals on cards/buttons` · `--gex-positive / --gex-negative / --gex-flip / --gex-star / --projection-glow (and -bg variants) — dedicated GEX data palette (yellow walls / purple puts / cyan arc) used by the 3D gamma surface and GEX pills; data colors, not UI accents` · `--grade-s…--grade-f — grade letter colors for S-tier signal badges` · `NEW --space-card-pad / --row-h / --stat-label-size / --gap-grid — density-scale spacing tokens switched by [data-density] (compact|comfortable|spacious)` · `--confidence-high/medium/low + --glow-cyan/blue/teal — confidence bar fills and ambient glow for the premium depth layer`

**Avoid:** Hand-rolling surfaces with `bg-zinc-900/40 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg` (or bg-card+border ad hoc) instead of QECard — currently done in the supposedly-canonical signal-card/SignalCard.tsx, the #1 offender against the rule its own qe/index.ts states. · Using raw Tailwind semantic colors (emerald-400/500, red-400/500, cyan-400/500, amber-400, violet-400) instead of CSS-var tokens (--trade-bullish/bearish/neutral, --brand-cyan, --brand-gold). Seen across SignalCard, stat-card, empty-state actions, density controls. · Importing base shadcn primitives (ui/button, ui/card, ui/badge, ui/input, ui/tabs, ui/table) directly in pages/features — 146 ui/card and 152 ui/badge importers bypass the QE layer, defeating the single-source design. · Multiple competing implementations of the same concept: 5+ SignalCards, 3 tab systems (QETabs/ui/tabs/premium-tabs), 3 metric tiles (QEStat/stat-card/animated-stat), 4 card surfaces (Card/QECard/premium-card/glow-card), 3 input focus colors (--ring/teal/cyan). · Multi-accent creep: teal→cyan gradient primary buttons, green `success` buttons, violet AI badges, teal focus rings — violates 'one accent only' (must be cyan, with gold as the single secondary). · Two density systems with overlapping vocabulary: componentStyles.density (compact/comfortable as a Tailwind child-override hack) and use-content-density.tsx (minimal/standard/detailed for verbosity) — different axes, confusing names, neither a real spacing scale. · Marketing-grade spacing in a data terminal: shadcn table h-12 headers + p-4 cells, card p-6 defaults, section py-16/py-24 — the opposite of compact/data-first. · Mixed number rendering: prices/counts in font-sans without tabular-nums (columns jitter, can't scan) instead of mono tabular-nums everywhere. · Inconsistent badge geometry: rounded-full pills (componentStyles.badge.default/success/warning) coexisting with rounded 4px mono chips — two badge shapes for the same role. · Letting premium FX leak into dense primitives: framer-motion hover-lift/shimmer and 3D effects applied by default on list rows/tiles, slowing scanning — they belong in an opt-in motion/3D layer (full SignalCard, hero surfaces) only. · Dead primitives kept on life support: trade-idea-card, premium-card, premium-tabs, glow-card, stat-card, animated-stat, confidence-meter, mini-sparkline (all 0 importers) and 1-2-importer composites (trade-card, screener-card, strategy-card, earnings-card, winners-showcase) bloating ui/ to 80 files and inviting accidental reuse. · Inline empty states ('No results' divs) instead of QEEmptyState (which exists at ui/empty-state.tsx with 0 importers) — and its primary action uses raw bg-cyan-500 rather than QEButton primary.

## 2. New Components & Assets to Build (the kit)

- CSS spacing-density tokens in index.css — e.g. --space-card-pad, --row-h, --stat-label-size, --gap-grid, switched under [data-density='compact'|'comfortable'|'spacious']
- DensityProvider + useDensity — single root provider emitting `data-density` (compact default | comfortable | spacious) consumed by CSS-var spacing tokens; replaces componentStyles.density hack
- ESLint no-restricted-imports config — block ui/{button,card,badge,input,tabs,table} imports outside client/src/components/ui/qe to enforce the single public layer
- MicroLabel — uppercase tracked sans caption primitive (wraps .stat-label) for field/column labels
- Num — mono+tabular numeric primitive (wraps .data-cell) for all raw numbers/counts
- Pct — percent value with sign-aware bullish/bearish/neutral coloring, mono tabular
- Price — formats currency/price with price-* sizing tier prop and bullish/bearish color
- QEBadge — alias/rename of QEPill to make the canonical name match the brief; one mono uppercase rounded-4px chip, full semantic variant set, folds in freshness-badge
- QEBreadcrumb (thin mono breadcrumb built on the existing ui/breadcrumb primitive, used ONLY for true depth like Research → ticker → contract)
- QEButton — wrap ui/button.tsx; variants {primary(cyan solid),secondary,outline,ghost,danger,glass}, sizes {sm,md,lg,icon}; cyan-only accent; mono for embedded numbers; opt-in `motion` magnetic hover
- QECommandPaletteRegistry (a single exported nav manifest consumed by both app-sidebar.tsx and command-palette.tsx so the 6 primaries + shell tabs are defined once, eliminating the current duplicated PRIMARY/NESTED_TABS drift)
- QEDensityToggle (surface DensityControl from use-content-density.tsx in the topbar/Settings so compact-default density is user-switchable)
- QEEmptyState (+ QEEmptyState.Inline) — promote ui/empty-state.tsx into the QE namespace; lucide icons (no emoji), QEButton primary actions on cyan
- QEFx / motion+3D layer (e.g. ui/fx/) — home for framer-motion micro-interaction wrappers and the GammaSurface3D (react-three-fiber) so premium depth/motion is opt-in and isolated from dense primitives
- QEGammaSurface (the signature 3D GEX gamma-surface canvas, rendered at zIndex.behind with pointer-events-none, the premium depth centerpiece)
- QEInput (+ QETextarea, QESelect to match) — consolidates ui/input.tsx + glass-input.tsx + .terminal-input CSS; variant {solid,glass}, size {sm,md,lg}, font {sans,mono}, lucide icon slots, --brand-cyan focus ring
- QEShell (extract the repeated shell skeleton — container div.space-y-3.px-4.py-3 + mono <h1> with cyan accent + subtitle + QETabs(prefixLabel=VIEW) + tab-hint line + PageErrorBoundary/Suspense — so all six shells and future ones import one wrapper instead of copy-pasting)
- QEShellHeader (mono uppercase tracking-widest title + optional cyan accent word + subtitle + actions slot, replacing the ad-hoc <header> blocks duplicated in every shell)
- QESignalCard — single canonical signal component (sizes mini/standard/full) built purely on QECard+QEStat+QEPill+QEBar; token-only colors; opt-in framer-motion + 3D GEX gamma surface in full mode; replaces 5+ existing SignalCards
- QETable, QETableHeader, QETableBody, QETableRow, QETableHead, QETableCell — density-aware data-grid family wrapping ui/table.tsx; default Bloomberg density; QETableCell `numeric` prop → mono tabular-nums right-aligned; row height follows data-density
- QETabsPanel — thin wrapper over Radix Tabs styled with componentStyles.tab.underline, for the genuine mounted/unmounted tab-panel cases QETabs (sub-nav strip) doesn't cover
- StatRow — label(sans muted) + value(mono tabular) key-value pair primitive
- Ticker — uppercase mono tracked symbol primitive (wraps .ticker-font)
- client/src/components/gex/qe-gamma-surface-fallback — reuse of existing GEXExpiryMatrix as the 2D fallback (no new file; wire it as the Suspense/reduced-motion/no-WebGL fallback)
- client/src/components/gex/qe-gamma-surface.tsx — the signature React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface (strike x expiry x netGEX terrain; cyan call-wall peaks, purple put-wall valleys, orange flip-plane section line; drei OrbitControls + Html monospace tooltips; lazy-loaded, frameloop=demand)
- client/src/components/landing/product-shot.tsx — <video autoPlay muted loop playsInline> wrapper for Screen Studio/Rotato captures with poster fallback under reduced-motion (landing-only)
- client/src/hooks/use-reduced-motion.ts — thin wrapper over framer-motion useReducedMotion()/matchMedia, used to gate both framer-motion and the 3D Canvas mount
- client/src/lib/motion.ts — shared framer-motion variants/transitions (fadeUp, fade, scaleIn, stagger) bound to ease:[0.4,0,0.2,1] and the 150/200/300ms band, so components import one motion vocabulary
