## 3. Per-Surface Redesign Specs

### P0 — core trading surfaces (do first)  (10)

#### stock-detail  · L effort · 15 issues

- **Violations:** Massive hardcoded Tailwind palette usage: 365 occurrences of bg/text/border/from/via/to-{teal,cyan,slate,emerald,rose,amber,violet,purple,blue,red,gray,yellow}-NNN across the file (e.g. text-teal-400 x31, from-slate-900 x15, bg-emerald-500/20 x12, bg-gray-200 x18). Almost no surface/text/border value goes through a token. Directly violates 'never hardcode; consume hsl(var(--token))'. _(high)_; Page treats teal/cyan as its primary accent everywhere (header price tier, Trade CTA gradient, tab active state, all card left-borders, radar). Law demotes teal #14b8a6 to logo-stripe + GEX-surface ONLY; the single chromatic accent must be brand cyan --brand-cyan #22d3ee. Page uses --brand-teal/teal-400/cyan-400 as the everyday accent. _(high)_; Six-hue chromatic 'engine rainbow' (cyan/emerald/amber/violet/rose/purple) used as decorative identity for the 6 analysis engines in radar dots, engine badge grid, and expanded-engine colorStyles maps. Law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent plus 3 semantic money/direction tokens; a 6-color decorative palette is the strongest violation of the color law. _(high)_; Semantic trade tokens misused as generic chrome/decoration: emerald/amber/rose hues encode engine categories (Fund, Quant, Flow) and section accents, not money/direction. e.g. quant card icon bg-amber-500/20, Flow=rose, Fundamental=emerald. --trade-* must encode ONLY money/direction. _(high)_; Decorative ambient glow is pervasive and explicitly banned: 18 absolute blur-2xl/blur-3xl 'orb' divs (bg-teal-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl, etc.) plus a teal scanning-line and rotating-Sparkles loop in AIInsightCard. Law: 'NO ambient/decorative glow.' _(high)_; 11 raw rgb()/hex literals in the SVG radar and a div: stroke='rgb(51,65,85)', fill='rgba(20,184,166,0.2)', and an rgb colorMap (cyan/emerald/amber/violet/rose/purple), plus bg-[#fafafa] on the loading screen. Must be hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Hero/data numbers rendered in sans not mono: ~12 text-2xl/3xl/4xl font-bold values without font-mono (QuantEdge Score %, analyst consensus, '% upside', 'X analysts', AI Grade pts, engine grade letters at text-lg). Law: EVERY number is IBM Plex Mono with tabular-nums. Many small stats also rely on the Card not enforcing mono. _(high)_; 40 bg-gradient utilities for chrome (card backgrounds from-slate-900 via-slate-900 to-teal-950/30, gradient tab/button fills, gradient day-range bar from-red-500 via-amber-500 to-emerald-500). Law mandates neutral grayscale elevation ladder + token borders; gradient surfaces are off-system. _(high)_; Elevation ladder not followed: surfaces are gradient slate-900 or raw bg-gray-50/bg-gray-200 rather than the L0-L3 ladder (--background/--card/--secondary/--surface-overlay-solid). bg-gray-200 dark:bg-muted/50 appears 18x as ad-hoc raised surface. _(high)_; Broken/contradictory class combo on 5 Cards: 'bg-white/80 dark:bg-white dark:bg-card/80' (lines ~1533,1577,1696,1766,2098) — dark:bg-white renders pure white before partial override. Clear bug + token violation. (Flagged as spinoff task_ce512dd5.) _(med)_; Radius inconsistency: rounded, rounded-sm, rounded-md, rounded-lg, rounded-xl, rounded-2xl, rounded-full all mixed (49 full, 33 lg, 18 xl, 13 md, 10 bare). No single radius scale; e.g. tier badge w-20 h-20 rounded-2xl vs sibling cards rounded-xl. _(med)_; Motion is decorative and outside the 150-300ms micro-interaction signature: AIInsightCard runs an infinite typewriter, infinite Sparkles rotation, infinite scanning-line, infinite pulsing dots, and a 6-engine fake 'progress bar' parse animation. Loading spinners use bespoke ring-pulse. These are landing-style/ambient, banned in-terminal, and the typewriter ignores prefers-reduced-motion. _(med)_; Default-data masquerading as real values in the radar labels (score: ...?.score || 42 / 48 / 62 / 38 / 72 / 58) — hardcoded fake percentages shown when data missing. Not a color-law issue but a data-integrity/trust gap on a P0 trading surface. _(med)_; Font tokens themselves diverge from law: index.css --font-sans='DM Sans', --font-mono='JetBrains Mono'; law specifies IBM Plex Sans/Mono. Page consumes the tokens correctly where it uses them, so this is a token-definition issue, not a component one. (Flagged as spinoff task_2ba61ef7.) _(low)_; Hardcoded white/text-white (7) and bg-white usages for chips/handles (e.g. day-range handle bg-white border-teal-500); CTA text uses text-white instead of token foreground-on-accent. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong information architecture (sticky compact header with ticker+price+tier, .data-strip key-stats row, 6-up quick-stat grid, tabbed body: AI Report / Overview / Options / News / Analysts / Insiders / Institutions). The layout is genuinely good and dense — do NOT restructure. This is purely a color/typography/motion re-skin onto the token system. Collapse the 6-up quick-stat grid and the engine-badge grid to canonical .stat-card / .metric-block components so number formatting and elevation come for free.
- **Apply:** COLOR: Global find/replace the 365 palette utilities to tokens. (1) All accent teal/cyan chrome (header tier-S/A, Trade CTA, active tab, card left-borders, radar fill/stroke, LineChart icon chips) -> --brand-cyan: use accent-card--cyan for left-border cards, bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.1)] / text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] for chips, and a SOLID --brand-cyan fill (no gradient) for the one primary CTA. (2) Money/direction values -> --trade-bullish / --trade-bearish / --trade-neutral ONLY (high/low, change, support/resistance, upside, STRONG BUY/HOLD/WAIT, MACD bull/bear) — many already correct, finish the migration so emerald/red literals next to token text are removed. (3) Kill the 6-hue engine rainbow: render all six engine badges/dots/radar in NEUTRAL surface ladder + --border, and encode each engine's RESULT (its grade) with the 3 semantic tokens (A/B = bullish, C = neutral, D/F = bearish) plus --brand-cyan only for the currently-selected engine. Delete the cyan/emerald/amber/violet/rose/purple colorMaps and rgb colorMaps entirely. (4) Surfaces: replace from-slate-900 via-slate-900 gradient cards with flat bg-card (L1) / bg-secondary (L2) and depth via border-border / border-strong; replace bg-gray-50/100/200 with bg-secondary or --surface-raised; fix the 5 broken bg-white dark:bg-white cards to bg-card. (5) SVG radar: replace rgb(51,65,85) grid with stroke='hsl(var(--border))', and the teal fill with hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.2) fill + hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) stroke. TYPOGRAPHY: wrap every number in font-mono tabular-nums — convert the 12 sans hero numbers to .price-display-md/.price-display-lg (score, target, consensus, % upside, pts) and engine grades to mono 600; uppercase micro-labels already use tracking but standardize to the 9/11/12/13/15px scale via data-xs/data-sm classes. MOTION: strip the AIInsightCard typewriter/scanning-line/rotating-Sparkles/parse-bars and all 18 blur-orb divs; replace with a static token-styled card. Apply the canonical hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) at 150-300ms ease-out-smooth to interactive cards/engine buttons and press scale(0.98); gate all motion behind prefers-reduced-motion. GLOW: delete every 'absolute ... rounded-full blur-2xl/3xl' element. COMPONENTS: reuse .data-strip (already used), .stat-card, .metric-block/.metric-value/.metric-label, .price-display-*, .accent-card--{cyan,bullish,bearish}, and the canonical <Badge>/<Card>/<Button> variants instead of bespoke gradient buttons.
- **Components:** Canonical: <Card> (flat bg-card), <Button> (use the real primary variant for the cyan CTA, not a hand-rolled gradient), <Badge>, <Tabs/TabsList/TabsTrigger> (token active state, not gradient), plus CSS helpers .data-strip / .stat-card / .metric-block / .price-display-md|lg / .accent-card--cyan|bullish|bearish. Replace the inline 6-engine badge grid and expanded-engine panel with a small reusable EngineGradeChip that maps grade->semantic token.
- **Motion/3D:** No new 3D here — the RT-Three-Fiber gamma surface is the GEX surface's signature, not this page. Opportunity: the SVG pentagon radar is the natural hero of stock-detail; keep it 2D SVG but re-skin to neutral grid + single cyan polygon, and add a one-shot 200-300ms ease-out-smooth draw/scale-in on mount (reduced-motion: render static). Engine buttons get the standard border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover. Remove all ambient/looping motion.

#### gex-dashboard  · L effort · 12 issues
*Today:* A dense, Bloomberg-style gamma dashboard: header + symbol search bar, a large strike x expiration heatmap table beside a Key Levels / AI Analysis sidebar, then a Gamma Walls bar chart and a 2-column Sniper Signals grid. It looks competent and dark, but the heatmap renders in emerald/red instead of the app's purpose-built GEX yellow/purple/teal palette, and color is scattered across raw Tailwind classes rather than tokens.

- **Violations:** DEDICATED GEX TOKENS IGNORED. index.css ships a purpose-built GEX palette (--gex-positive yellow #facc15, --gex-negative purple #a855f7, --gex-flip orange #fb923c, --gex-star, --gex-alert, --gex-neutral teal #134e4a baseline) and the law names this surface as the sanctioned home for teal/purple. The component uses NONE of them. gexCellColor() (lines 106-120) hardcodes bg-emerald-600/70, bg-emerald-500/50|25|10, bg-red-600/70, bg-red-500/50|25|10, text-red-100|200|300. The whole heatmap renders in the wrong color language. _(high)_; Flip marker / flip price uses raw violet-400 (lines 314, 325, 367, 416, 572) instead of --gex-flip orange. Max-gamma star uses --trade-neutral amber (lines 315, 324, 366, 431) instead of --gex-star. Semantic trade-* tokens misused as GEX chrome. _(high)_; 39 raw Tailwind color usages total (emerald-*, red-NNN, violet-*, amber-*, slate-*, yellow-400) bypassing tokens. text-yellow-400 used for neutral bias/regime in 4 places (lines 438, 508, 540, 781) — should be --trade-neutral. _(high)_; Ambient/decorative glow banned by motion law: S+ sniper card uses shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(239,68,68,0.1)] (line 648) with raw rgb. Depth must come from bg-step + border, never glow. _(high)_; Raw-rgb / non-token gradients as card chrome: bg-gradient-to-br from-red-950/40 to-slate-900/60 (line 648) and from-amber-950/20 to-slate-900/60 (line 650). slate-900 is not a token; gradients are decorative chrome on a data card. _(med)_; Retry link uses text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 (line 863) — raw cyan instead of the --brand-cyan token for links/actions. _(med)_; text-white used ~15x for hero/data numbers (lines 313, 362, 427, 453, 588, 637, 667, 692, 841) instead of text-foreground — breaks dark-first inverse and light-mode derivation. _(med)_; Typography off fixed scale: numbers use text-xs/text-sm/text-2xl/text-lg/text-base Tailwind sizes (lines 309, 412, 416, 427, 667, 681, 841, 843) rather than data-xs/data-sm/data-base/price-* tokens. Hero anchor/flip (text-2xl font-black) should use a price token. _(med)_; THE signature 3D moment is missing: this is the GEX page yet there is no React-Three-Fiber gamma surface (cyan-lit peaks / purple valleys, OrbitControls, 2D fallback). The law names this as the one sanctioned in-terminal 3D, and this is exactly the surface for it. _(med)_; Glyph arrows used as icons in Gamma Walls badges: 'MAGNET ↑' / 'RESIST'/'BOUNCE' with ↑ glyph (line 608). Law mandates lucide icons, no glyph/emoji-as-icon. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions anywhere. Hover states are color-only (quick-select buttons, refresh, sniper cards) with no border-brighten + translateY(-1px) / press scale(0.98). Bars use bare transition-all duration-500 (line 595). _(low)_; Inconsistent radius/padding: cells/badges use rounded, buttons/inputs/cards rounded-lg; card padding varies (px-4 py-3 vs p-0 vs p-6 vs p-8) rather than the density-driven 8-10px standard and a single radius token. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong skeleton: header (GAMMA // EXPOSURE micro-label + Crosshair title + regime badge + refresh), symbol bar (INDEX quick-select + search + active symbol/price), lg:grid-cols-4 with HeatmapPanel (col-span-3) beside stacked KeyLevels + AIAnalysis sidebar, then GammaWalls full-width, SniperSignals 2-col grid, explainer, timestamp. Add ONE hero block: a lazy-loaded R3F gamma surface (col-span-3) with the 2D table heatmap as the reduced-motion / no-WebGL fallback, switched by a segmented 3D/2D toggle. Normalize card padding to the 8-10px density standard and a single radius token.
- **Apply:** 1) Rewrite gexCellColor() to use GEX tokens: positive ramps -> var(--gex-positive)/var(--gex-positive-bg), negative -> var(--gex-negative)/var(--gex-negative-bg), empty baseline -> var(--gex-neutral-bg); drop all emerald-*/red-NNN classes. 2) Flip marker+price -> text-[var(--gex-flip)] and flip ring colored from --gex-flip; max-gamma Star -> text-[var(--gex-star)]; extreme/alert ring -> --gex-alert. 3) Kill every emerald-*/red-NNN/violet-*/amber-*/slate-*/yellow-400: bias/regime POSITIVE->trade-bullish, NEGATIVE->trade-bearish, NEUTRAL/yellow-400->trade-neutral; keep trade-bullish/bearish for sniper LONG/SHORT direction (real money/direction). 4) Remove shadow-[0_0_15px_...] and both gradient backgrounds on sniper cards; express conviction via elevation (bg-card -> --secondary/--surface-overlay-solid) + border/border-strong, not glow. 5) Retry link + active/links -> text-[var(--brand-cyan)]; ensure focus rings inherit --brand-cyan. 6) Swap text-white -> text-foreground everywhere. 7) Apply font-mono + tabular-nums + slashed-zero to every number and move sizes onto the fixed scale (data-xs/sm/base; price-md/lg for KeyLevels anchor/flip hero). 8) Replace ↑ glyph in wall badges with lucide ArrowUp/ArrowDown.
- **Components:** Use canonical Card/CardHeader/CardContent/CardTitle, Badge (variant=outline), Button, Input, Separator (already imported) but drive their colors from tokens. Replace the hand-rolled <input>/<button> search + quick-select with canonical Input + Button so focus rings inherit --brand-cyan. Introduce a shared lazy GexSurface3D (R3F) component + a SegmentedToggle for 3D/2D. Reuse the existing micro-label, regime Badge, and stat-row patterns.
- **Motion/3D:** Primary opportunity: implement the sanctioned R3F gamma surface here — strikes x expirations grid extruded by GEX magnitude, cyan-lit peaks / purple valleys per the law, OrbitControls clamped, lazy-loaded, with the current 2D table as the prefers-reduced-motion / no-WebGL fallback. Add framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth micro-interactions: card hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98); animate gamma-wall bars in with a width spring (<=300ms); stagger sniper-card entrance. All gated on prefers-reduced-motion.

Purple is used as a structural theme color for Squeeze + Breakout + Intelligence sections (banner bg, card borders, titles, strength bars, links). Orange themes the entire WSB/Social tab. The law permits exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan); purple/orange are not tokens at all. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors leaking into non-money chrome. emerald/green and red are used for generic card borders by sentiment (L1438-1439 WSB cards), badge chrome, and S/A/B grade badges (L2352-2353) — grade is not money/direction. trade-bullish green is also used for decorative ring-1 avatar circles (L710) and the section accent border on the watchlist panel. The law says bullish/bearish encode ONLY money/direction. _(high)_; Teal is over-used. Law demotes #14b8a6 (--brand-teal) to logo stripe + GEX surface ONLY, yet the Bull Flag tab uses --brand-teal as its primary accent throughout (CardTitle hover L2342, flag icon L2360/2481, all 8 Pattern-DNA labels L2491-2519, signal '+' markers L2455, RSI sweet-spot L2407) plus a teal-950/600/200 intro banner (L2306-2308). _(high)_; Ticker symbols rendered as plain '$AAPL' text strings (L1456, L1606) instead of canonical uppercase-mono-600 +0.04em ticker treatment; the literal '$' prefix is glued to the ticker rather than treating it as money. Squeeze table ticker (L1948) uses ad-hoc 'font-mono font-bold tracking-wider' instead of a shared ticker class. _(med)_; Non-tabular / non-mono numbers in several spots. Earnings 'Report Date' and surge price/volume rely on default sans; safeToFixed values inside sans spans (e.g. squeeze table uses tabular-nums but not font-mono on price L1956). Mixed: some numbers font-mono, some only tabular-nums, some neither — inconsistent application of the mono+tabular-nums+slashed-zero rule. _(med)_; text-black hardcoded on MEDIUM surge badge (L1165 'bg-[var(--trade-neutral)] text-black') and text-foreground on colored severity badges (L1163-1166) — should use a token, not literal black/white. _(med)_; Density off-spec. Many cards use p-4 padding and gap-4/gap-6 grids, text-lg/text-xl titles, w-12 h-12 empty-state icons — comfortable, not the compact default (card pad 8-10px, base 12px). No data-density awareness; spacing is hand-tuned per section. Surge/watchlist panels ARE compact (good), but the tab bodies are not, creating inconsistent density across one page. _(med)_; Inconsistent radius and elevation. Surge + watchlist use --surface-raised/--surface-base ladder (correct), but all tab bodies use bare <Card> plus ad-hoc 'bg-card/50', 'bg-muted/30', 'bg-purple-950/20' overlays and rounded-lg vs rounded vs rounded-full mixed freely — no consistent 4-step elevation ladder; depth conveyed by tinted glow-ish bg washes rather than bg-step + border. _(med)_; Decorative pulse/animate on chrome. 'animate-pulse' on the HOT count badge (L1109) and on ANALYZING text (L1285) is ambient/decorative motion on non-essential chrome; law bans ambient glow/decorative motion in-terminal and requires prefers-reduced-motion respect (not present). _(low)_; Raw <table> in squeeze tab (L1921) and raw <a href> link (L1989 'view →' with a literal arrow char) instead of canonical Table component + lucide icon / wouter Link; the '→' is a text glyph standing in for an icon. _(low)_; Hover/press motion not to spec. Cards rely on legacy 'hover-elevate' + 'transition-all'; law specifies framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth, hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98). No translateY/scale press feedback on the clickable surge/WSB/earnings cards. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing IA (top filter row + Surge Scanner strip + 6 strategy tabs: Breakouts/Squeeze/Day Trade/Swing/Bull Flag/WSB). Unify the whole page onto the compact Bloomberg-dense rhythm already used by the Surge + Watchlist panels: wrap every tab body in the same '--surface-raised' panel shell with a 2px-tall uppercase-mono section header bar over '--surface-base'/40, drop comfortable p-4/gap-6 down to compact card pad 8-10px / row 28px / base 12px, and gate all spacing through data-density rather than per-section hand-tuning. #### flow-heatmap  · M effort · 11 issues
*Today:* A sector-treemap flow board: a 4-up KPI bar over a 3-column grid of sector cards holding bull/bear-tinted ticker tiles, plus a fixed bottom-right detail panel on tile click. It partly consumes the token system (componentStyles.card/button, trade-bullish/bearish/neutral) but is comfortable-density, leans on sans for numbers, hardcodes tile rgba, sprinkles emoji glyphs, and introduces an off-palette violet accent.

- **Violations:** Emoji used as icons, banned by law (lucide only): ⚡ in H1 (line 99), ↻ refresh glyph in button (line 109), ✕ close glyph (line 252), plus ticker.badges.join('') rendering raw badge strings/emoji in tiles and detail header (lines 226, 247). _(high)_; Second chromatic accent violates 'exactly ONE accent' rule: WHALES KPI uses text-violet-400 (line 158, accent='purple'). Purple is reserved for the GEX gamma-valley 3D moment, not flow chrome. Should be neutral or cyan. _(high)_; Hardcoded rgba colors in components instead of tokens: tile backgrounds rgba(16,185,129,...) / rgba(239,68,68,...) (lines 208-209) and a raw neutral gray rgba(100,100,100,0.1) (line 210). Law forbids raw rgb/hex in components; must derive from --trade-bullish-bg / --trade-bearish-bg tokens or hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Numbers not rendered in mono with tabular-nums: KPI values are text-2xl font-bold sans (line 163), all Stat values are font-bold sans (line 318), price/IV/change/sentiment/breadth %, and bull/bear $M split labels (lines 269-270) and sector greenCount/totalCount (line 187) are sans. Law requires IBM Plex Mono + tabular-nums + slashed zero on EVERY number. _(high)_; Ticker symbols not styled per law (uppercase mono 600, +0.04em): rendered font-bold text-sm sans in tiles (line 223) and detail header (line 245). Should use componentStyles.text.ticker. _(med)_; Hero/number sizes off the fixed scale: text-3xl H1 (line 99), text-2xl KPI (line 163), text-xl/text-base/text-sm stats — none map to the micro/data-xs/sm/base/lg or price-sm/md/lg scale. _(med)_; Comfortable density instead of compact default: p-6 page, p-4 KPI/detail, gap-3, mb-6, no data-density attribute. Law mandates Bloomberg-compact as default (card pad 8-10px, 28px rows). _(med)_; Micro-labels use arbitrary text-[10px] + tracking-wider rather than canonical text.statLabel / text.chromeLabel tokens; tracking not in the 0.05-0.08em spec (lines 162, 187, 294, 317). _(low)_; Missing the motion signature: tiles/cards use only transition-colors; no framer-motion hover (border-brighten + translateY(-1px)) or press scale(0.98), and the 60s auto-refresh has no prefers-reduced-motion consideration. The LIVE FLOW dot (line 139) is static, not the canonical status.live pulse. _(low)_; Detail panel is a hand-rolled fixed overlay using shadow-2xl (ambient/decorative shadow leans against the no-glow rule) and ad-hoc border-[var(--brand-cyan)]/40 rather than a canonical panel/Sheet component; close affordance is an unlabeled glyph button. _(low)_; Loading and is-fetching states are bare text ('Loading flow heatmap...', '...') with no skeleton/spinner consistent with other surfaces. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 3-zone structure (KPI bar → sector treemap grid → detail rail) but tighten to compact density and wrap the page in data-density='compact'. Header: drop the ⚡, replace with a lucide Zap icon (size 16, text-foreground) beside an h1 at data-lg/sectionHeader.title; subtitle timestamp in font-mono text-muted-foreground. Use componentStyles.container.wide for the outer wrap. KPI bar: componentStyles.panel.bloomberg with chrome header ('LIVE FLOW' using componentStyles.text.chromeLabel + componentStyles.status.live pulsing dot, timestamp in font-mono). Sector grid: 3-col on lg, gap-1.5/gap-2 compact. Detail rail: replace the hand-rolled fixed div with the canonical Sheet/side-panel component (or panel.bloomberg fixed) and drop shadow-2xl.
- **Apply:** Replace text-violet-400 WHALES accent with neutral text-foreground (or cyan if it must pop) — kill the 'purple' accent option entirely. Convert tile backgrounds to token-derived: use color-mix or the --trade-bullish-bg / --trade-bearish-bg tokens with opacity driven by intensity via CSS var, and the neutral tile to bg-secondary/40 instead of rgba(100,100,100). Add font-mono tabular-nums to KPI values, all Stat values, price/IV/change/sentiment, bull/bear $M, sector counts. Map Stat color 'emerald'→trade-bullish, 'red'→trade-bearish, 'zinc'→foreground (rename, keep token mapping). Move numeric sizing to the data-*/price-* scale (KPI → price-md, stats → data-base/data-sm). Standardize micro-labels to tracking-[0.06em].
- **Components:** componentStyles.card.default / panel.bloomberg for KPI + sector cards; componentStyles.button.compact for refresh (already used — keep); componentStyles.text.ticker for all symbols; componentStyles.text.statLabel + chromeLabel for micro-labels; componentStyles.text.dataValue/price/change for every number; componentStyles.status.live for the LIVE dot; lucide icons (Zap, RefreshCw, X) replacing ⚡/↻/✕; replace ticker.badges emoji with componentStyles.badge.* pills (cyan/bullish/bearish/gold/ai) mapped from badge codes; canonical Sheet for the detail rail; a Skeleton component for loading.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion to ticker tiles and sector cards: hover = border brightens to --border-strong + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98), 150-200ms on --ease-out-smooth, all gated behind prefers-reduced-motion. Animate the detail rail in with a 200ms slide/fade. No new 3D here — the gamma-surface remains the single 3D moment in the sibling GEX matrix tab; this heatmap should stay flat and fast. Make the LIVE dot use the canonical pulse and pause it under reduced-motion.

#### terminal-heatmap  · M effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** MATRIX heatmap cell backgrounds are hardcoded raw rgba bypassing GEX tokens (gex-expiry-matrix.tsx L598-600): bgColor = isPositive ? `rgba(34,197,94,...)` (green) : `rgba(168,85,247,...)` (purple). Spot/flip/max are chrome+direction markers that should map to tokens — anchor/spot to --brand-cyan (focus/active per law) or --gex tokens, flip/max to --gex-flip / --gex-star which already exist. None should be raw Tailwind palette classes. _(high)_; Raw rgba in arbitrary class: drawSpotLineBelow box-shadow shadow-[0_2px_0_rgba(251,191,36,0.4)] (L563) hardcodes amber rgb. Must be a token. _(med)_; Emoji/glyph used as icons instead of lucide (law: lucide only, no emoji): HTML-entity glyphs ► (&#x25BA;), ⚡ (&#x26A1;), ⊙ (&#x2299;), ★ literal star (L574-576, L482, L613), and literal ▲/▼ triangles in truncation rows (L527, L629). Should be lucide icons (ChevronUp/ChevronDown, Zap, Crosshair/Target, Star). _(med)_; PAGE: ALL STRIKES / COLLAPSE toggle (terminal-heatmap.tsx L70) active state uses hardcoded border-amber-400/30 text-amber-400 bg-amber-400/10 for generic chrome. Per law the single chromatic accent for active/selected chrome is --brand-cyan; amber (--trade-neutral) encodes money/direction only, not UI state. _(med)_; TOOLBAR: VEX toggle active state uses bg-violet-400/15 text-violet-400 (heatseeker-toolbar.tsx L169) — violet is not a token; should map to --gex-negative (VEX/puts are purple in the GEX palette) or --brand-cyan for active chrome. Same violet-400 active in matrix VEX toggle L333. _(med)_; TOOLBAR: spinning RefreshCw turns text-amber-400 while fetching (L244) — loading/busy is chrome, not money/direction; amber misuse. Use muted-foreground or --brand-cyan. _(low)_; Number/typography: numbers in cells/labels use font-mono + tabular-nums (good) but rely on JetBrains Mono via --font-mono, and the law specifies IBM Plex Mono with slashed-zero. NOTE: this is a token-file-wide mismatch (index.css ships DM Sans / JetBrains Mono, law wants IBM Plex Sans/Mono), not unique to this surface — flag at the token layer, not here. _(low)_; No data-density attribute consumed; spacing is hand-tuned (py-1.5 px-3, text-[8px]/[9px]/[10px] arbitrary sizes rather than the fixed micro/data-xs/data-sm scale). Sizes drift from the canonical 9/11/12/13/15px scale (8px and 10px are off-scale). Density should be one attribute, sizes from the scale. _(low)_; Error/loading states (terminal-heatmap.tsx L84-107) correctly use --gex-negative / --gex-positive tokens, but those are GEX direction colors being used for generic load/error status — acceptable on a GEX surface but slightly conflates status with direction; minor. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Layout is already correct and compact: fixed-height flex column (h-[calc(100dvh-80px)]), sticky toolbar on --surface-raised, sticky table head + sticky STRIKE column, floating spot pill, scroll container with arrow-key paging. Keep the structure. Only refinements: (1) add data-density='compact' on the page root and drive cell padding/row height from it instead of hand-tuned py-1.5/px-3; (2) snap all off-scale text sizes (text-[8px], text-[10px]) onto the canonical scale: micro 9px for labels, data-xs 11px for cell numbers, data-sm 12px for the spot pill price; (3) add a small heatmap color legend (call/put + intensity) since none exists.

- **Layout:** Keep the strong newer shell: MarketPulseHeader ticker strip + max-w-[1600px] compact container + main feed (left) / collapsible 72-unit insights aside (right). Convert the right-aside cards (Top Conviction, Session Stats, Hot Symbols, Watchlist) to the canonical .bloomberg-panel + .panel-header pattern with mono .panel-title labels. Replace the two legacy sub-pages (TomorrowSurgersSubPage lines ~709-1070, BestSetupsSubPage ~1075+) wholesale: render every prediction/setup through the canonical SignalCard (the adapters predictionToSignal/setupToSignal already exist and are wired) and delete the hand-rolled fallback card markup with its violet/amber gradients. Convert the four tier-count summary tiles to .metric-block (label + .metric-value mono). Standardize section headers to .section-chrome with a mono .section-label instead of gradient icon tiles.
- **Apply:** Global find/replace the 324 palette literals to tokens: emerald/green→var(--trade-bullish), red/rose→var(--trade-bearish), amber/yellow→var(--trade-neutral), cyan→var(--brand-cyan); ELIMINATE violet/purple/orange/indigo/pink/blue/gray entirely — recolor those surfaces to neutral (bg-card, bg-muted/30, border-border, text-muted-foreground) since they're chrome, not money. Delete all 30 bg-gradient-to-* card fills and the absolute 'glow' overlay divs; depth = bg-card + border-border (hover:border-border-strong). Remove hover:shadow-{color}-500/10. Replace ⚡ emoji (lines 287,1158) with <Zap className='w-3 h-3'/> or <AlertTriangle/>. Swap text-2xl font-bold stat numbers → price-display-md/data-lg + font-mono tabular-nums. Normalize rounded-xl/2xl → rounded-lg (--radius-card). Tighten legacy p-4/gap-4/mb-6 to compact p-2.5/gap-2.5 and let data-density drive it. SEPARATE PR: fix index.css --font-sans→IBM Plex Sans, --font-mono→IBM Plex Mono, and retarget ::selection / .shadcn-card hover glow from teal to brand-cyan / border-only.
- **Components:** SignalCard (canonical Oracle-style card) for ALL idea/prediction/setup cards — already imported, lean on it and drop fallbacks. bloomberg-panel/panel-header for every sidebar + sub-page panel. metric-block for stat tiles. accent-card--bullish/--bearish/--cyan (left 2px bar) to encode direction on rows instead of full gradient backgrounds. data-cell/price-display-* + ticker-font for all numbers and symbols. Badge with token-driven variants via componentStyles.badge.* (already partially used) instead of inline bg-violet-500/20 etc.
- **Motion/3D:** Adopt framer-motion for card hover (border-brighten + translateY(-1px), 150-200ms ease-out-smooth) and press scale(0.98), replacing transition-all duration-300. No 3D moment belongs on this surface (the single R3F GEX gamma surface lives on the GEX page); keep trade-desk flat and dense. Respect prefers-reduced-motion. Optional: reuse the existing .price-tick-up/down flash utility for live ticker updates in MarketPulseHeader.

#### gex-scanner  · M effort · 10 issues

- **Violations:** FONT FAMILIES ARE WRONG AT THE TOKEN ROOT. client/src/index.css defines --font-sans: 'DM Sans' (lines 272, 431) and --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono'/'Space Mono' (lines 274, 433, 527, 588). The law mandates IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono. Every word and every number on this surface renders in the wrong typeface. Foundation-level — the page consumes tokens correctly but the tokens resolve wrong. _(high)_; SECOND PSEUDO-ACCENT via --brand-gold (#f59e0b, byte-identical to --trade-neutral). Used as decorative chrome, not money/direction: SORT control active state (gex-scanner.tsx L223 'border-[var(--brand-gold)]/50 text-[var(--brand-gold)] bg-[var(--brand-gold)]/10'), top-pick label (L158), 'Top Plays' header + hover (hub-panels L147, L157), INDEX tier badge (confluence-row L66), the 'IDEA' send-to-desk button idle state (confluence-row L143, hub-panels L251), and sector star hover (hub-panels L304). Law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent (cyan); amber/#f59e0b is reserved for the neutral money/direction semantic ONLY. This is the single biggest color violation on the surface. _(high)_; GEX domain colors yellow/purple/orange are outside the token allowlist. --gex-positive #facc15 (yellow), --gex-negative #a855f7 (purple), --gex-flip #fb923c (orange), --gex-star #facc15 drive the score breakdown bars (confluence-row L104-108), call/put wall levels, market-tape regime bar, top-play score bar, and sector bars. The law sanctions teal #14b8a6 for the GEX surface but says nothing about a yellow/purple/orange ramp; these read as additional chromatic accents. Defensible as domain semantics (call wall vs put wall vs flip) but currently undocumented in the law and visually competes with the cyan accent + bullish/bearish money colors. _(med)_; Raw Tailwind palette colors not via tokens. hub-panels.tsx L61 hardcodes 'text-violet-400' for after-hours session; design-tokens.ts badge.ai uses 'violet-500/violet-400' (L57) and badge.delayed uses 'amber-500/amber-400' (L59); button.primary uses 'from-teal-500 to-cyan-500' (L38). These bypass hsl(var(--token)). _(med)_; Typography off the fixed scale. Surface uses text-[8px] (gex-scanner L186/L211/L230, hub-panels L250/L297 — below the 9px micro floor), text-base (confluence-row L82), and text-[13px] (hub-panels L208) ad hoc instead of the named micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 tokens. Sizes are close but not snapped to the scale, and 8px is sub-spec. _(med)_; No framer-motion / signature micro-interaction. Hover is plain CSS 'transition-colors'/'transition-all' (confluence-row L72, all tab/sort buttons). Missing the law's default hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) and press = scale(0.98) on --ease-out-smooth. No in-component prefers-reduced-motion guard (only a global @media block at index.css L1745). _(low)_; Density not driven by data-density. The page hardcodes its own compact spacing (px-4 py-3 rows, space-y-3) and never reads the data-density attribute the law requires as the single density control. Cannot switch to comfortable/spacious. _(low)_; THE signature 3D moment is absent on its home surface. The law names the React-Three-Fiber gamma surface (cyan-lit peaks / purple valleys, OrbitControls, lazy-loaded, 2D fallback) as the one in-terminal 3D moment — and GEX is exactly where it belongs. This page renders only flat CSS breakdown bars and a 1px regime bar. Opportunity, not a regression. _(low)_; Emoji-as-icon: the max-gamma star uses the literal '★' glyph (confluence-row L202, hub-panels L306) instead of a lucide Star icon. Minor but the law says lucide only. _(low)_; Hardcoded rgba in component: Bar background 'bg-black/40' (confluence-row L188) instead of a surface/border token; opacity:0.7 inline magic numbers throughout the bars rather than token-driven. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong existing workflow-first IA (toolbar -> market tape -> top plays -> view/sort tabs -> confluence list -> collapsible sectors). It is already dense and scannable; do NOT restructure. Changes are skin-deep plus one hero addition: (1) Add a collapsible/lazy R3F gamma-surface panel between the MarketTape and TopPlays (or as an expandable on the selected confluence row) showing the per-ticker gamma profile — this is the surface's signature 3D moment. (2) Snap all text sizes to the named scale: replace text-[8px] -> data micro (min 9px) micro-label, text-base ticker -> data-lg, text-[13px] -> data-base. (3) Add data-density consumption at the page root so row height/padding flex.
- **Apply:** TOKENS: (1) Fix --font-sans/--font-mono in index.css to IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono (foundation fix, benefits all surfaces) and ensure font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums + slashed-zero on the mono face. (2) Demote --brand-gold everywhere on this surface: SORT active -> brand-cyan tokens (gex-scanner L223); Top Plays header -> brand-cyan or neutral chrome (hub-panels L147,157); IDEA button idle -> brand-cyan (confluence-row L143, hub-panels L251); INDEX tier badge -> neutral muted (confluence-row L66); sector star hover -> foreground/brand-cyan (hub-panels L304). (3) Replace text-violet-400 (hub-panels L61) with a token; if a third session color is truly needed, add a --session-* token rather than raw violet. (4) Replace bg-black/40 bar track with bg-muted/40 or a --surface token; drop inline opacity magic numbers in favor of bg-[var(--gex-*)]/70. (5) Either codify the GEX yellow/purple/orange ramp as named --gex-* tokens explicitly blessed in the law for this one surface, or remap call/put wall to trade-bullish/trade-bearish + flip to brand-cyan to collapse the extra accents.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical componentStyles from design-tokens.ts: card.default / card.inset / badge.bullish|bearish|cyan|default already used correctly — keep them. Replace componentStyles.card.accentGold on Top Plays (hub-panels L145) and the gold SORT/IDEA/INDEX/star usages with accentCyan + brand-cyan, OR introduce a sanctioned neutral-chrome treatment so amber is freed for money only. Swap the literal '★' for lucide <Star>. Route the score breakdown bars through a single tokenized <Bar> that takes a semantic role (wall/flip/regime) -> token, not a raw color string + bg-black/40.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion to ConfluenceRow and the tab/sort buttons: default hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = whileTap scale(0.98), 150-300ms on cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1) (--ease-out-smooth). Animate the workflow-tab content swap with a 200ms fade/slide. Lazy-load (React.lazy + Suspense) the R3F gamma surface with a 2D CSS fallback (the existing bars) for prefers-reduced-motion and low-power. Keep the RefreshCw spin (already present). Wrap all of it in a prefers-reduced-motion guard at component level, not just global.

#### client/src/pages/terminal-chart.tsx  · M effort · 9 issues

- **Violations:** Pervasive hardcoded Tailwind palette colors instead of tokens: regime badge + levels strip use emerald-400/500, orange-400/500, red-400/600, amber-400, violet-400, zinc-400/600/700, cyan-400/60 (lines 254-258, 341, 355-360, 369, 377, 388, 390, 405, 408, 416, 423, 435). Per law, trade-bullish/bearish green/red encode ONLY money/direction; GEX polarity is its own scale (yellow/purple via --gex-positive/--gex-negative). Mixing them is meaning corruption. _(high)_; Inconsistent GEX level color scheme that contradicts the file's own tokens. Call Wall rendered emerald, Put Wall red, Magnet cyan-400, Flip/MaxΓ amber — an ad-hoc green/red/amber/cyan palette, while index.css defines the GEX surface as yellow(--gex-positive)/purple(--gex-negative)/cyan(--projection-glow). The matrix/heatmap canonical components likely use the tokens, so the toolbar strip and the body will not match. _(high)_; Arbitrary off-scale font sizes throughout: text-[8px], text-[9px], text-[10px], text-[11px] (lines 195, 209, 223, 240, 272, 339, 343, 354, 377, 405, 432, 448, 460, 482, 507, 564, 575). Law fixes the scale at micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13; 8px is below the floor and the rest are raw px instead of scale classes. _(med)_; Glyph-as-icon: ★ used for Max Γ (line 423) and ● bullet for 'linked levels' marker (lines 341, 435). Law mandates lucide icons only, no emoji/glyph icons. _(med)_; Decorative/ambient color treatments banned by law: focus ring uses --gex-positive (line 195) rather than brand cyan focus token; isNear row uses bg-[var(--projection-glow)]/5 as a soft glow tint (line 561); loading uses animate-pulse on colored text (line 460). Focus should be cyan-driven; ambient glow is disallowed. _(low)_; Density hand-tuned (px-4, py-0.5/1.5/2/3, gap-2/5) rather than driven by the data-density attribute. Spacing is bespoke per element instead of the compact-default token system. _(low)_; Motion not on the framer-motion signature: buttons use CSS transition-colors only (no border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover, no scale(0.98) press). Tick flash holds 700ms (line 50) which is fine for a price flash but the fade transition is bespoke CSS, not the --ease-out-smooth 150-300ms band. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the three-zone layout (toolbar row / unified levels strip / view body) — it is well-structured and dense, no structural change needed. The fix is almost entirely a color + typography token sweep plus motion polish; the IA is sound.
- **Apply:** COLOR: Replace every hardcoded palette class with tokens. GEX polarity scale — Call Wall + positive/Max Γ -> text-[hsl(var(--gex-positive))] / bg via --gex-positive-bg; Put Wall + extremes -> --gex-negative / --gex-negative-bg (drop emerald/red). Magnet/zero-gamma -> --projection-glow / --projection-glow-soft (replace cyan-400). Flip boundary -> keep amber but route through --trade-neutral. Regime badge: positive_gamma -> --gex-positive token set, negative_gamma -> --gex-negative set, neutral -> --secondary/--muted-foreground (replace emerald/orange/zinc). NET GEX/VEX value: color by GEX polarity (--gex-positive when >0, --gex-negative when <0), NOT trade green/red, since this is exposure magnitude not money. flashStyle(): swap rgba(34,197,94)/rgba(239,68,68) for color-mix or rgba derived from --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish (price tick IS money direction, so trade tokens are correct here). VEX toggle active state: --gex-negative (purple) instead of violet-400. 'low OI'/'none' placeholders: text-muted-foreground (replace zinc-600). TYPOGRAPHY: map all arbitrary px to the scale — text-[8px]->micro/9px floor (raise to 9), [9px]->text-micro, [10px]->text-data-xs(11) or keep as micro-label class, [11px]->text-data-xs, body data->text-data-sm/base; apply the .data-label / micro-label utility (UPPERCASE sans-500, tracking 0.05-0.08em, muted) to the Pill labels which currently hand-roll uppercase+tracking. Confirm all numbers carry font-mono + tabular-nums + slashed-zero (mostly present; the Pill label dots and placeholders are fine). ICONS: replace ★ with lucide Star (w-3 h-3), replace ● linked marker with lucide Link2 or a small Dot from lucide, colored --projection-glow. FOCUS: ticker input focus:ring -> brand cyan focus token, not --gex-positive. DENSITY: wrap the page root with the data-density attribute and convert bespoke padding to density-aware spacing where a token exists.
- **Components:** Reuse the canonical GEX components already imported (GEXExpiryMatrix, GEXHeatmapGrid, GEXLevelBadge, CacheFreshnessIndicator, EngineStatusFooter, EmptyGexState) — these are correct. Extract the inline Pill (lines 337-347) and the regime badge (353-362) into shared gex components (e.g. GexLevelPill, GexRegimeBadge) so the token mapping lives in one place and matches the badge/matrix coloring. Use the standard micro-label/data-label text utility classes rather than inline uppercase+tracking strings.
- **Motion/3D:** Apply the framer-motion micro-interaction signature to the toolbar/view-pill buttons: hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98), 150-300ms on --ease-out-smooth, gated by prefers-reduced-motion. Keep the price tick flash but base its colors on --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish and respect reduced-motion. Remove the ambient bg-projection-glow/5 'isNear' glow and the animate-pulse loading glow (no ambient/decorative glow). This page is the natural launch point for the ONE signature 3D moment — the R3F gamma surface (cyan-lit peaks/purple valleys) belongs as a 4th view pill or replacing the Profile bars, lazy-loaded with a 2D fallback to the existing GEXHeatmapGrid.
