#### gex-shell  · S effort · 6 issues

- **Violations:** Global font tokens violate the two-family rule. --font-mono resolves to JetBrains Mono and --font-sans to DM Sans in index.css (lines 272-274, 431-433), not IBM Plex Sans/Mono as the law requires. The shell's font-mono header/labels therefore render the wrong family. This is a global index.css issue surfaced here, not shell-local. _(high)_; Arbitrary pixel font sizes instead of the fixed named type scale. The shell uses text-[11px], text-[10px], and text-[9px] for the subtitle, the Research deep-link line, and the tab hint. The codebase already defines named utilities in tailwind.config.ts (text-data-xs = 11px, plus micro 9px) that map to the law's scale. Hardcoded px bypasses the token system. _(med)_; Inherited banned glow on active tab. QETabs (the canonical component this shell renders) applies shadow-[0_0_12px_-2px_var(--brand-cyan)] to the active tab, an ambient/decorative cyan glow. The law states depth comes from bg-step + border, not glow, and explicitly bans ambient/decorative glow in-terminal. Manifests on every tab switch in this shell. _(med)_; Dead/unreachable buckets tab. The Tab type and the render block both include 'buckets' (lines 28, 88-98) with a full 'coming next sprint' QECard, but the TABS array (lines 30-36) no longer contains a buckets entry, so VALID_TABS excludes it and useTabState can never resolve to 'buckets'. The buckets branch is unreachable dead code. _(low)_; Stale doc comment. The file header (lines 1-12) documents a 'Per-Symbol' tab and a 6-tab layout (Hub/Per-Symbol/Matrix/Heatmap/Buckets/Analysis) that no longer matches the actual 5-tab array. Per-Symbol was intentionally moved to Research (noted at lines 38-39) but the top comment was never updated. _(low)_; Header h1 uses font-mono for a word-label brand title ('GEX Hub'). Per the law, mono is for numbers/tickers; 'GEX Hub' is brand chrome. Defensible as a ticker-like brand mark, but strictly the title text should be sans. Minor. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing structure — it is correct and already compact: header (title + subtitle + Research deep-link) → RotationBrief → QETabs (prefixLabel VIEW) → one-line tab hint → PageErrorBoundary>Suspense lazy tab content. The shell's job is layout/routing and it does it cleanly. Changes are token hygiene and dead-code removal, not a restructure. Optionally move the standalone tab-hint line (lines 77-79) to render only when a hint exists, and consider folding it into a single QESection header to reduce two muted-text lines stacked under the tab bar.
- **Apply:** 1) Replace arbitrary px sizes with named scale: subtitle text-[11px] -> text-data-xs; the Research deep-link text-[10px] -> text-micro (or text-data-xs); tab-hint text-[9px] -> text-micro. Keep text-muted-foreground opacity ramps but prefer text-muted-foreground without the /70,/60,/50 magic opacities or standardize to one muted level. 2) Remove the banned glow from QETabs ACTIVE_VARIANT (tabs.tsx lines 63-67): drop the shadow-[0_0_12px...] segment for cyan/gold, rely on bg-[var(--brand-cyan)]/15 + ring for the active state per the elevation/border rule. 3) Delete the dead buckets branch: remove 'buckets' from the Tab type (line 28) and the render block (lines 88-98), or re-add a buckets entry to TABS if it is meant to be visible (as a disabled tab with the QETabItem disabled flag, which already renders a 'soon' chip). 4) Update the file header doc comment (lines 1-12) to the real 5-tab set and the Per-Symbol->Research note. 5) Cyan usages (h1 accent span, Research link, code tag, Loader2 spinner) are all correct — brand-cyan for brand/link/focus — leave them. 6) Fix global font tokens in index.css to IBM Plex Sans/Mono (out-of-scope for this shell but flagged as the upstream cause).
- **Components:** Reuse canonical QETabs and QECard (already used). For the buckets placeholder, the QECard 'coming soon' pattern is fine but is currently dead; prefer expressing it as a disabled QETabItem (uses the built-in 'soon' chip) rather than a separate render branch. No new components needed — this surface should consume primitives, not define them.
- **Motion/3D:** No new motion needed in the shell. The one motion fix is removing the ambient cyan glow on active tabs (handled in QETabs) to comply with the no-decorative-glow rule; keep the existing 150ms transition-all on tab hover (border-brighten style) which already matches the micro-interaction spec. The signature 3D GEX gamma surface lives in child tab content (matrix/analysis), not the shell.

### P1 — important  (30)

#### pricing  · M effort · 13 issues

- **Violations:** Ambient/decorative glow banned in-terminal: <FloatingBubblesBackground/> (client/src/components/floating-bubbles-background.tsx) renders a continuous requestAnimationFrame canvas of cyan/purple radial-gradient bubbles behind the whole page. This is exactly the ambient/decorative motion-glow the law forbids in-terminal, it hardcodes hsla() colors (hue 280/188), and it has NO prefers-reduced-motion guard so it never stops for reduced-motion users. _(high)_; Second chromatic accent everywhere: the page uses purple as a co-primary accent (purple-400/500, from-purple-500/10, bg-purple-500/20, text-purple-300, the FlaskConical beta icon, the Pro plan icon+title+gradient, BETA badges). Law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan); purple is only sanctioned for the GEX surface. All purple here is chrome/decoration. _(high)_; Wrong cyan and raw Tailwind palette instead of tokens: cyan-400/cyan-500 (Tailwind #22d3ee-adjacent but NOT the token) are hardcoded for the accent instead of hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) / text-brand. Brand accent must come from the --brand-cyan token, never the Tailwind cyan-* scale. _(high)_; Raw hex surface bypasses the elevation ladder: hero header uses bg-[#0a1525]/90 with border-white/10 and backdrop-blur-xl — a hardcoded navy hex that is not on the neutral L0-L3 grayscale ladder and not a token. border-white/10 is also a raw color. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors misused as chrome/decoration: green-500/emerald-500 gradient + border on the 'Save 2 months' annual banner, and amber/amber-500 (border-l-amber-500, bg-amber-500/10) used decoratively. trade-bullish/neutral may encode ONLY money/direction; a billing-cadence promo banner and a disclaimer accent are chrome, not P&L. (The disclaimer's amber-as-warning is borderline acceptable, but border-l-amber-500 should be the token, not raw amber-500.) _(high)_; slate-* hardcoded: Free-plan icon tile uses from-slate-500/20 to-slate-400/10 — raw Tailwind gray off-ladder instead of a neutral token (--secondary/--muted). _(med)_; Decorative drop-shadow glow on logo: img has drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,212,255,0.4)] — raw rgba glow, both a hardcoded color and the ambient-glow-as-decoration the law bans (and #00d4ff is yet another off-token cyan). _(med)_; Numbers not in mono / not tabular: getPeriod ('/mo','/year') price suffix is mono but the '~25% OFF', 'Save ~25%', 'Save {savings}% vs monthly' percentages, and badge percentages are rendered in sans (no font-mono, no tabular-nums slashed-zero). Every number — including % — must be IBM Plex Mono with tabular-nums lining-nums. _(med)_; Font family is wrong at the token level: --font-mono in index.css resolves to 'JetBrains Mono', not IBM Plex Mono, and there is no IBM Plex Sans wired as --font-sans. So even the font-mono numbers on this page violate the two-families-only rule. (Token-level fix, but it directly degrades this page's hero $39/$79 numerals.) _(med)_; Typographic scale ignored: micro-labels and badges use ad-hoc sizes text-[10px]/text-[9px]/text-xs/text-sm/text-4xl rather than the fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 / price-*). The hero $price uses text-4xl instead of a price-lg token. Micro-labels ('Research Platform') use tracking-widest rather than the spec 0.05-0.08em. _(med)_; Inconsistent radius: mix of rounded-xl (header/banners), rounded-lg (cards/buttons), and rounded (badges). No single radius token applied; law expects consistent token-driven radius. _(low)_; No density awareness: padding is p-6/p-8, generous gap-6/gap-8, spacing tuned for a marketing look. Page does not honor the data-density attribute / compact defaults (though as a public marketing page this is the least-bad violation). _(low)_; Italic brand wordmark: h2 'Quant Edge Labs' uses italic — not in the type system (IBM Plex Sans weights 400/500/600/700, no italic style specified). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 3-column plan grid (grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3) and overall top-to-bottom flow (header → disclaimer → beta banner → hero → billing toggle → cards → footer), but neutralize the chrome. Remove <FloatingBubblesBackground/> entirely (delete the import + render). Replace the hero's raw bg-[#0a1525]/90 navy box with a neutral elevated card on the L1/L2 ladder (bg-card border border-border, or the canonical glass-card surface for this marketing page) and drop the from-cyan-500/10 gradient overlay. Collapse the two stacked promo banners (annual-discount + beta) into tighter rows, and switch all card containers to a single radius token. Drop the per-plan colored icon-tile gradients in favor of a single neutral tile (bg-secondary) with the accent expressed only on the ONE popular plan.
- **Apply:** Tokens/classes: (1) Accent → replace every cyan-400/cyan-500/#00d4ff/cyan-500/10 with text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] / border-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] / the brand utility; restrict the cyan accent to the single 'Most Popular' (Advanced) card's left border + price + CTA. (2) Purple → remove from the terminal palette: render the Pro plan, all BETA badges, and the early-access banner in NEUTRAL chrome (bg-secondary, text-muted-foreground, border-border) with at most the cyan accent; do not reintroduce purple (it is GEX-only). (3) Surfaces → hero & banners use bg-[hsl(var(--card))]/bg-[hsl(var(--secondary))] + border-[hsl(var(--border))]/border-[hsl(var(--border-strong))] on the L0-L3 ladder; delete bg-[#0a1525] and border-white/10 and the logo drop-shadow rgba glow. (4) Semantic colors → keep --trade-bullish ONLY where it represents real money saved IF you must, but prefer recasting the annual-savings banner as neutral chrome with a cyan CTA; replace raw green-500/emerald-500/amber-500 with tokens; the disclaimer warning may use var(--trade-neutral) sparingly via a token class, not raw amber-500. (5) Numbers → wrap EVERY numeral (prices, '/mo', '/year', all % savings, badge percents) in font-mono with font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums and slashed-zero; map $price to a price-lg/price-md token size, suffixes to data-sm. (6) Type → micro-labels ('RESEARCH PLATFORM') use uppercase sans-500 tracking 0.05-0.08em muted; move sizes to the fixed scale (micro/data-xs/data-sm/data-base/data-lg); remove italic from the wordmark. Token-level follow-ups (outside this file): point --font-mono at IBM Plex Mono and add --font-sans = IBM Plex Sans. Canonical components: keep Button (use the cyan/primary variant only for the single popular CTA, glass-secondary/outline for the rest), Switch, and the glass-card/hover-elevate utilities already in index.css (hover-elevate already gives the lawful border-brighten + translateY micro-interaction).
- **Components:** Button (cyan primary variant for the one popular CTA; glass-secondary/outline elsewhere), Switch (billing toggle), glass-card + hover-elevate utilities from index.css, lucide icons (already used — no emoji present, good), SEOHead. Introduce/standardize a Badge component for BETA/Coming-Soon/Soon chips so they share one neutral token style instead of three ad-hoc inline spans.
- **Motion/3D:** No 3D here. Replace banned ambient motion with lawful micro-interactions only: rely on the existing hover-elevate utility (border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale(0.98)) on cards and CTAs via framer-motion 150-300ms on var(--ease-out-smooth). Add a subtle 150-200ms cross-fade/height transition on the billing Monthly↔Yearly toggle so prices swap smoothly. Everything must be wrapped to respect prefers-reduced-motion. The deleted FloatingBubbles canvas is the single biggest motion fix.

#### automations  · L effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** Semantic green (--trade-bullish / emerald-500) is used as generic brand/chrome everywhere it should NOT encode money: page header icon + title accent, the 'Automations Hub' Zap badge, ALL active-tab states (data-[state=active]:bg-emerald-500/10 data-[state=active]:text-[var(--trade-bullish)] on every TabsTrigger), the Options-Bot card, Small-Account card, Quick-Action 'Market Scanner' icon/button, the Settings 'Capital Allocation' & 'Options Strategy' card headers, and the Shield section heading. Law: bullish/bearish/neutral encode ONLY money/direction; the single chromatic accent for focus/active/brand is brand-cyan. This is the dominant violation across the file. _(high)_; Massive hardcoded Tailwind palette usage bypassing tokens: emerald-500/10, slate-900/20, slate-900/60, purple-500/10, purple-500/20, purple-500/30, purple-400, amber-500/5, amber-500/10, amber-500/20, amber-500/30, amber-500/40, red-500/5, red-500/10, red-500/20, green-500/20, green-500/30, green-600, blue-400, cyan-500, cyan-600/700, orange-400. Law: never hardcode palette colors in components; consume hsl(var(--token)) / named tokens only. _(high)_; Arbitrary chromatic accents with NO token backing used as category colors: Futures bot = purple, Crypto bot = amber, Quant scanner = blue-400, Social Sentiment = purple-400, Futures allocation = blue-400, Crypto allocation = orange-400, Advanced Controls card = purple. Law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent (cyan) plus the three semantic money colors; purple/blue/orange are not in the permitted in-terminal set (purple is reserved for the GEX surface). _(high)_; Ambient/decorative glow + gradient washes banned by the law: bg-gradient-to-b from-background via-background to-slate-900/20 page wrapper; bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-500/5, from-emerald-500/10 to-slate-900/60, from-purple-500/10, from-amber-500/10, from-green-500/10 on bot cards; shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(239,68,68,0.1)] glow on immediate-exit rows; ExitIntelligence header card gradient. Law: depth via bg-step + border, NO ambient/decorative glow. _(high)_; Raw rgba hex literal in component: shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(239,68,68,0.1)] (ExitIntelligenceCard immediate row). Law forbids hardcoded rgb in components. _(med)_; Inconsistent / mismatched semantic badge styling in getExitWindowBadge: 'watch' uses bg-emerald-500/20 with text bullish but border-cyan-500/40 (mixes cyan border with green text); 'soon' mixes amber-500 bg with --trade-neutral text and amber-500/40 border; 'immediate' mixes bg-red-500/20 with --trade-bearish text and --trade-bearish/40 border. Token usage is half-applied — should be consistent --trade-* tokens for bg/text/border. _(med)_; Status dots/labels and 'ACTIVE' pills hardcode green-500 borders and bg-[var(--trade-bullish)]/20 as decorative chrome rather than a real status semantic; four bot cards each repeat the same green ACTIVE chip with green-500/30 borders. Active/idle state is chrome and should be cyan or neutral, not money-green. _(med)_; Number/typography: several numbers render without the mono treatment the law mandates for EVERY number. Win% and Open counts ('Win: {winRate}%', 'Open: {n}') in the four bot cards are plain sans spans; '{n} / 4' active counter and Badge counts mostly use font-mono but the scanner StatusBadge text and various inline counts are inconsistent. Hero P&L numbers use text-lg font-mono but not the price-display utility scale the law defines. _(med)_; Typography scale drift: header title is text-base, page subtitle is text-[10px] (below the micro 9 / data-xs 11 scale floor used loosely), settings H2 is text-xl font-bold, card titles range text-sm/text-base/text-base — no adherence to the fixed data-xs/sm/base/lg ladder or uppercase-micro-label convention for the many stat labels (Capital, P&L, Entry, Price). _(low)_; Radius inconsistency: mix of rounded-lg, rounded-md, rounded, rounded-xl (header), rounded-full across cards/badges/pills instead of the token radii (--radius-card 10px, --radius-button 8px, --radius-badge 4px). _(low)_; Density: card padding and spacing are comfortable (CardContent space-y-3/space-y-4, p-3/p-4, py-6 buttons, h-11 tabs, space-y-6 tab content) rather than the compact Bloomberg default (8-10px pad, 28px row, base 12px) driven by data-density. No data-density attribute consumed anywhere. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions; hover states are ad-hoc (hover:border-... only on scanner buttons, hover:bg-cyan-700 on a button). Misses the canonical border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover / scale(0.98) press signature. animate-pulse used on urgent badges is acceptable but the rest of the surface is static. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 5-tab IA (Trading Bots / Market Scanner / Positions / Signal Scanners / Settings) and the 1600px container, but recompress to compact density: header rounded-[var(--radius-card)] with bg-card border-border (drop the slate gradient + backdrop-blur), pad p-3. Convert the 4 bot cards into a uniform compact stat-card grid (md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4) where ALL four share identical neutral chrome (bg-card, border-border, hover border-strong) — the ONLY color on them is the P&L number (bullish/bearish) and an optional left accent-card stripe. Use the existing .metric-block / .accent-card / .stat-row CSS utilities for the Capital/P&L/Open/Win cells. Settings tab: keep 2-col card grid but neutralize all card border colors to --border and lead each card header with a lucide icon in muted-foreground, not a money color.
- **Apply:** Replace every hardcoded palette class with tokens: emerald-500/green-* chrome → bg-[hsl(var(--card))]/border-[hsl(var(--border))] with active/brand states using --brand-cyan (text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))], data-[state=active]:bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]/10 data-[state=active]:text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]); purple/blue/amber/orange category colors → neutral chrome (only retain --trade-* on actual P&L/direction numbers). Drop all bg-gradient-to-* washes and the slate-900/20 page wrapper (use bg-background). Remove shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(...)] glow; signal urgency via border-[hsl(var(--trade-bearish))] + animate-pulse on the badge only. Make getExitWindowBadge consistent: each window uses one --trade-* token for bg(/10), text, and border(/40) — no mixed cyan/green. Wrap EVERY number (Win%, Open, counts, allocations, DTE, deltas) in font-mono with tabular-nums; convert all stat labels (Capital, P&L, Entry, Price, Trades Today, Last Scan) to UPPERCASE sans-500 tracking-0.06em muted micro-labels. Normalize radii to --radius-card/button/badge. Add data-density-driven compact spacing.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical primitives already imported: Card/CardHeader/CardTitle/CardContent, Badge, Tabs, Switch, Slider, Select, Button. Adopt the index.css utility classes built for exactly this: .metric-block (Capital/P&L cells), .stat-row + .stat-label/.stat-value (Open/Win rows), .accent-card--cyan / --bullish / --bearish (left stripe instead of full gradient fills), .bloomberg-panel + .panel-header for the scanner cards, .ticker-font for symbols in ExitIntelligence, .price-display-sm/md for hero P&L. Centralize the repeated 'ACTIVE' status pill into one StatusDot component using cyan (chrome) not green.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion to the bot/scanner/quick-action cards: whileHover { y: -1, borderColor: brand } and whileTap { scale: 0.98 } at 150-200ms on --ease-out-smooth, all gated by prefers-reduced-motion. Animate tab-content mount with the existing animate-page-in opacity fade. No 3D moment belongs here (the single R3F gamma surface is GEX-only) — keep this surface flat and fast.

#### landing  · L effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** ONE-ACCENT rule shattered: section labels use 6 different chromatic colors as decorative chrome — text-[var(--trade-bullish)] (TRADE DESK, l.494), text-[var(--brand-gold)] (GEX HUB l.513 + PRICING l.586), text-cyan-400 (GEX TERMINAL l.531), text-purple-400 (WATCHLIST l.549), text-[var(--brand-teal)] (INDEX MODE l.569), and only CONVERGENCE (l.480) correctly uses --brand-cyan. Cyan is the sole permitted accent for non-semantic chrome. _(high)_; Semantic green used as brand/chrome everywhere it must not: hero accent word 'your edge' (l.377), eyebrow badge + pulse dot (l.364-367), engine icons (l.233), feature/pricing bullet dots (l.188, l.611), header LABS pill (l.299), market-status dot/text (l.96-97), search focus ring + 'Try' pills (l.448,466), search Analyze button (l.458). --trade-bullish/green may encode ONLY money/direction, never decoration or generic brand. These should be --brand-cyan (chrome/focus/links) or neutral. _(high)_; Raw Tailwind palette classes instead of tokens: bg-emerald-600 on the two primary CTAs (l.324, l.338), from-teal-600 to-cyan-600 gradient on Pro CTA (l.653), text-cyan-400 (l.531), text-purple-400 (l.549). purple has no token equivalent at all and is off-palette for any in-terminal surface. _(high)_; Raw hex literals in components: Discord brand #5865F2 hover (l.317), traffic-light dots #ff5f57/#febc2e/#28c840 (l.134-136), ShimmerButton background linear-gradient(#0d9488,#14b8a6) + shimmerColor rgba(20,184,166,.3) (l.398-399), BorderBeam #14b8a6/#06b6d4 (l.623), final-CTA shimmerColor #10b981 (l.671). Law: never hardcode hex; consume hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Teal (#14b8a6 / --brand-teal) used as a primary surface accent on the Pro pricing card border, BorderBeam, badge, and CTA gradient (l.622-653) and as the INDEX MODE label (l.569). Law demotes teal to logo-stripe + GEX surface ONLY; here it is a generic decorative/brand accent and should be cyan. _(high)_; ALL numbers correctly carry font-mono (LiveStats l.255-272, ticker % l.112, prices $0/$29 l.597/628), but font-variant-numeric tabular-nums/slashed-zero is not applied — they rely on font-mono alone. Hero/section number tokens (price-sm/md/lg) and the .data-cell/.ticker-font canonical classes are not used; raw text-3xl font-mono ad-hoc sizing instead of the fixed data scale. _(med)_; Magic/Aceternity-family effects in use: ShimmerButton (animated shimmer, l.395 & l.669), BorderBeam (rotating beam, l.623), NumberTicker. Law bans springs/Aceternity/Magic in-terminal; landing is a marketing surface so these are tolerable here, but the shimmer/beam should be cyan-toned and the BorderBeam currently uses banned teal->cyan hardcoded hex. _(med)_; Section-label micro-labels are font-mono uppercase (l.175, l.480 etc.). Law specifies micro-labels = UPPERCASE SANS 500 with tracking 0.05-0.08em; these use mono font-bold tracking-widest (too wide, wrong family/weight for a label vs a number). _(med)_; Hover/active states use ad-hoc hover:bg-white/5 and hover:text-white (l.107, l.309, l.336, l.694-696) and bg-white text-black on the Request Access button (l.329) — raw white not via --foreground/--primary tokens; breaks in light mode and bypasses the elevate system. _(med)_; Non-canonical radii: cards/screenshots use rounded-xl (l.130, l.593, l.622) while law's --radius-card is 10px (0.625rem ~ rounded-[0.625rem]); badges/pills mix rounded-full, rounded-lg, rounded-md, rounded ad hoc rather than --radius-button/--radius-badge. _(low)_; Motion durations exceed the 150-300ms law: SectionReveal 0.6s (l.39), hero image 0.7s (l.434); easing passed as string 'easeOut'/'easeInOut' rather than the --ease-out-smooth cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1). No prefers-reduced-motion guard on the framer-motion reveals or the animate-marquee ticker. _(low)_; Eyebrow badge dot uses animate-pulse (l.366) and the convergence/grids imply ambient decoration; law bans ambient/decorative glow and pulsing chrome (status pulse is acceptable only for live market state, not a static marketing badge). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong existing structure (fixed header + market ticker, two-col hero with ProductShot, alternating FeatureSections, EngineStrip, 2-up pricing, final CTA, footer) — the bones are good and on-brand for a dense quant terminal. Changes are color/type/motion normalization, not restructuring. Header: 14px row is fine. Consider tightening section vertical padding from py-16/24 toward the --section-gap rhythm for a denser, more terminal feel, but marketing breathing room is acceptable here.
- **Apply:** 1) Collapse ALL six section-label colors to one: text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] for every eyebrow (CONVERGENCE/TRADE DESK/GEX HUB/GEX TERMINAL/WATCHLIST/INDEX MODE/PRICING). Drop the labelColor prop entirely. 2) Hero accent word 'your edge', eyebrow badge, engine icons, all bullet dots, search focus ring, LABS pill, 'Try' pills -> swap --trade-bullish to --brand-cyan (these are chrome/brand, not money). Keep --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish ONLY on the live market-ticker % values and status dot (real direction). 3) Primary CTAs (Get Started / Open App / Start Free / Analyze): use bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] text-[hsl(var(--primary-foreground))] (or the Button primary variant) — remove bg-emerald-600, bg-white text-black, and the teal->cyan gradient. 4) Replace every raw hex: Discord hover via a --brand-blue-ish token or muted-foreground; traffic-light dots are acceptable as literal macOS chrome but move to a local const; ShimmerButton background/shimmerColor + BorderBeam colorFrom/To -> hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) / hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/.3). 5) Demote teal: Pro card border, BorderBeam, badge, CTA -> brand-cyan. 6) Add font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums + slashed-zero to all number spans (via the canonical .data-cell/.price-display classes which already define it). 7) hover:bg-white/5 -> hover:bg-[hsl(var(--foreground)/.05)] or hover-elevate; hover:text-white -> hover:text-foreground. 8) Eyebrows -> uppercase SANS 500 tracking-[0.06em], not mono font-bold tracking-widest.
- **Components:** Replace ad-hoc number spans with canonical classes: LiveStats and pricing $ figures -> .price-display-lg/.price-display-md (or price-sm); ticker % -> .change-indicator--up/--down; ticker symbols -> .ticker-font; section eyebrows -> a single MicroLabel pattern (uppercase SANS 500, tracking 0.06em, text-muted-foreground). Standardize CTAs on the canonical Button (primary = brand-cyan fill). Keep ShimmerButton/BorderBeam only because this is the landing surface, but re-skin them to cyan tokens.
- **Motion/3D:** Bring framer-motion durations into 150-300ms and pass ease=[.4,0,.2,1] (--ease-out-smooth) instead of string easings; cut SectionReveal to ~0.25s and hero image to ~0.3s. Wrap reveals and the animate-marquee ticker in a prefers-reduced-motion guard (disable transform/marquee when set). Default card/CTA hover should be border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale(0.98) per law. Drop animate-pulse on the static eyebrow dot. 3D opportunity: the hero ProductShot currently shows a static gex-terminal.png — replace with (or link to) the canonical lazy-loaded React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface (cyan-lit peaks / purple valleys, clamped OrbitControls, 2D image fallback) as the ONE signature 3D moment, which doubles as the most honest product preview.

#### public-watchlist  · M effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** Page background is hardcoded `bg-[#0a0d12]` (a raw hex, and not even the L0 token #0a0a0a). Every other page in the app uses `bg-background text-foreground` (confirmed in analysis.tsx, blog.tsx, academy.tsx, etc.). This page bypasses the token system entirely. _(high)_; All text colors are raw Tailwind palette: `text-slate-100/400/500`, `text-emerald-400`, `text-red-400`, `text-cyan-300/400`. Law mandates `hsl(var(--token))`/named tokens only. Should be `text-foreground`, `text-muted-foreground`, and trade tokens. _(high)_; Brand wordmark 'QuantEdge' uses `text-emerald-400` — emerald is a SEMANTIC money/direction color (`--trade-bullish`). Using it for chrome/brand is explicitly banned. Brand/accent must be `--brand-cyan`. _(high)_; Momentum direction colors use raw `text-emerald-400`/`text-red-400` instead of `--trade-bullish`/`--trade-bearish` tokens. The intent (money/direction) is correct but the implementation hardcodes the palette. _(high)_; Typography violates the two-family rule. Numbers correctly use `font-mono` (resolves to JetBrains Mono — note: index.css ships JetBrains Mono/DM Sans, not the law's IBM Plex; that is a project-wide token gap, not page-specific), BUT the ticker symbol (`<span className="font-semibold">{it.symbol}</span>`) and tier letter render in default SANS. Tickers must be uppercase mono 600 +0.04em; strike/grade letters are data and should be mono. _(high)_; Upside-to-target value uses `text-cyan-400` — but upside is a money/direction number, so it is mis-encoded with the brand accent. Per the law cyan is brand/focus only; directional money should use a trade token (bullish/neutral), not cyan. _(med)_; Fixed type scale ignored. Uses arbitrary `text-[10px]`, `text-[9px]`, `text-[11px]`, `text-lg`, `text-sm`, `text-xs` instead of the canonical scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15) or the named helper classes (`.data-cell`, `.price-display-sm`, `.ticker-font`) that exist in index.css. _(med)_; Borders/dividers hardcoded as `border-white/10`, `border-white/15`, `divide-white/[0.06]` instead of `--border`/`--border-strong`. Depth must come from the border tokens + elevation ladder. _(med)_; No elevation ladder. The whole list sits flat on L0 with hairline dividers; no L1 `--card` surface, no row hover (28px row + border-brighten hover is the density/motion default). List rows are `py-3` (~24px content, loose) and not aligned to the compact 28px row spec. _(med)_; Tier chip color map (S=purple, A/B=emerald, C/D=amber/orange, F=red) uses raw palette classes. This matches the codebase-wide tier convention (symbol-journey-modal.tsx, elite-setups-grid.tsx, performance-attribution.tsx all do the same), so it is internally consistent but still technically non-token. A=emerald + B=emerald also collides emerald (a money color) onto a chrome/grade chip. _(low)_; Zero motion. No framer-motion entrance, no row hover micro-interaction, no NEW-badge emphasis. Law's default hover (border-brighten + translateY(-1px)) is absent. (No emoji or springs present — that part is clean.) _(low)_; No `data-density` awareness and no `tabular-nums`/slashed-zero guarantee beyond whatever `font-mono` inherits; rank/grade/momentum/upside should explicitly carry tabular-nums lining-nums. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the centered single-column read-only layout (max-w-3xl) — it is appropriate for a shareable public page; do not over-build. Swap the flat divided <ul> for a single L1 card container (`bg-card border border-[hsl(var(--border))] rounded-lg`) wrapping compact 28px rows separated by `divide-y divide-[hsl(var(--border))]`. Header sits above the card; footer disclaimer below. Tighten row vertical rhythm from py-3 to the compact row spec (h-7/28px, px 8-10).
- **Apply:** Replace `bg-[#0a0d12] text-slate-100` -> `bg-background text-foreground`. All `text-slate-400/500` -> `text-muted-foreground`. Brand wordmark `text-emerald-400` -> `text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]` (brand). Momentum: `mom>=0 ? text-emerald-400 : text-red-400` -> `text-[var(--trade-bullish)]` / `text-[var(--trade-bearish)]`. Upside `text-cyan-400` -> `text-[var(--trade-bullish)]` when positive / `--trade-bearish` when negative (it is money, not brand). Loading/empty `text-slate-400` -> `text-muted-foreground`; error `text-red-400` -> `text-[var(--trade-bearish)]` or `text-destructive`. All `border-white/1x` and `divide-white/[…]` -> `border-[hsl(var(--border))]` / `divide-[hsl(var(--border))]`. Symbol span -> add `.ticker-font` (mono 600 uppercase). Arbitrary `text-[9/10/11px]` -> the fixed scale (micro/data-xs) classes. Tier map: keep S=purple but route A/B off emerald onto neutral/cyan-grade tones OR centralize via shared TierBadge so the emerald-on-grade collision is at least consistent and tokenized.
- **Components:** Reuse existing canonical pieces instead of bespoke spans: (1) for the ticker, apply the `.ticker-font` class (client/src/index.css:849) or a TickerSymbol pattern — uppercase mono 600 +0.04em; (2) for numeric cells (rank, grade·score, momentum %, upside) use `.data-cell`/`.data-strip-value` helpers which already set tabular-nums; (3) the NEW badge should use the existing `client/src/components/ui/freshness-badge.tsx` rather than a hand-rolled cyan span; (4) consider the existing tier badge styling from `elite-setups-grid.tsx`/`performance-attribution.tsx` extracted into a shared TierBadge so the public page and owner Overview stay in lockstep.
- **Motion/3D:** Add a lightweight framer-motion staggered fade/translateY entrance on list rows (150-300ms, `--ease-out-smooth`), guarded by prefers-reduced-motion. Row hover = border-brighten to `--border-strong` + `translateY(-1px)` per the default micro-interaction. No 3D opportunity here (the GEX gamma surface is the only sanctioned 3D moment); keep this surface motion-minimal. NEW badge can get a one-time subtle cyan pulse on mount only.

#### unified-watchlist  · L effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** Semantic money/direction green (--trade-bullish #10b981) is used pervasively as BRAND/CHROME, not money: header Star icon (L832), count Badge (L835), every ticker link (L502, L1173), 'CONNECTED' wifi state (L217,221), the watchlist Select trigger (L973), the 'Overview' and 'Moving Averages' tab active states (L994,1002), the 'View' eye action button (L1276), the notifications Bell (L1376), and the 'All' notification filter (L1395). The law restricts --trade-bullish to encoding money/direction ONLY. This is the single biggest violation and it recurs ~12 times. _(high)_; Teal (--brand-teal #14b8a6) is used as terminal chrome across the page — demoted by law to logo stripe + GEX surface ONLY. Appears on: 'This Week' tab active state (L986), loading spinner (L255), empty-state CTA button (L267), ticker link color in ThisWeek table (L303), AUTO source badge (L342), bulk-import panel border + uppercase label + textarea focus ring + import button (L929-957), and 'Bulk Import' button (L918). All of these should be neutral chrome or the cyan accent. _(high)_; Raw Tailwind palette classes bypassing tokens (law: never hardcode, consume hsl(var(--token))). Counted across the file: bg-emerald-500/20 & emerald-500/30 & emerald-500/10 (L376,377,835,973,994,1002,1250,1395 — paired with --trade-bullish text), border-cyan-500/40 / text-cyan-400 / bg-cyan-500 / hover:bg-cyan-600 (L843,903,990,1230,1357), bg-purple-500/20 & text-purple-400 (L375,998), bg-amber-500/20 (L378), bg-orange-500/20 & text-orange-400 (L379), bg-red-500/20 & red-500/30 & hover:text-red-300 (L355,380,1252,1292). The TIER_CONFIG map (L374-381) is the worst offender — it hardcodes 6 palette ramps. _(high)_; The primary 'Add Symbol' CTA and the modal 'Save' CTA use raw bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 (L903, L1357) instead of the brand-cyan token / canonical primary Button variant. Right intent (cyan = primary CTA) but wrong, hardcoded mechanism, and the hover ramp (cyan-600) is off-token. _(med)_; Unicode glyphs used as data icons/decoration instead of lucide: '▲' and '▼' in the Today insight tile (L1059), and '·' as a separator in tier display (L1052) and grade display (L509). Law: lucide only, numbers/labels through the type system — not glyph art. Arrow direction already has TrendingUp/TrendingDown imported, so the triangles are redundant. _(med)_; Tier badges are rendered three different ways with three different shapes/sizes: inline span px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] (L326), 8x8 rounded square (L496), and px-2 py-1 rounded (L1263) — plus a duplicated TIER_CONFIG fallback object literal repeated 3x. No canonical TierBadge component is used; grade tier coloring also leaks purple/orange/amber palette outside the semantic system. _(med)_; Non-token / inconsistent surface elevation. Cards use bg-card/60 (arbitrary 60% alpha, L252,471,484,1108,1373) and insight tiles use bg-muted/30 (L1432); inputs/textarea use bg-muted/50 (L235,1022,1330,1339,1348). The law specifies a strict 4-step solid elevation ladder (L0 background -> L1 card -> L2 secondary -> L3 surface-overlay-solid) with depth via border, not alpha washes. bulk-import panel uses --surface-raised (L929) which is a different token than the card uses, so elevation is inconsistent. _(med)_; Typography scale not honored. Hero/data numbers use ad-hoc Tailwind sizes (text-lg L1437, text-sm, text-xs, and raw text-[9px]/text-[10px]/text-[8px] L342,346,524,930) rather than the fixed micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 / price-* scale. Micro-labels mix text-[9px] and text-[10px] tracking-widest inconsistently. Several numbers ARE mono+tabular (good) but the Type badge value and some labels are sans where mono is expected for tickers. _(med)_; Some numbers/tickers render without the mono treatment or only partially: the count Badge (L835-837) has font-mono but the symbol links rely on font-semibold sans (tickers must be uppercase mono 600 +0.04em per law). Price in main table (L1183) uses toLocaleString in a font-mono span (ok) but lacks tabular-nums/slashed-zero utility class guarantee. _(low)_; Density not driven by data-density. Spacing is hand-tuned (space-y-3, p-12 empty states, py-2.5 rows) rather than flowing from the single data-density attribute; row heights and card padding don't match the compact defaults (row 28px, card pad 8-10px) — the p-12 empty states are far larger than the dense baseline. _(low)_; Native confirm() dialog for delete (L1294) and native window alert path for share fallback — breaks the in-terminal motion/surface language; should use the canonical Dialog/AlertDialog already imported. _(low)_; Motion is header-only (initial/animate fade-in on wrappers). Table rows, insight tiles, and interactive cards have plain CSS transition-colors with no border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover or scale(0.98) press per the 3D/motion signature; no prefers-reduced-motion guard is visible at this layer. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing IA (sticky header with actions, watchlist selector + tab bar + add-symbol search, insights tile row, then the per-tab body, edit modal, notifications card) — the structure is sound and dense. Tighten to the compact baseline: drive all spacing from data-density (rows ~28px, card pad 8-10px), shrink the p-12 empty states to the compact empty-state block, and align the insight tile row, This Week table, Today buckets and Overview table to one shared row rhythm. Collapse the three divergent tier-badge shapes into one fixed-size badge used everywhere.
- **Apply:** Replace all raw palette utilities with tokens: bg-emerald-500/20 + text emerald -> trade-bullish ONLY where it encodes money/direction; everywhere it is chrome (Star, count badge, ticker links, wifi CONNECTED, tab active states, eye button, bell, 'All' filter, Select trigger) switch to neutral chrome (text-foreground/muted-foreground + --border) or the cyan accent for active/selected states. Replace every bg-cyan-500/border-cyan-500/text-cyan-400/cyan-600 with hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) via the Button primary variant and accent classes. Remove --brand-teal entirely from this page (This Week tab, spinners, AUTO badge, bulk-import panel chrome, Bulk Import button) -> use --brand-cyan for active/selection/focus and neutral border for the rest; teal is logo/GEX-only. Recolor TIER_CONFIG to tokens (no purple/orange/amber/red-300 literals). Swap '▲'/'▼' for lucide TrendingUp/TrendingDown and '·' separators for spacing or a lucide dot/Slash; keep them mono. Normalize surfaces to the 4-step ladder: cards = L1 --card solid + --border; insight tiles + inputs = L2 --secondary; bulk-import + modal = L3 --surface-overlay-solid; drop the /60 and /50 alpha washes, express depth via --border / --border-strong. Apply the fixed type scale (micro/data-xs/data-sm/data-base/data-lg) and ensure every number/ticker is IBM Plex Mono with tabular-nums + slashed zero; tickers uppercase mono 600 +0.04em. Replace native confirm()/alert with the imported Dialog/AlertDialog.
- **Components:** Introduce/consume canonical components: (1) TierBadge — one component fed by a single TIER_CONFIG that maps S/A/B/C/D/F to TOKENS, not raw palette (use --trade-bullish for A/B-grade up-tiers only where it genuinely means 'good money', --trade-neutral for mid, --trade-bearish for D/F; demote the decorative purple 'S' to neutral-on-elevated or a single accent treatment — do NOT introduce purple/orange). (2) Canonical primary Button (cyan) for Add Symbol and Save instead of bg-cyan-500. (3) DirectionCell / PctChange that pairs lucide ArrowUp/Down with mono tabular value in --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish (reuse for YTD, %Chg, momentum). (4) Reuse the shared Card elevation classes (accent-card / surface tokens) instead of bg-card/60.
- **Motion/3D:** Add the standard micro-interaction layer: framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth on interactive rows/tiles/cards — hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98); wrap in prefers-reduced-motion guard. Keep header fade-ins. No ambient glow. No 3D moment here — the R3F gamma surface stays GEX-only; this page should stay flat, dense, and fast.

#### trade-journal  · L effort · 12 issues

Law: lucide only, no emoji. Must map insight categories to lucide icons. _(high)_; Off-palette chrome colors with no token equivalent: blue-400 (Avg Hold stat 1974, 'disciplined' emotion, Stock toggle 958, open-status badges 549/1289), orange-400 (FOMO emotion 259/1614), purple (above). blue/orange/purple are not in the neutral-grayscale + cyan + 3-semantic system at all. Law: IBM Plex Mono for EVERY number with tabular-nums + slashed zero. _(high)_; Wrong typography families at the token level. Law mandates IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono; index.css currently defines --font-sans as 'DM Sans' and --font-mono as 'JetBrains Mono'. Page also never references font tokens, relying on inherited defaults + inline 'font-mono'. _(med)_; Non-compact density. Card padding p-4/p-5/p-6/p-8 (16-32px) vs law's 8-10px compact default; gaps gap-3/gap-6; no data-density attribute. Stat heroes text-2xl. Radii rounded-xl/2xl (12-16px) everywhere vs dense terminal aesthetic (rounded-md/lg). Section gap space-y-6 is loose. _(med)_; Raw rgba/hex in SVG charts: fill 'rgba(16,185,129,0.08)'/'rgba(239,68,68,0.08)' and stroke '#10b981'/'#ef4444' in PnLCurveChart (320-325) and WeeklyPnLChart (1513-1515). Should be var(--trade-bullish)/(--trade-bearish). (zero-line stroke='var(--border)' is already correct.) _(med)_; Decorative white-alpha surfaces (bg-white/5, /8, /10, border-white/10, hover:bg-white/5) used for elevation and hover across ~40 spots instead of the elevation ladder (--secondary / --surface-overlay-solid) and --border/--border-strong. Law: depth via bg-step + border, not alpha-white overlays. _(med)_; Micro-labels inconsistent: uppercase labels use tracking-wider and ad-hoc sizes (text-[8px]/[9px]/[10px]/[11px], text-xs) + font-medium rather than the fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11) with tracking 0.05-0.08em and 500 weight via a canonical class. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions; hover is plain transition-colors / hover:shadow-md (InsightCard 346). Law default hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98), 150-300ms ease-out-smooth. hover:shadow-md (glow) is also wrong in dark — depth via border, not glow. _(low)_; Native confirm()/alert() used for the destructive Delete-All flow (lines 2166, 2177, 2180) — unstyled OS dialogs break the design system. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep IA (sticky header: title + 6-tab pill switcher; 6-up StatCard strip; per-tab grids). Tighten to compact density: page py-6->py-4, section space-y-6->space-y-3, card pad p-5/p-6->p-2.5 (10px), grid gap-6->var(--card-gap) 10px. Reduce radii rounded-xl/2xl->rounded-lg (cards)/rounded-md (badges, inputs, tab pills). StatCard hero text-2xl->price-md mono token. Active tab pill uses --brand-cyan tint instead of bg-white/10.
- **Apply:** Also fix index.css tokens: --font-sans->IBM Plex Sans, --font-mono->IBM Plex Mono. High-volume mechanical swap in one 2211-line file, done in passes: (1) fix the 4 const color maps + EMOTION_COLORS; (2) global-replace palette classes (emerald/red/amber/zinc/blue/orange/purple) with token utilities; (3) replace bg-white/* elevation with secondary/overlay tokens; (4) add font-mono to all numeric spans; (5) emoji->lucide in InsightCard; (6) tighten padding/radius/gaps for compact density; (7) add framer-motion + replace confirm/alert. Verify dark first, then light.
- **Components:** Replace the 4 hardcoded style maps with token helpers: SEVERITY positive->trade-bullish, warning->trade-neutral, critical->trade-bearish, neutral->muted/border; GRADE hot->bullish, warm->neutral, cold->bearish, even->muted; EMOTION collapse blue/orange/purple onto the 3 semantics by sentiment (confident/disciplined->bullish, fearful/fomo->neutral, greedy/revenge->bearish, neutral->muted). Promote shared primitives: canonical <StatCard>, <DataTable> (TickerTable/RecentTradesTable/PersonalTradesTable duplicate markup), <Badge variant>, <Card>, and <Button variant=primary|ghost|danger> so purple/white-alpha buttons funnel through cyan/secondary/bearish. - **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion per law: StatCard/InsightCard/Card hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) at 150ms ease-out-smooth; button press = scale(0.98); replace InsightCard hover:shadow-md glow with border brighten. Tab content crossfade (opacity/y, 200ms); StatCard value count-up; PnL/Weekly SVG path draw-on-mount. Guard all with prefers-reduced-motion. No 3D here (the single R3F gamma surface belongs to GEX); keep charts as token-colored inline SVG.

#### unified-watchlist  · L effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** Semantic money/direction color used as chrome/brand identity. The single chromatic accent for brand/active/focus is --brand-cyan. _(high)_; Hardcoded Tailwind palette colors instead of tokens, pervasive. emerald-500 (L376,377,835,973,994,1002,1250,1395), cyan-500/cyan-400/cyan-600 (L843,903,990,1230,1357,1903), purple-500/purple-400 (L375,998), amber-500 (L378), orange-500/orange-400 (L379), red-500/red-300 (L355,380,1252,1292). The law forbids zinc/slate/gray/emerald/rose/amber/etc. raw classes; all color must come through hsl(var(--token)) or token-named utilities. _(high)_; Primary CTA 'Add Symbol' and modal Save use raw bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-600 (L903, L1357) and the Share button uses border-cyan-500/40 text-cyan-400 (L843) instead of the --brand-cyan token. Correct color, wrong source — must consume var(--brand-cyan). _(high)_; TIER_CONFIG (L374-381) is built entirely from raw palette classes (bg-purple-500/20, bg-emerald-500/20, bg-amber-500/20, bg-orange-500/20, bg-red-500/20 with matching text-*-400). Tiers A/B reuse --trade-bullish green and C uses --trade-neutral amber, conflating grade tiers with money/direction semantics. Tier badges are chrome and should not borrow the trade-* semantic colors at all. _(high)_; Tab accent colors are inconsistent and off-system: This Week = brand-teal, Today = cyan-500, Overview = emerald (green), Technical = purple, Moving Averages = emerald (green) (L986-1005). Active-tab state should be a single accent (--brand-cyan), not five different hues, two of them green chrome. _(high)_; Non-mono numbers. The Overview price cell renders price.toLocaleString without font-mono inheritance issues only because the span has font-mono, but the count Badge at L835 ({...size}) and InsightTile values are mono — OK — however the tier score '/100' and several inline counts are fine. The real miss: arrow glyphs '▲ ▼' used as data in InsightTile Today value (L1059) are unicode-as-icon inside a number field; law says lucide icons only, no glyph icons. Also the en-dash '–' placeholders (L296,310,332,337) are fine but the '·' separators are acceptable. _(med)_; Emoji/glyph-as-icon: '▲' and '▼' triangles used in the Today insight tile value (L1059) instead of lucide TrendingUp/TrendingDown. Law: icons = lucide only, no emoji/glyph icons. _(med)_; Hover/active red hardcoded: Trash buttons use text-[var(--trade-bearish)] hover:text-red-300 (L355,1292) — hover falls back to a raw palette color, and destructive hover is using money-red as chrome for a delete affordance. _(med)_; Inconsistent surface/elevation. Cards use bg-card/60 (L252,484,1108,1373) and InsightTile uses bg-muted/30 (L1432); the bulk-import panel uses var(--surface-raised) (L929) while the law's elevation ladder is --background/--card/--secondary/--surface-overlay-solid. Mixing /60 alpha cards with muted tiles breaks the strict 4-step ladder; depth should come from bg-step + border, not arbitrary alpha. _(med)_; Density not driven by data-density. Spacing is hand-tuned (space-y-3, px-4 py-2.5, h-8) rather than inheriting the compact default via the data-density attribute; rows are ~larger than the 28px compact target and pad values are ad hoc. _(low)_; Outlook badge builds its own emerald/red/muted variants inline (L1247-1254) duplicating semantic logic that should be a canonical trade-direction badge. _(low)_; Motion is limited to entrance fades with staggered delays (L824,964,1030,1082,1093,1103,1368). No hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px) on rows/cards, no press scale(0.98); the signature micro-interactions from the law are absent. No prefers-reduced-motion guard on the entrance animations. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the structure (header + selector/tabs/search row + insights strip + tab content table + notifications card) — it is sound and dense-friendly. Tighten to compact density: drive all spacing from data-density (compact default: card pad 8-10px, 28px rows, 12px base). Collapse the insights strip to the existing 6-tile grid but standardize tile surface. Unify the three table tabs (Overview/Technical/Moving Averages) to share one column-header component with sortable affordances.
- **Apply:** (1) Brand/chrome -> --brand-cyan: recolor Watchlist H1 Star, the count Badge, the Default selector trigger, the Eye view action, the Bell icon, the active 'All'/notification tab, and every active TabsTrigger to a single cyan accent via hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) (e.g. text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))], data-[state=active]:text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] data-[state=active]:bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.12)]). (3) Rewrite TIER_CONFIG to neutral grayscale chrome (use --secondary / --surface-overlay-solid backgrounds + --border, text-foreground), NOT trade-* colors — tiers are not money/direction. (4) Keep --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish ONLY on: YTD % change, 5d momentum, top gainer/loser, Outlook Outperform/Underperform — these are real direction. Fix Trash hover from hover:text-red-300 to a token (hover:text-[hsl(var(--trade-bearish))] is the static color; for a delete chrome affordance prefer neutral->bearish on hover via border-brighten). (5) Replace all raw palette classes (emerald/cyan/purple/amber/orange/red 300-600) with hsl(var(--token)). bulk-import focus ring -> --brand-cyan (already partly), keep --surface-raised but align it to the elevation ladder token --surface-overlay-solid. Micro-labels in InsightTile already UPPERCASE mono — switch label font to sans 500 per law (micro-labels are sans, numbers are mono). (7) Surfaces: standardize cards to the L1 --card step with --border, InsightTiles to L2 --secondary; drop arbitrary /60 and /30 alphas.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical ui/card, ui/badge, ui/button, ui/table, ui/tabs, ui/select, ui/dialog, ui/input (already imported). Replace ad-hoc tier pills and outlook badges with a single token-driven Badge variant. Keep lucide icons (already used) and remove the ▲/▼ glyphs in favor of lucide TrendingUp/TrendingDown.
- **Motion/3D:** Add the signature micro-interaction to table rows and InsightTiles: framer-motion hover = border-brighten (border-[hsl(var(--border-strong))]) + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98), 150-300ms ease-out-smooth cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1). Wrap the staggered entrance fades in a prefers-reduced-motion guard. No ambient glow. This page has no 3D moment and should not add one (GEX surface is the only sanctioned 3D).

#### backtest  · M effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** Raw Tailwind palette colors used for chrome/decoration instead of tokens: header icon chip uses bg-purple-500/10 + text-purple-400 (line 324-325), RSI card icon text-purple-400 (line 460), Analyze button bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 (line 352). Trade colors must encode ONLY direction/money. _(high)_; Badge borders hardcode raw palette while text uses tokens, creating inconsistency: border-green-500/30, border-red-500/30, border-amber-500/30 paired with text-[var(--trade-bullish/bearish/neutral)] and bg-red-500/10, bg-amber-500/10 (lines 59-71, 525-595). Up-candle green #22c55e diverges from --trade-bullish #10b981; chart should consume CSS vars via getComputedStyle. _(high)_; Off-scale typography: title text-base, body text-[10px], stat values text-xl font-bold, card titles text-lg, labels text-xs. Law mandates fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15) and price-sm/md/lg for hero numbers. text-xl/text-[10px] are off-system; hero stat numbers should use price-md/price-lg, not text-xl. _(med)_; Numbers use ad-hoc 'font-mono tabular-nums' rather than the canonical .data-cell / .price-display utilities (which add slashed-zero, lining-nums, tnum feature-settings). Ticker (line 396) is mono but not the .ticker-font treatment (uppercase 600 +0.04em). _(med)_; glass-card class is used on every panel (12 occurrences) instead of the canonical Card default elevation. In dark, .glass-card resolves to flat #0a0a0a with no elevation ladder; mixing it onto the <Card> (which already provides bg-card L1 + border) is redundant/conflicting and bypasses the 4-step elevation system. _(med)_; No data-density attribute and non-compact spacing: container p-3/p-4, CardContent p-4, grids gap-4/gap-6, empty-state p-12. Default should be compact (card pad 8-10px) driven by data-density. _(med)_; Default sans font token is 'DM Sans' and mono is 'JetBrains Mono' (index.css lines 272-274, 431-433), not the law's IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono. This is a token-level mismatch affecting the whole app but visible here. _(med)_; Empty-state copy says charts are 'powered by TradingView' (line 664) but charts are lightweight-charts; minor but a chrome accuracy issue. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions; cards rely on global .shadcn-card hover which also adds a teal-tinted box-shadow glow in dark (index.css line 94) — borderline vs 'NO ambient/decorative glow'. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the vertical stack: header + search Card, error banner, 4-up KPI grid (Symbol/Price/Change/Signal), price chart, RSI subchart, two-up Patterns/Signal Breakdown, indicators table, summary table, empty state. Tighten to compact density. Replace the 4 KPI Cards with metric-block-style stat cells (label = micro uppercase 500 tracked, value = price-md mono). Make the indicators + summary tables use .data-table-compact density. Wrap the page root in data-density='compact' and drop glass-card from all panels, letting canonical <Card> supply L1 elevation.
- **Apply:** 1) Header icon chip: replace bg-purple-500/10 + text-purple-400 with neutral bg-secondary + text-muted-foreground, or brand: bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.1)] text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]. 2) Analyze button: drop bg-cyan-500/hover:bg-cyan-400; use canonical <Button> default (primary) or variant with brand-cyan token. 3) Target icon (line 448): make text-muted-foreground (chrome, not direction). RSI icon: text-muted-foreground. 4) All badges: convert to bg-[var(--trade-bullish)]/10 text-[var(--trade-bullish)] border-[var(--trade-bullish)]/30 (and bearish/neutral equivalents) so border matches token; better, extract a single <DirectionBadge direction> and reuse for SignalBadge/PatternBadge/table-status. 5) lightweight-charts: read tokens via getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--trade-bullish'|'--trade-bearish'|'--border'|'--muted-foreground') and feed them into layout/grid/series so up=#10b981, down=#ef4444, grid=hsl(var(--border)), text=hsl(var(--muted-foreground)); BB lines = hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) dashed + muted mid; RSI line = brand-cyan (not raw purple); overbought/oversold lines = trade-bearish/trade-bullish. Re-run on theme change. 6) Typography: KPI value text-xl font-bold -> price-md (mono); card titles text-lg -> data-lg font-600; labels text-xs uppercase -> micro/text-data-xs uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground; body text-[10px] -> text-data-xs. 7) Numbers: apply .data-cell to all numeric cells, .price-display/price-display-md to KPI numbers, .ticker-font to the symbol. 8) Remove glass-card everywhere; rely on <Card>. 9) Root div: add data-density='compact', reduce p-3 spacing via density.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical <Card>/<CardHeader>/<CardTitle>/<CardContent>, <Badge> (token-driven), <Button> (default primary = brand-cyan), <Input>, <Table> family. Introduce a shared DirectionBadge component to dedupe SignalBadge + PatternBadge + the 8 inline status badges in the indicators table. Consider .metric-block for the KPI row and .data-table-compact for the two tables.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth: KPI cards stagger-fade on data load (animate-page-in already exists), Analyze button press scale(0.98) (global rule covers it), card hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) WITHOUT the teal glow shadow. Respect prefers-reduced-motion. No 3D here — the R3F GEX surface is the only 3D moment and does not belong on this page. Optional: price-tick flash util on the Change KPI when refetched.

#### design-system-test  · L effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** This is the living style-guide page, yet it documents a STALE palette that contradicts the current design law. It presents 'Electric Cyan #00d4ff' as the primary accent, but the law's single accent is brand cyan --brand-cyan #22d3ee (index.css line 236). Every cyan reference on the page (lines 61, 255, 257, 338, 418, 429, 432) hardcodes the wrong hex. As the canonical reference others copy from, this is the most damaging violation: it actively teaches the wrong color. _(high)_; Color-palette section (lines 435-468) legitimizes banned/demoted colors as first-class: 'AI Violet #8b5cf6' (law has NO violet — exactly one chromatic accent allowed), 'Live Lime #b6ff33' (not a token at all), and presents Gold #f59e0b without noting it is the semantic --trade-neutral reserved ONLY for money/direction, not generic 'Premium/Warning' chrome. The swatch grid itself hardcodes all six hex values instead of rendering hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Typography section (lines 408-419) documents the WRONG font stack: 'Display — Space Grotesk', 'Body — Inter', 'Mono — JetBrains Mono'. Law mandates exactly two families: IBM Plex Sans (--font-sans) + IBM Plex Mono (--font-mono). (Note: even the live index.css uses DM Sans/JetBrains Mono, so the page is stale vs BOTH the law and the running CSS.) font-display is used 5x on the page but has no token definition in index.css — it is a dead class pointing at a removed font. _(high)_; Ambient/decorative glow is used pervasively, which the law explicitly bans in-terminal ('NO ambient/decorative glow'). QEPageShell is invoked with orbs={true} (line 56) rendering corner glow orbs, and QEPanel is given glow="cyan" (line 249) and glow="ai" (line 287). Depth must come from bg-step + border, not glow. _(high)_; Violet is used as a real UI accent, not just shown as a swatch: Brain icon text-violet-400 (line 292), and the 'Full Brief' CTA uses violet-500/10 bg, violet-500/20 border, violet-400 text (line 320). This is a second chromatic accent, forbidden by the one-accent rule. _(high)_; Hardcoded hex literals throughout the page (~18 occurrences) instead of token consumption: #00d4ff x7, #8b5cf6 x2, #10b981 x2, #ef4444 x2, #f59e0b x2, #b6ff33 x2, #00bfe8. The 'Log First Trade' primary CTA (line 338) hardcodes bg-[#00d4ff] / hover bg-[#00bfe8] / text-black — primary CTA should use the brand-cyan token. _(high)_; Root-cause note: several violations are inherited from the canonical components this page renders, so fixing them upstream cleans many surfaces at once. qe-panel.tsx hardcodes #00d4ff (grade S, line 321), emerald-500/amber-500 and from-emerald-500 to-cyan-500 / from-blue-500 to-violet-500 confidence gradients (lines 322-356), and font-display (line 178). qe-page-shell.tsx hardcodes #00d4ff, violet-500, amber-500, emerald-500 glow variants (lines 156-159) and the orbs/grid decoration. _(high)_; Numbers are rendered with the Tailwind type scale (text-3xl font-bold) instead of the law's fixed data/price scale with tabular-nums + slashed-zero. Hero stat $5,847.23 (line 123) and the display sample (line 410) use text-3xl; data labels use text-xs/text-[10px] ad hoc rather than the micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 tokens. No price-sm/md/lg hero classes are used. _(med)_; Direct DOM color text-white is used for headings/values repeatedly (lines 123, 139, 143, 147, 298, 309, 360, 370, 380, 390, 431, 438, etc.) instead of --foreground / --card-foreground tokens; breaks the derived light-mode inverse. _(med)_; Interactive Panel uses native alert() on click (line 106) — not a real UI pattern, fine for a test stub but it sidesteps the canonical motion signature (hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale(0.98)); the showcase never demonstrates the mandated framer-motion micro-interactions or the prefers-reduced-motion contract. _(low)_; Density is not demonstrated or honored: the law's data-density attribute (compact/comfortable/spacious) is the core density mechanism, but this style-guide page neither shows the three densities nor wires a toggle, despite being the natural place to document them. Spacing (gap-4, gap-6, space-y-3, mb-12) is hand-tuned rather than density-driven. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong sectioned showcase skeleton (QEPageShell > QEPageHeader > numbered QESectionDivider blocks > QEGridLayout). Re-sequence and expand into the canonical reference it should be: (00) Foundation/tokens, (01) Elevation ladder L0-L3, (02) Color = the ONE cyan accent + the three semantic trade colors only, (03) Typography = IBM Plex Sans + Plex Mono with the fixed 9/11/12/13/15 + price scale, (04) Density toggle demo (compact/comfortable/spacious via data-density), (05) Panels, (06) Stat/grade/confidence, (07) Composed panels, (08) Workspace, (09) Motion + reduced-motion, (10) the signature GEX 3D surface as the one allowed 3D moment. Drop orbs (orbs={false}) and use grid='subtle' at most; depth comes from the elevation ladder, not glow.
- **Apply:** Replace ALL hardcoded hex with tokens: #00d4ff/#00bfe8 -> hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) or a .text-brand/.bg-brand utility; #10b981 -> var(--trade-bullish); #ef4444 -> var(--trade-bearish); #f59e0b -> var(--trade-neutral) (and relabel it 'semantic neutral — money/direction only', not 'Premium/Warning'). DELETE the AI-Violet and Live-Lime swatches and every violet/lime usage (lines 292, 320, 436-439, 463-467); recolor the Intelligence-Brief icon/CTA to brand-cyan. Rewrite the Typography section to 'Sans — IBM Plex Sans (400/500/600/700)' and 'Mono — IBM Plex Mono — every number, tabular-nums + slashed zero'; remove the Space Grotesk/Inter/JetBrains labels and replace font-display with font-sans. Convert all numeric displays (price $5,847.23, %, pts, VOL/IV) to font-mono with font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums and the price-md/price-lg classes; swap ad-hoc text-3xl/text-xs/text-[10px] for the data-token classes (.text-data-xs/.text-data-sm/.text-micro etc.). Replace text-white with text-foreground/text-card-foreground. Set orbs={false}; remove glow='cyan'/glow='ai' from QEPanel. CRITICAL upstream fix (cleans this page + every consumer): in qe-panel.tsx swap #00d4ff->brand-cyan, emerald/amber/blue/violet gradient classes -> bg-[var(--trade-bullish)]/[--trade-neutral]/[--brand-cyan] solid fills, font-display->font-sans; in qe-page-shell.tsx replace the glowVariants map (#00d4ff/violet/amber/emerald) with border+bg-step depth and delete the orbs render. Add a data-density toggle demo and a framer-motion micro-interaction demo (border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover, scale(0.98) press, 150-300ms ease-out-smooth) that respects prefers-reduced-motion. Use lucide icons only (already true). Lazy-load the R3F GEX surface in the new 3D section as the single signature moment.
- **Components:** Reuse the QE* canonical set already imported (QEPanel/QEPanelGlass/QEPanelNested/QEPanelStat/QELayer/QEPanelHeader/Content/Footer, QEStatCard, QEGradeDisplay, QEConfidenceBar, QEStatusDot, QEPageShell/Header, QESectionDivider, QEGridLayout, QEEmptyState, QEWorkspace). Fix them at the source rather than forking. Add a small DensityToggle and a MotionDemo card, and import the lazy R3F GEXSurface for the 3D section. Replace the raw <button> CTAs (lines 257, 320, 338) with the canonical primary/ghost button so the brand-cyan CTA style is defined in one place.
- **Motion/3D:** Add a dedicated Motion section demonstrating the mandated default hover (border-brighten + translateY(-1px)), press (scale 0.98), 150-300ms on --ease-out-smooth, with a prefers-reduced-motion toggle showing the reduced variant. Add a 3D section that lazy-loads the React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface (cyan-lit peaks / purple valleys, OrbitControls clamped, 2D fallback) so the style guide actually showcases the product's one signature 3D moment. Remove all ambient orb/glow motion.

#### history  · M effort · 11 issues
*Today:* A two-tab (Chat / Research) list page inside a centered max-w-5xl column: a header with search input, a tab bar, and a single Card per tab holding divided list rows with a circular icon, title/ticker, meta line, a signal Badge, and View/Trash actions. Functionally complete and partially token-aware (it already uses --trade-bullish/bearish/neutral and --border in places), but it's riddled with raw Tailwind palette colors (emerald/cyan/slate/red/amber), uses chromatic accents as chrome, renders dates/counts/tickers in the sans body font instead of mono, and runs at a loose p-4/16px density rather than the Bloomberg-compact default.

- **Violations:** Accent-as-chrome: active tab uses data-[state=active]:bg-emerald-500/20 (lines 123, 131) and the chat-row icon container uses bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30 (line 160). Semantic emerald is reserved for money/direction ONLY and must never be used for tab/active chrome or decorative icon chips. _(high)_; Raw Tailwind cyan instead of the brand token: text-cyan-400 appears on tab active text (123,131), the chat icon (161), View buttons (174,241), and the ticker link (219). Brand accent must be hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) / the brand-cyan token, not a hardcoded Tailwind shade. _(high)_; Hardcoded neutral palette: divide-slate-800/50 on both list containers (lines 152, 208). Dividers/borders must come from --border, not slate-800. _(high)_; Mixed raw + token semantic colors in getSignalColor (lines 80,83,85): bg-emerald-500/20 + border-emerald-500/30, bg-red-500/20 + border-red-500/30, bg-amber-500/20 + border-amber-500/30 are hardcoded around the correct --trade-* text tokens. Backgrounds/borders should use --trade-bullish-bg / --trade-bearish-bg / --trade-neutral-bg (and a matching border alpha of the same token), not raw Tailwind. _(high)_; Numbers not in mono: message count + 'messages' (line 169), and formatDate output in the meta lines (167, 230) render in the default sans font. Per the law EVERY number — dates, counts, time — must be IBM Plex Mono with tabular-nums + slashed zero (font-mono tabular-nums). _(high)_; Ticker not styled per spec: research.symbol (line 219) is a brand-cyan link but rendered in sans, not uppercase mono 600 with +0.04em tracking (.ticker-font exists in index.css). Tickers must be uppercase mono 600. _(med)_; Semantic color used as decorative hover: the ticker link hovers to text-[var(--trade-bullish)] (line 219). Bullish green encodes direction/money, not a generic link-hover affordance — hover should brighten via brand-cyan or border, not flip to green. _(med)_; Density too loose: Card section pad and every row use p-4 (16px) with ~64px rows; empty states use p-12. The compact default calls for 8–10px card padding and ~28px rows, and the page ignores the data-density attribute entirely. _(med)_; Motion present but minimal/non-conformant: only two entrance fades on header/tabs; rows have plain transition-colors hover with no border-brighten + translateY(-1px) micro-interaction, no press scale(0.98), and no prefers-reduced-motion guard called out. Hover affordance is bg-muted/30 wash rather than the canonical border-brighten lift. _(low)_; Translucent surfaces over the elevation ladder: bg-card/60 (141,197) and bg-muted/50 (107,120) use ad-hoc alpha instead of the solid 4-step ladder (--card L1, --secondary L2). Depth should come from bg-step + border, not opacity washes. _(low)_; Heading uses text-foreground/95 and rows use text-foreground/90 (lines 100,164) — arbitrary opacity on text color instead of the token text hierarchy (--foreground / --muted-foreground). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the centered max-w-5xl column, header (title + search), tab bar, and one Card per tab — the IA is correct. Tighten to compact density: Card body sections from p-4 to p-2.5 (~10px); list rows from p-4 to a fixed ~28px row (py-1.5 px-3) with the icon chip shrunk from w-10 h-10 to w-7 h-7 (rounded-md, not rounded-lg). Convert each tab's list into a true compact list/table rhythm (consider the existing .data-table-compact rhythm) with the divider from divide-[hsl(var(--border))]. Empty states p-12 -> p-8 and the 12x12 icon -> 8x8.
- **Apply:** Color: replace ALL text-cyan-400 with the brand token — text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] (View buttons, ticker link, active tab text, chat icon). Replace data-[state=active]:bg-emerald-500/20 with a neutral active chrome: data-[state=active]:bg-[hsl(var(--secondary))] (L2) + data-[state=active]:text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] + a subtle border-strong, since the active accent for chrome is cyan, never emerald. Chat icon chip: drop emerald bg/border -> bg-[hsl(var(--secondary))] border-[hsl(var(--border))] with a brand-cyan glyph. Replace divide-slate-800/50 -> divide-[hsl(var(--border))]. Rewrite getSignalColor to: BUY/BULLISH/LONG -> bg-[var(--trade-bullish-bg)] text-[var(--trade-bullish)] border-[var(--trade-bullish)]/30; SELL/BEARISH/SHORT -> the --trade-bearish-* trio; HOLD/NEUTRAL -> the --trade-neutral-* trio; default -> muted via tokens. Surfaces: bg-card/60 -> bg-[hsl(var(--card))], bg-muted/50 -> bg-[hsl(var(--secondary))]. Text: text-foreground/95 and /90 -> plain text-foreground; keep meta in text-muted-foreground. Type: dates + '{n} messages' wrapped in font-mono tabular-nums; ticker via .ticker-font; section/meta micro-labels ('CHAT HISTORY' style descriptors) as uppercase sans 500 tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground if promoted to labels.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical primitives: Card, Tabs/TabsList/TabsTrigger, Badge, Button (variant=ghost size=sm/icon), Input, Skeleton, Link — all already imported. For the signal pill, route through the trade-* tokens (or an existing badge variant) instead of the inline getSignalColor string. For the ticker, apply the existing .ticker-font utility (uppercase mono 600, +0.04em) from index.css. Apply font-mono tabular-nums to dates and the message count (the analyze/backtest pages are the reference pattern, e.g. backtest.tsx 'font-mono tabular-nums').
- **Motion/3D:** Add the signature row micro-interaction: framer-motion (or CSS) hover = border-brighten to --border-strong + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98), 150–200ms on --ease-out-smooth, replacing the flat bg-muted/30 wash; guard with prefers-reduced-motion. Keep the existing staggered entrance fades (they're conformant). No 3D opportunity here — this is a non-GEX list surface, so no R3F and no ambient glow.

#### learning-dashboard  · L effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** Banned chromatic accent palette throughout: purple/indigo/violet is the page's primary theme color (header icon bg-purple-500/20, Brain text-purple-400, 'Tracking' badge, primary CTA bg-purple-600, insight recommendation text-purple-400, confidence ring stroke #8B5CF6, brain hub gradient from-purple-500 to-indigo-600). Law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent = brand-cyan; purple is never permitted as chrome. _(high)_; Raw hex literals pervade the composed components (live-metrics-grid color props #10B981/#3B82F6/#8B5CF6/#F59E0B/#EC4899/#6366F1; ai-brain-visualization engine colors; confidence ring #374151/#8B5CF6 inline on the page). Law forbids hardcoded hex in components — must consume hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Cards bypass the elevation ladder and tokens: bg-gray-900/50 border-gray-800, inner blocks bg-gray-800/50 border-gray-700/50. Should be the canonical <Card> (shadcn-card bg-card border-card-border) or token surfaces L0-L3. _(high)_; NO mono numerals anywhere. Every number is sans (text-2xl font-bold text-white for thresholds 55/70/1.5:1/10:00, win-rate %, P&L $12,847, confidence pts, sparkline values). Law requires IBM Plex Mono + tabular-nums + slashed-zero for EVERY number. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors misused as decoration: metric cards arbitrarily assign bullish-green (#10B981) to Win Rate, blue to P&L, purple to positions, pink to trades — color encodes nothing about money/direction. Green/red must encode ONLY money/direction. _(high)_; Ambient/decorative glow banned by law: live-metrics-grid renders blur-3xl glow orbs behind each card and colored boxShadow on hover; ai-brain hub has shadow-purple-500/30. Law: NO ambient/decorative glow; depth via bg-step + border only. _(high)_; Spring/scale micro-interactions banned in-terminal: whileHover scale:1.02 with colored boxShadow on metric cards; insight cards animate. Law: default hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98); springs/Aceternity are landing-only. _(med)_; Density violation: rounded-2xl + p-5 metric cards, rounded-xl + p-4 insight blocks, gap-6 grids, mb-8 header. Law default is compact (card pad 8-10px, radius-card 10px, tighter gaps). Radii are inconsistent (rounded-2xl/xl/full vs token radius-card). _(med)_; Hardcoded text-white / text-yellow-400 instead of tokens (text-foreground/text-card-foreground; Lightbulb uses text-yellow-400 as chrome). Header h1 is only text-base font-bold — not the uppercase micro-label / proper heading scale. _(med)_; Misleading semantics in copy + UI: confidence value relabeled 'pts signal strength' and CTA 'Run Learning Cycle' / 'Brain' iconography frame a deterministic point-summing engine as ML/neural — file header itself admits visuals are decorative. Not a token issue but conflicts with honest-data intent. _(low)_; page-atmosphere wrapper adds cyan grid + radial glow background; acceptable per token but combined with per-card glow orbs it doubles ambient decoration. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the macro grid (header row; xl:3-col split with engine map left / metrics+insights right; then radar, equity+signals 2-col, market pulse full-width, learned thresholds). Tighten to compact density: header mb-4 not mb-8, grids gap-3 (var(--card-gap)) not gap-6, card padding 8-10px. Convert every panel to the canonical <Card>/<CardHeader>/<CardContent> so surface/border/radius come from tokens. Rename header to honest 'PERFORMANCE' (uppercase sans-500 micro-label + IBM Plex Sans heading), drop Brain/neural framing toward an engine/Activity metaphor.
- **Apply:** COLOR: replace every purple/indigo/violet with brand-cyan for chrome/brand/active (icon chips bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.12)] text-[var(--brand-cyan)]; confidence ring stroke var(--brand-cyan), track hsl(var(--border)); CTA = default Button). Strip ALL raw hex from live-metrics-grid/ai-brain-visualization color props; the only colored values allowed are var(--trade-bullish/bearish/neutral) and ONLY where the metric encodes money/direction (Win Rate, P&L deltas -> bullish/bearish by sign; non-directional metrics like Active Positions/Today's Trades render neutral foreground, no chroma). TYPOGRAPHY: wrap every number in font-mono via .data-cell/.price-display so % / $ / pts / ratios / times get tabular-nums + slashed zero; threshold values 55/70/1.5:1/10:00 -> price-display-md. SURFACE: cards -> <Card>; inner blocks -> .inset-panel or .metric-block; remove text-white -> text-card-foreground, remove text-yellow-400 chrome -> text-muted-foreground icon. REMOVE: blur-3xl glow orbs, colored hover boxShadow, shadow-purple, scale:1.02. MOTION: standardize to framer 150-300ms ease-out-smooth; hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale(0.98); honor prefers-reduced-motion; keep the entrance opacity/y fades but drop spring scaling.
- **Components:** Use canonical components: client/src/components/ui/card.tsx (replaces all bg-gray-900/50 panels), ui/button.tsx (primary CTA -> default variant, drop bg-purple-600), ui/badge.tsx (drop purple/yellow variants). Reuse existing token-correct CSS utilities already defined in index.css: .metric-block / .metric-value / .metric-label for the LiveMetricsGrid cards (mono value, uppercase label, token surface) instead of the bespoke glow cards; .accent-card / .accent-card--cyan for left-accent emphasis; .data-cell, .price-display-md/lg, .change-indicator--up/down/flat for numbers and deltas; .section-chrome for section dividers; .bloomberg-panel for sub-panels.
- **Motion/3D:** This page is the natural home for the ONE sanctioned R3F moment ONLY if it shows real data — but the 'engine map' brain viz is decorative, so do NOT add a 3D brain. Keep visualizations 2D and token-colored: the confidence donut and radar are fine as flat SVG once recolored to cyan + token border. Reserve the lazy R3F gamma surface for the GEX surface, not here. The signature interaction here is just the compact border-brighten hover on metric cards.

#### settings  · M effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** Primary Save CTA hardcodes Tailwind palette color 'bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-foreground' in TWO places (line 213 top-bar Save, line 816 floating Save). Compounded by using raw emerald-500 instead of --trade-bullish-bg. _(high)_; Numbers rendered in sans, not mono. Slider value labels are plain sans: 'Max Risk Per Trade: {x}%' (378), '${botThresholds.maxPositionSize}' (712), '{confidenceThreshold}pts' (724), '{dailyTradeLimit} trades' (736), '{stopLossPercent}%' (748); also subscription tier value (341), positions-count text (636). Law: EVERY number uses font-mono + tabular-nums. _(high)_; Mixed semantic + raw red on Clear-logs button: 'text-[var(--trade-bearish)] hover:text-red-600' (664). Token for base, raw Tailwind red for hover — inconsistent and off-token. Destructive action also shouldn't use a half-token treatment. _(med)_; Inconsistent border-radius across the page: rounded-md (account-status rows, notif rows, log list), rounded-lg (bot cards 582), rounded-xl (floating save bar 811), rounded-full (Active badge 587), rounded (position chips 687). No single radius scale applied. _(med)_; Header is hand-rolled (h1 text-base font-semibold + p text-[10px], lines 204-205) instead of the canonical QEPageHeader/QEPageShell that other pages (olalgo, design-system-test) use.
