The page even exposes a 'Layout Density' control yet its own chrome ignores density tokens. Law: density driven by one data-density attribute; compact is default. _(med)_; 'Layout Density' select defaults to 'comfortable' (455) and notification toggles default checked — but law states compact is the platform default. Default contradicts the design law's density stance. _(low)_; Disclaimer card hardcodes 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5' (823) using raw amber palette instead of --trade-neutral / --trade-neutral-bg tokens. Also: neutral/amber is a money-direction semantic, debatable for a legal disclaimer chrome. _(low)_; Active 'Active' badge is static/hardcoded for ALL four bots (587) regardless of real isRunning state (BotStatus type exists but unused) — every bot always shows green Active. Minor truthfulness/data issue, not pure design. _(low)_; GlassInput (variant 'glass' = bg-muted/50 backdrop-blur-md, focus:border-cyan-500/60) is used for all form fields; backdrop-blur glass is a landing/Aceternity flavor and focus ring uses raw cyan-500/60 not --brand-cyan. In-terminal forms should use the solid tokenized input, not glass. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Wrap the page in the canonical QEPageShell and replace the hand-rolled header (lines 202-219) with QEPageHeader (icon=Settings/SlidersHorizontal, label='CONFIGURATION', title='Settings', description, actions=Reset+Save buttons). Remove the unused PageHeader import. Keep the 5-tab Tabs structure but let card padding/row height come from data-density rather than literal py/space-y/p-* values. Normalize ALL surfaces to one radius token (rounded-md for rows/inputs, rounded-lg only for top-level Cards) and drive inter-card gaps from --card-gap. Keep the floating save bar but restyle it as a tokenized solid overlay (--surface-overlay-solid + --border-strong), not bg-background/95 backdrop-blur with rounded-xl.
- **Apply:** 1) Save buttons: replace 'bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-foreground' with the default/primary <Button> variant (which already maps to --brand-cyan + correct foreground) on both line 213 and 816 — delete the className color overrides. 2) Active badge (587): drop bg-emerald-500/15; this is chrome, so use a neutral chip (bg-secondary text-muted-foreground) OR, if it must signal running state, wire it to real bot.isRunning and use class 'text-[hsl(var(--trade-bullish))] bg-[var(--trade-bullish-bg)]' ONLY when truly running. 3) Wrap every number in mono: add class 'font-mono tabular-nums' (or reuse the .data-cell / .stat-value utility classes) on all slider value labels (378,712,724,736,748), subscription tier (341 already part-mono — make value mono), positions count (636), and ensure GlassInput type=number already carries font='mono' (it does for trading inputs; bot-threshold sliders need the label fix). 4) Clear button (664): use a single bearish treatment — class 'text-[hsl(var(--trade-bearish))] hover:bg-[var(--trade-bearish-bg)]', drop hover:text-red-600. 5) Disclaimer card (823-825): swap amber-500 literals for 'border-[var(--trade-neutral)]/30 bg-[var(--trade-neutral-bg)]' and keep AlertTriangle on --trade-neutral. 6) Replace bg-muted/30 and bg-muted/50 info rows with the elevation ladder: use bg-secondary (L2) for nested rows inside cards for consistent depth. 7) Swap GlassInput glass variant for the solid tokenized input in-terminal; focus ring should resolve to --brand-cyan. 8) Set Layout-Density default to 'compact' to match the law.
- **Components:** QEPageShell + QEPageHeader + QESectionDivider (from components/ui/qe-page-shell.tsx) for frame; canonical Button default variant for primary CTA, outline for secondary; canonical Card/CardHeader/CardContent (already used); solid Input over GlassInput; reuse index.css utility classes .data-cell/.stat-row/.stat-value/.ticker-font for numeric + ticker rendering instead of ad-hoc spans; lucide icons already in use (no emoji — good).
- **Motion/3D:** Low surface, no 3D moment here (the R3F gamma surface belongs to GEX only). Apply standard micro-interactions: tab triggers and the four Quick-Link buttons get the default hover (border-brighten + translateY(-1px)) and press scale(0.98) on --ease-out-smooth 150-200ms. The floating save bar should slide/fade in via framer-motion (y:8->0, opacity, 200ms ease-out-smooth) instead of appearing instantly, and respect prefers-reduced-motion. No ambient glow.

#### trade-audit  · M effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** Pervasive hardcoded Tailwind palette colors instead of tokens. cyan-400/cyan-500 used everywhere for chrome (icon chips, 'Open' badge, FileText/Activity/idea_published icons, timing-conf metric, 'Live' badge) — should be brand-cyan token. Plus blue-400/blue-500 (FullAnalysis + page-header icon chips, validation_check icon), purple-400/purple-500 (Price Timeline icon chip + signal bars + quality-signals header), amber-500 (Comparison + disclaimer + scoring icon chips), indigo-500/pink-500 (signal color map), green-500/red-500 borders, and bg-white/10 fallback. Violates 'Never hardcode... consume hsl(var(--token))'. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors and off-palette hues used as decoration/chrome, which the law forbids ('encode ONLY money/direction — never chrome'). amber-500/orange gradient powers the Scale (scoring) icon chip and the disclaimer card border/icon; blue powers the audit-trail header icon and the Brain analysis icon; purple powers the Camera/timeline icon and signal bars. None of these encode money or direction. _(high)_; glass-card class (backdrop-blur(16-20px) saturate(180%), ambient box-shadow 0 8px 32px glow) is used on every card. Law mandates the strict 4-step neutral elevation ladder via bg-step + border, 'depth via... not glow', and bans ambient/decorative glow. Cards should be --card / --border, no blur, no glow. _(high)_; Raw hex literals for the Discord CTA: bg-[#5865F2] hover:bg-[#4752C4] text-white. Off-token brand color and a non-token white. Primary/branded CTAs should use brand-cyan or a neutral token; a third-party brand color hardcoded in-component violates the single-accent + token rules. _(high)_; Decorative gradient on the scoring icon chip: bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-500/20 to-orange-500/20. Multi-hue decorative gradient with no data meaning — banned (one chromatic accent; semantic = money only; no decoration). _(med)_; Font family mismatch at the foundation: --font-sans resolves to 'DM Sans' and --font-mono to 'JetBrains Mono' (index.css L272-274, L431-433), but the law requires IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono. Numbers on this page do correctly use font-mono + tabular-nums, but the underlying family is wrong app-wide. Global token fix, surfaced here. _(med)_; Hardcoded score-bar fill uses bg-amber-500 / bg-red-500 (L263) for neutral/bearish thresholds instead of var(--trade-neutral)/var(--trade-bearish), while the bullish branch correctly uses the token — inconsistent. _(med)_; Density is too loose for a Bloomberg-dense terminal. Card pad uses pb-4 + space-y-4 + gap-6 grids; icon chips are h-10 w-10; hero numbers are text-lg/text-2xl/text-3xl ad-hoc Tailwind sizes rather than the fixed data/price scale (price-sm/md/lg, data-xs..lg). No data-density attribute consumed. Law default is compact (pad 8-10px, row 28px, base 12px). _(med)_; Micro-labels use text-xs (12px) sans, but the law's micro-label spec is 9-11px UPPERCASE 500 tracking 0.05-0.08em. Tracking-wider (0.05em) is close but size is one step large; the 9px micro / 11px data-xs scale is not used. Section/stat labels should map to micro/data-xs tokens. _(low)_; getOutcomeBadge mixes token text color with hardcoded palette bg/border: e.g. bg-[var(--trade-bullish)]/20 paired with border-green-500/30, and bg-red-500/20 with text-[var(--trade-bearish)]. Half-tokenized — should derive bg/border from the same semantic token (--trade-bullish-bg etc.). _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions. Cards rely on global .shadcn-card hover (which adds a teal glow box-shadow, itself off-law) but these glass-cards get only border-color transition. No translateY(-1px) hover / scale(0.98) press signature on the interactive cards/rows; animate-pulse used on 'Live' badge and in-progress icon is fine but not the defined motion language. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing information architecture — it is sound: sticky-ish header (back + ticker/timestamp + status badges + Refresh/Share actions), then a 2-col grid (PlanCard | OutcomeCard), then full-width ConfidenceScoring, FullAnalysis, ComparisonCard table, PriceSnapshotTimeline, and disclaimer footer. Tighten the whole page to compact density: replace gap-6 grids with gap-[var(--card-gap)] (10px) or gap-3, CardHeader pb-4 -> pb-2/pb-3, CardContent space-y-4 -> space-y-3, stat tiles p-3 -> p-2. Shrink icon chips from h-10 w-10 to h-8 w-8 (header chip already h-7). Convert the Plan/Outcome stat tiles and the Quantitative-Metrics 3-up into the canonical metric-block / stat-row classes already defined in index.css (L1047, L857) instead of bespoke stat-glass divs. Keep max-w-[1600px] mx-auto.
- **Apply:** COLOR: replace every hardcoded palette class with tokens. All cyan-400/500 chrome -> text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] / bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]/10 / border-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]/30 (brand-cyan is the ONE accent for open/live/links/primary). blue-400 header+analysis icons -> neutral text-muted-foreground on bg-secondary chip (chrome, not semantic). purple timeline/signal chrome -> neutral muted chip; the signal-bar color map should collapse to: R:R signals -> var(--trade-bullish); volume/liquidity -> var(--brand-cyan); technical/trend/breakout/catalyst -> var(--trade-neutral) ONLY if they encode direction, otherwise neutral muted — drop indigo/pink/purple entirely. Score-bar neutral/bearish branches -> var(--trade-neutral)/var(--trade-bearish). getOutcomeBadge: derive bg+border from matching semantic token (bg-[var(--trade-bullish-bg)] border-[color-mix or token]/30) so text/bg/border agree. Discord button: drop bg-[#5865F2]; use variant='outline' neutral or the brand-cyan primary, keep SiDiscord icon. SURFACE/ELEVATION: replace class='glass-card' with a neutral card — bg-card border border-border rounded-[var(--radius-card)] (no backdrop-blur, no glow). Remove the amber-500/orange gradient chip; use a flat bg-[var(--trade-neutral)]/10 chip. Remove border-amber-500/20 on the disclaimer card -> border-border, keep the single amber accent on the AlertTriangle icon + label only (it is a genuine warning, the one allowed non-money amber use, or demote to muted). TYPE: keep font-mono+tabular-nums on all numbers (good); map hero numbers to price-display-md/lg utilities (L998) and micro-labels to a 9-11px uppercase 500 spec — define/use a .micro-label or data-xs class rather than text-xs. Globally fix --font-sans -> 'IBM Plex Sans' and --font-mono -> 'IBM Plex Mono' in index.css :root and .dark (and ensure the fonts are loaded).
- **Components:** Reuse canonical primitives already in the codebase/CSS: shadcn Card/Badge/Button (keep), and the index.css data-system utilities — metric-block (stat grids), stat-row (key/value pairs), data-table-compact (rewrite the Plan-vs-Reality <table> to this class for Bloomberg density + mono tabular cells), price-display-* (hero P&L and prices), section-chrome / bloomberg-panel .panel-header (card section headers), change-indicator--up/down/flat (the percent-gain pills). The score bar should use the existing .confidence-bar / .confidence-bar-fill classes (L556) instead of a hand-rolled bg-muted/30 bar. Icons stay lucide (already compliant, no emoji). data-testid hooks must be preserved.
- **Motion/3D:** Add the standard micro-interaction signature via framer-motion (150-300ms, --ease-out-smooth): cards lift translateY(-1px) + border-brighten on hover, action buttons scale(0.98) on press. Animate the score-bar fill width and the percent-gain number on load/poll-update (count-up or a brief price-tick flash using existing .price-tick-up/.price-tick-down on the live 30s refresh). Timeline rows: subtle staggered fade-up on mount, and hover-elevate (already present) re-pointed to the token elevate vars. Remove the off-law teal box-shadow from global .shadcn-card:hover. Keep prefers-reduced-motion guards. No 3D here — the R3F gamma surface is GEX-only; the live-polling P&L flash is the right signature moment for this surface.

#### trading-rules  · L effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** Glassmorphism is the page's entire structural language — every card uses `glass-card`, every input/checkbox row uses `glass`, status pills use `glass-success`/`glass-danger`, separators/borders use `border-white/10`. The law mandates neutral grayscale surfaces on a 4-step elevation ladder (--background/--card/--secondary/--surface-overlay-solid) with depth from bg-step + --border, NOT translucency/blur. Glass is a landing-only effect, banned in-terminal. _(high)_; Decorative ambient glow: header has `bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/10 via-transparent to-cyan-400/10` overlay (line 99), and every card icon tile is a `bg-gradient-to-br from-{cyan|green|amber}-500/20 to-...-400/10` chip. Law explicitly bans ambient/decorative glow and gradient chrome; depth must come from elevation+border only. _(high)_; Hardcoded Tailwind palette colors instead of tokens throughout: `cyan-500`/`cyan-400`/`text-cyan-400` (should be --brand-cyan), `border-l-green-500`/`from-green-500` (should be --trade-bullish), `border-l-amber-500`/`amber-400` (should be --trade-neutral), `border-l-cyan-500`. Law: never hardcode palette colors; consume hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Semantic colors used as chrome/decoration, not money/direction. Green is the accent border + icon tile of the 'Stop Loss & Targets' card header and the Target/Exit-Rules icons; amber is the accent border + icon of the 'Rules' card and Risk-Management headings; cyan tints icon tiles. Law: --trade-bullish/bearish/neutral encode ONLY money/direction, never chrome. Card-frame accents must be neutral border or the single cyan brand accent. _(high)_; Cyan over-applied as decoration. Three card left-borders are `border-l-cyan-500` and icon tiles are cyan-gradient. Law restricts the one chromatic accent (brand cyan) to focus/active/selection/links/brand/primary-CTA — not a generic card-edge decoration on every card. _(med)_; Non-mono / inconsistent numbers. Numbered rule badges (1–4) render in default sans, and ratio/percent strings inside rule text ('2:1 R:R','3.5%','7%','10.5%') are plain sans. Law: EVERY number uses font-mono + tabular-nums. Hero stop/target values do use font-mono (good), but the crypto card uses raw `toLocaleString` (lines 876/890/901) while the rest use safeToFixed — inconsistent formatting helper. _(med)_; Spacing/radius off the compact default. Cards use `rounded-xl` + `p-5`/`p-6` pad and large `h-10 w-10` icon tiles; law's compact default is card pad 8–10px and ~28px rows. Header uses `text-2xl sm:text-3xl` hero, oversized vs the fixed data scale. No `data-density` hook respected. _(med)_; Bespoke card markup instead of canonical component. Each card is a hand-rolled `<div className="glass-card rounded-xl ...">` with inline header/body divs rather than the repo's components/ui/card.tsx (Card/CardHeader/CardTitle/CardContent). Inconsistent with the rest of the terminal and duplicates structure ~9x. _(med)_; Token-level: the law names IBM Plex Sans/Mono, but `--font-mono` resolves to 'JetBrains Mono' (index.css lines 274/433) and no IBM Plex Sans family is declared. Components correctly consume `font-mono`, so this is a token-definition gap, not a per-component fix. _(low)_; 'X' literal used as an icon-substitute in the 'Things to Avoid' badges (`<span className="glass-danger ...">X</span>`). Law: icons are lucide only — use an XCircle/Ban lucide glyph, not a typed letter glyph as decoration. _(low)_; No motion signature. Cards rely on the legacy `hover-elevate` class; there is no framer-motion 150–300ms border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover or scale(0.98) press, and tab transitions are unanimated. Misses the prescribed micro-interaction layer (must still respect prefers-reduced-motion). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 3-tab Options/Stocks/Crypto structure and the per-tab grid (two calc/target cards on top in md:grid-cols-2, full-width Rules card, Options checklist). Tighten to compact density: replace `container p-6 space-y-6` with a denser shell (p-4, space-y-4, --card-gap 10px grids), cards at p-2.5 with smaller/flatter icon tiles, ~28px rows; add data-density awareness. Convert the hero header from a glass+gradient banner to a flat header row on --background: a UPPERCASE tracked micro-label ('RISK MANAGEMENT') + plain --foreground H1 at the fixed data-lg/price scale, no gradient overlay.
- **Apply:** 1) Swap glass→token surfaces: glass-card→Card(bg-card); nested rows (inputs, checklist items, stop/target sub-panels)→bg-secondary; glass-success/danger pills→--trade-*-bg tokens; border-white/10→border-border; remove ALL from-*/to-* gradients and the header overlay. 2) Replace hardcoded cyan/green/amber Tailwind classes with hsl(var(--brand-cyan)) / hsl(var(--trade-bullish)) / hsl(var(--trade-neutral)) / hsl(var(--trade-bearish)). 3) Re-scope color: cyan only for brand/focus; semantic colors only on money/direction; neutralize decorative card edges; drop or flatten gradient icon tiles to bg-secondary. 4) Mono-ize every number (rule badges 1–4, percents/ratios in rule text, all calc outputs) with font-mono + tabular-nums; unify on safeToFixed (replace the three raw toLocaleString calls in the crypto card). 5) Adopt compact density + canonical Card components. 6) Token fix (separate task): point --font-mono at IBM Plex Mono and add IBM Plex Sans as --font-sans, or formally accept JetBrains as the project mono.
- **Components:** Replace all ~9 hand-rolled card divs with the canonical Card/CardHeader/CardTitle/CardContent from components/ui/card.tsx. Reuse existing Tabs/TabsList/TabsTrigger, Input, Label, Checkbox, Separator, Button (drop the `glass`/`glass-secondary` button+input variants for the default token-driven variants). For Within-Limits/Too-Risky and Ready-to-Trade pills use the repo's badge pattern backed by --trade-bullish-bg/--trade-bearish-bg (semantic is legitimate — it encodes risk/direction). Use .accent-card + .accent-card--cyan|--bullish|--bearish|--neutral (already token-backed, 2px edge) instead of `border-l-{color}-500`, and apply a semantic accent ONLY where it maps to money/direction (bearish edge on Stop-Loss card, neutral edge on cautions), neutral border elsewhere. Replace the 'X' text badge with a lucide Ban/XCircle glyph.
- **Motion/3D:** Add the standard micro-interaction layer and drop the legacy `hover-elevate`: framer-motion on each Card — hover = border-brighten (--border→--border-strong) + translateY(-1px), press/tap = scale(0.98), 150–300ms on --ease-out-smooth; crossfade TabsContent on tab switch; subtle checkbox check spring. All gated behind prefers-reduced-motion. No 3D moment applies here — the R3F gamma surface is GEX-only.

#### conviction-backtest  · M effort · 10 issues

These ARE money/direction so they should be semantic — but via tokens, not raw palette. _(high)_; Numbers are not mono. Every figure (KPI values text-3xl font-bold, table cells, %, R values, counts) relies only on tabular-nums; none use font-mono. Law requires IBM Plex Mono for EVERY number. The hero numbers use text-3xl font-bold sans, not the price-sm/md/lg mono scale. Only the 'As of' timestamp (line 213) and source name (line 350) use font-mono. _(high)_; Band badge for B uses sky (bg-sky-500/20 text-sky-400) and C uses amber as decorative chrome (line 95-96). Amber=--trade-neutral must encode only money/direction, never a generic band-tier chip; sky is not a sanctioned color at all. _(high)_; Elevation ladder ignored. All cards use bg-card/60 (alpha-blended card, line 147,176,187,199,...) rather than the solid 4-step ladder (--background L0 / --card L1 / --secondary L2 / --surface-overlay-solid L3). Inner strips use bg-muted/20 and bg-muted/30 (lines 102,321) and SelectTrigger bg-muted/50 (line 152) — translucency instead of token surfaces. Bar track bg-muted/30 should be a border/secondary step. _(med)_; Density is spacious, not compact default. py-8 page pad, p-4/p-12 card pad, gap-4/gap-6 grids, text-3xl hero numbers, text-lg headings, no data-density attribute. Law default is Bloomberg-compact (card pad 8-10px, 28px rows, base 12-13px data). Table rows/headers use default shadcn sizing rather than data-table-compact. _(med)_; Typography scale off-spec. Headings use ad-hoc text-3xl/text-lg font-bold; micro-labels use text-xs uppercase without the 0.05-0.08em tracking + 500 weight spec; the [10px] footnote (line 321) is a raw arbitrary size instead of the micro (9px) / data-xs (11px) token scale. _(med)_; Motion is minimal/partial. Only the header has a one-shot fade (lines 131-134). No hover micro-interactions on cards/rows (law default = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale .98 at 150-300ms on --ease-out-smooth). RefreshCw spin is fine. No prefers-reduced-motion guard noted on the entry animation. _(low)_; Bar component is a bespoke inline div with hardcoded color classes passed as strings (bg-purple-500/bg-emerald-500/bg-rose-500) rather than a tokenized canonical bar/sparkline. Color is passed positionally, encouraging more raw-palette leakage. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong existing structure (header + lookback control row + 4-up KPI grid + Per-Band table + Per-Source table + 2-up Blind Spots/Overpromises). Tighten to compact density: set data-density="compact" on the page root, reduce container py-8→py-4, card p-4→p-2.5 (8-10px), grid gap-4/gap-6→gap-2.5 (var(--card-gap)), hero numbers text-3xl→price-md mono. Convert both tables to the canonical .data-table-compact pattern (28px rows, data-xs cells, uppercase 500 tracked headers). Make the 4 KPI cards a denser data-strip-style stat row.

Purple is not a permitted color in the law (allowed: cyan brand + bullish/bearish/neutral + teal demoted to logo/GEX). _(high)_; Raw Tailwind palette classes instead of tokens — 31 instances of bg/text/border-(red|green|emerald|amber|orange)-NNN. Hero numbers use generic 'text-3xl font-bold' (859,1421) and 'text-2xl' (579) rather than the price-display-lg/md helpers. Body uses generic text-sm (50x) / text-xs (50x) / text-lg (11x) instead of the data-xs/sm/base/lg token scale. Arbitrary 'text-[10px]'/'text-[9px]' (7x) for micro-labels/badges instead of the 9px micro / 11px data-xs tokens. _(med)_; Numbers are mono (good — font-mono is applied broadly) but do NOT use the canonical .data-cell (tabular-nums + slashed zero) or .ticker-font helpers. Ticker/optionSymbol rendered as plain 'font-mono' (846) without uppercase+tracking ticker treatment; strikes/greeks lack tabular alignment guarantee on narrow columns. _(med)_; No data-density adoption. Spacing is hand-tuned per element (CardHeader py-3 px-4, CardContent p-4, table p-2, mixed gap-2/3/4) rather than driven by the one data-density attribute. Density is also not compact-default Bloomberg spec — table rows and card padding sit at comfortable, not the 28px-row / 8-10px-pad compact default. _(med)_; Verdict/grade color helpers are internally inconsistent and duplicated. getVerdictColor (449) and getVerdictBadgeColor (1092) are near-identical copies; both mix token text ('text-[var(--trade-bullish)]') with raw borders ('border-green-500/30'). getGradeColor/getGradeTextColor (460,1103) also duplicated; both use 'text-orange-400' (raw) for grade D. Should be one tokenized helper. _(med)_; Hand-rolled flex-div bar charts for the volatility surface (1183-1207), term structure (1227-1244) and strategy payoff diagram (1662-1691). The vol surface uses red/green/amber raw-ish bars; payoff uses bullish/bearish tokens. These are exactly the surfaces the law earmarks for the R3F 3D treatment, yet rendered as crude CSS-height bars with no 2D-canonical chart component. _(low)_; Decorative motion gap / page-atmosphere ambient. Page wraps in 'page-atmosphere' (469) which paints fixed radial cyan/blue glows + grid — borderline ambient decoration the law discourages in-terminal (NO ambient/decorative glow). Bars use bare 'transition-all hover:opacity-80' (1193) rather than the 150-300ms ease-out-smooth border-brighten/translateY signature. _(low)_; Icons all lucide, zero emoji — compliant. Numbers consistently mono — largely compliant. These are the strengths; noted so the redesign preserves them. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 4-tab structure (Chain / Vol Surf / Pricing / Strategy) and the 2-col (lg:col-span-2 chain + 1-col analysis) split — the information architecture is sound. Tighten to compact density: convert the OptionsChainTable to .data-table-compact (28px rows, 11px mono cells, uppercase 10px headers), drop CardHeader to py-2 px-3, card body to p-2.5-p-3, normalize all gaps to the --card-gap (10px) / gap-2 rhythm. Make the chain the hero: ATM strike row pinned/highlighted, ITM shaded via --muted not raw amber/emerald.
- **Apply:** COLOR: delete every purple-* — reassign decorative section icons (IV Metrics, Full Greeks, Term Structure, Strategy Builder) to muted-foreground or --brand-cyan (chrome accent), and reassign the genuinely-semantic ones to tokens: 5x-bagger row → keep as a profit tier using --trade-bullish gradient stops (not purple), skew/strategy left-borders → .accent-card--cyan or .accent-card--bullish/bearish per meaning. Replace all 31 raw red/green/emerald/amber/orange palette classes with --trade-bullish/-bearish/-neutral (+ matching -bg tokens) — e.g. 'bg-red-500/10'→'bg-[var(--trade-bearish-bg)]', 'bg-emerald-500/10'→'bg-[var(--trade-bullish-bg)]', ATM row→'bg-[var(--trade-neutral-bg)] border-l-2 border-l-[var(--trade-neutral)]'. Replace both border-l-cyan-500 with the .accent-card--cyan helper. Collapse getVerdictColor+getVerdictBadgeColor into ONE tokenized helper, and getGradeColor+getGradeTextColor into one (grade D → --trade-neutral or --grade-d token, not text-orange-400). TYPOGRAPHY: hero numbers (stock price, BS theoretical price, overall grade) → .price-display-lg/.price-display-md; all numeric cells → add .data-cell; optionSymbol/ticker → .ticker-font; micro labels (the text-[9px]/[10px] badges, table headers, 'DTE','strike','prem') → uppercase sans 500 at 9px micro / 11px data-xs tokens; body text-sm→data-sm(12px), text-xs→data-xs(11px). DENSITY: wrap page root (or layout) in data-density="compact" and let spacing derive from it rather than per-element px. MOTION: replace 'transition-all hover:opacity-80' on bars with border-brighten + translateY(-1px) at 0.2s var(--ease-out-smooth); rows already use hover-elevate (keep). Reconsider page-atmosphere on this in-terminal surface — drop or reduce to a flat --background per the no-ambient-glow rule.
- **Components:** Use canonical helpers from index.css: .data-table-compact (chain), .data-cell + .ticker-font (all numbers/tickers), .price-display-lg/md (hero figures), .accent-card--cyan/--bullish/--bearish (left-border cards replacing border-l-cyan-500/border-l-purple-500), .metric-block (the p-3 rounded bg-muted/30 stat tiles in DeepAnalysisPanel/PricingLab → metric-block label/value), .stat-row (the flex justify-between key/value pairs in IV Metrics, Risk Analysis, Skew, Monte Carlo), .change-indicator--up/down (the stockChange badge at 582), .section-chrome (panel sub-headers). Replace the two duplicated color-helper pairs with a single shared util.
- **Motion/3D:** SIGNATURE OPPORTUNITY: the Vol Surf tab is the one place besides GEX with a real 3D moment. Replace the hand-rolled IV-by-strike bar div (1183-1207) and term-structure bars with a lazy-loaded React-Three-Fiber implied-volatility surface (strike × expiry × IV mesh), cyan-lit peaks / muted valleys, OrbitControls clamped, with the existing flex-bar render kept as the 2D prefers-reduced-motion / no-WebGL fallback. This directly fulfills the law's 'one signature 3D moment' pattern and the surface data (surface.strikes/expiries/ivMatrix) is already shaped as a matrix ready for a mesh. Strategy payoff diagram stays 2D but should move to a proper line/area chart component.

#### market-outlook  · L effort · 10 issues
*Today:* A dense, well-structured public 'tomorrow's outlook' dashboard (header + summary banner + 2/3·1/3 grid of futures, indices, gaps, swings, convictions, Friday recap, events, geo, news) that is visually busy and heavily off-spec on color: it leans on raw Tailwind palette classes (amber/emerald/red/indigo/purple), raw rgba hex in the sector treemap, teal as the primary accent, and emoji/unicode glyphs as icons.

- **Violations:** ~100 hardcoded Tailwind palette color classes instead of CSS tokens: amber-400 (24x), red-400 (10x), emerald-500/red-500 with opacity suffixes (~40x), indigo-400 (3x), indigo-500/15, purple-400, purple-500/15. Bullish/bearish TEXT uses the var but bg/border use raw emerald-500/red-500 (#22c55e), a different green from token --trade-bullish #10b981. Law: never hardcode; consume hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; 4 raw rgba() hex literals in SectorTreemap inline styles: rgba(34,197,94,...) (green #22c55e) and rgba(239,68,68,...). The bullish token is #10b981, so the treemap renders the wrong green AND is hardcoded. Must derive from --trade-bullish / --trade-bearish. _(high)_; --brand-teal used 20x as the primary chromatic accent: every card-header icon, section titles, tickers (swing/conviction/carry-over symbols), active sort header, weekly-focus badges, loading spinner, header logo tile. Law demotes teal to the logo stripe + GEX surface ONLY; the sole accent for focus/active/links/brand is --brand-cyan. This is not the GEX surface, so all 20 should become --brand-cyan. _(high)_; Emoji + unicode glyphs used as icons (law: lucide only, no emoji). {s.icon} (line 699) renders emoji from geopolitical data. 19 unicode glyphs as chrome: ▲/▼ in gainers/losers toggle and sector-impact arrows, → flow arrows in carry-over/swing rows, ↑/↓ sort indicators, — and · separators. Replace with lucide (ArrowUp/ArrowDown/ArrowRight/ChevronUp/ChevronDown/Dot). _(high)_; Off-palette colors with NO token: indigo (weekend Moon tile bg-indigo-500/15, Moon/crypto-Globe/sector-Activity icons) and purple (S-tier conviction band, purple-400/purple-500/15). Not in the token system at all. Must route to neutral, brand-cyan, or a new tokenized rank chip. _(high)_; Amber (--trade-neutral) used as generic chrome/decoration, not money/direction: Zap icons on Pre-Market Gaps / Breaking News / Carry-Over / Earnings headers, AlertTriangle alert, 'medium importance' event dots, volume>1.5x avg flag, freshness 'stale' badge, geopolitical pulse dot. Law: trade-neutral encodes ONLY money/direction. Header icons should be muted-foreground; only flat-gap/neutral-sentiment data keeps amber. _(med)_; Density exceeds compact default: all cards use p-4 (16px) vs law card pad 8–10px; rows are py-2/py-2.5; no data-density attribute consumed. Spacing is comfortable, not Bloomberg-dense. _(med)_; Motion incomplete vs signature: rows/cards use only transition-colors (no border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover, no scale(0.98) press). animate-pulse on the geopolitical risk dot is an ambient/decorative pulse (law bans ambient glow in-terminal). Framer-motion is used only for one-shot mount fades. _(med)_; Typography token gap: sizes use ad-hoc arbitrary values text-[8px]/[9px]/[10px]/[11px] instead of the fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13). 8px labels are below the 9px floor. Uppercase micro-labels not standardized to a shared class. _(low)_; Numbers correctly use font-mono almost everywhere (good); a few label/count badges mix sans/mono. Minor; relies on the global mono font rule for tabular-nums/slashed-zero. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong existing structure (max-w-[1400px] header + summary banner + high-impact alert + lg:grid-cols-3 2/3·1/3 grid). Tighten to compact: card pad p-4 -> p-2.5 (~10px), grid gap-4 -> gap-3, section header mb-3 -> mb-2, list rows py-2/py-2.5 -> py-1.5 (28px target), Friday movers table rows to 28px, sector treemap cells token-driven. Standardize every section to a single SectionHeader pattern (lucide icon + uppercase micro-label + ml-auto count Badge).
- **Apply:** COLOR: emerald-500/x -> --trade-bullish-bg / --trade-bullish-bg-subtle (or color-mix on hsl(var(--trade-bullish))); red-500/x -> --trade-bearish-bg variants; text-emerald-/red- -> text-[hsl(var(--trade-bullish))]/[hsl(var(--trade-bearish))]. SectorTreemap inline rgba(34,197,94) -> color-mix on hsl(var(--trade-bullish)); rgba(239,68,68) -> hsl(var(--trade-bearish)). ACCENT: swap all 20 --brand-teal -> --brand-cyan (not the GEX surface). AMBER: header chrome icons -> text-muted-foreground; keep --trade-neutral ONLY for flat-gap %, neutral sentiment, neutral VIX band. INDIGO: weekend Moon tile -> bg-secondary + muted-foreground or brand-cyan; crypto/sector icons -> muted-foreground. PURPLE S-band: define a tokenized rank chip (e.g. --rank-s) or reuse brand-cyan strong; never raw purple. ICONS: {s.icon} emoji -> map category to lucide; replace ▲▼→↑↓—· with lucide ArrowUp/ArrowDown/ArrowRight/ChevronUp/ChevronDown/Dot. TYPE: text-[8px] -> 9px floor; standardize 9/10/11/12/13px to the data-xs/sm/base scale; ensure all numerics keep font-mono. MOTION: add hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px) and active scale(0.98) on interactive rows/cards (framer-motion or an .interactive-row utility, 150ms --ease-out-smooth); remove animate-pulse on the geo dot (static colored Dot); gate all motion behind prefers-reduced-motion.
- **Components:** Use canonical client/src/components/ui Card + Badge (already imported). Standardize a SectionHeader (lucide icon in muted-foreground + UPPERCASE sans-500 micro-label + count Badge), a DataCell wrapper applying font-mono tabular-nums, and the local ChangeBadge (swap its arrows to consistent lucide, route color through --trade-bullish/bearish). If a shared DataTable/SortableTable primitive exists, replace FridayMoversTable's hand-rolled <table> with it; otherwise keep but tokenize. Reuse any existing conviction-band chip from home/hunt for the S/A/B/C bands.
- **Motion/3D:** No 3D here — the single R3F moment is reserved for the GEX gamma surface. Keep the existing staggered mount fades (already framer-motion). Add the standard hover/press micro-interactions to every clickable row (movers table rows, gainers/losers toggle, sort headers, swing/conviction/carry-over rows). Optionally animate sector treemap cell heights/fills on data refresh with a 200ms ease-out transition. Do NOT add ambient glow.

#### radar  · M effort · 10 issues

- **Violations:** Hardcoded Tailwind palette colors throughout instead of tokens. PickCard targets/stop use text-emerald-500 / text-rose-500 (lines 150-152); TrackTab hit-rate and avg-pct use ternary text-emerald-500/text-rose-500 (lines 289-294); FormingTab empty-state icon uses text-amber-500/40 (line 179). Law requires money/direction to consume --trade-bullish / --trade-bearish / --trade-neutral via tokens, never raw emerald/rose/amber. _(high)_; Local GradePill (lines 304-315) re-invents the grade system with raw bg-emerald-500/15, bg-blue-500/15, bg-amber-500/15, bg-rose-500/15 and ignores the canonical componentStyles.badge.grade map (S/A/B/C/D/F bound to --grade-s..--grade-f). It also has NO 'S' branch, so S-tier picks fall through to the rose/F color — a correctness bug given the user's S-tier criteria. blue-500 is a chromatic color outside the allowed accent set entirely. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors leaking into chrome. Patterns tab uses text-primary/60 and [{f.domain}] brackets plus the GradePill B-grade as blue — but more importantly the grade encoding mixes brand/semantic hues for what is conviction chrome, not money/direction. Canonical badge.grade keeps B as neutral muted-foreground; the local version forces chromatic blue. _(med)_; Hand-rolled cards instead of QECard. PickCard (line 116) and Patterns cards (line 212) use raw 'qe-card border border-border/40 rounded-md p-3'. Law/primitives mandate <QECard variant=...>; the qe/index.ts explicitly says stop hand-rolling card chrome. _(med)_; Hand-rolled signal pills instead of QEPill. PickCard signal chips (lines 137-144) use raw bg-muted/30 border border-border/30; should be <QEPill variant='default'>. Re-scan button (lines 357-364) is a raw styled button, not a canonical button/QEPill action. _(med)_; Local EmptyState + LoadingState (lines 317-334) duplicate the canonical EmptyState/InlineEmptyState in components/ui/empty-state.tsx. Duplication risks drift from the law's typography/spacing. _(low)_; Inconsistent radius: cards use rounded-md while the token system defines --radius-card (10px / rounded-[0.625rem]) consumed by QECard. Mixing rounded-md (6px), rounded (4px) on chips, and rounded-full elsewhere violates the single card-radius rule. _(low)_; Typography scale drift. Page uses ad-hoc text-[9px]/[10px]/[11px]/text-sm/text-lg rather than the fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15). 9px and 10px both appear for similar metadata; numbers use font-mono but not the .data-cell tabular-nums/slashed-zero treatment. _(low)_; Note: font tokens in index.css are DM Sans / JetBrains Mono, not the IBM Plex Sans/Mono the law names. This is a system-wide token mismatch (out of scope for one page) but means radar inherits the wrong family — flag for the token-layer owner. _(low)_; Hover uses bare 'transition' / 'hover:border-primary/30' (line 116) rather than the law's standard hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) on --ease-out-smooth 150-300ms. No press scale(0.98) on cards. Re-scan spinner OK (lucide), but no prefers-reduced-motion guard authored at page level. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 4-tab structure (header + QETabs + hint + tab body) — it is sound and compact. Replace the hand-rolled container scaffolding: wrap each tab body's list in QESection where a titled panel adds clarity (e.g. Track Record as a QESection titled 'PATTERN TRACK RECORD'). Convert PickCard and Pattern cards to QECard. Keep px-4 py-3 page padding and space-y-2 list rhythm (matches compact density). Track Record grid stays 7-col but should adopt .data-table-compact semantics (uppercase mono headers via the existing class) for token-consistent header tracking/border.
- **Apply:** Replace text-emerald-500 → text-[var(--trade-bullish)]; text-rose-500 → text-[var(--trade-bearish)]; text-amber-500/40 → text-[var(--trade-neutral)]/40. Track Record hit-rate/avg-pct ternary: bullish/bearish tokens. Grade pill: drop blue-500 entirely; use componentStyles.badge.grade (B stays neutral muted-foreground per law). Wrap every price/percent/grade/ticker number cell in .data-cell (or at least add font-variant-numeric tabular-nums lining-nums + slashed zero) — they are mono but lack tabular/slashed-zero. Normalize radius to QECard's --radius-card; chips use --radius-badge (rounded). Collapse the 9px/10px mix to the fixed scale (metadata = data-xs 11px or micro 9px UPPERCASE labels, not both arbitrarily).
- **Components:** Use canonical primitives from @/components/ui/qe: QECard (variant 'default' for PickCard, 'interactive' for hover-lift; 'accent-bull'/'accent-bear' left-stripe to encode direction instead of ad-hoc text colors), QEPill (variant 'default' for signal chips, 'bull'/'bear'/'cyan'/'gold' for status). Delete local GradePill and instead render componentStyles.badge.grade[letter] (S/A/B/C/D/F → --grade-* tokens) — this fixes the missing-S bug AND tokenizes color. Delete local EmptyState/LoadingState; import EmptyState/InlineEmptyState from @/components/ui/empty-state. Re-scan should use the canonical Button (variant ghost/outline) not a raw <button>.
- **Motion/3D:** Apply the law's standard card micro-interaction via QECard variant 'interactive': border-brighten + translateY(-1px) on hover (framer-motion 150-200ms, --ease-out-smooth), press scale(0.98). Wrap tab-body mount in a 150ms opacity fade (animate-page-in already exists) for tab switches. Re-scan: keep lucide RefreshCw spin; on success, flash the newly-fired count with a brief --trade-bullish tick (price-tick-up class pattern). All gated behind prefers-reduced-motion. No new 3D — radar is not the GEX surface; keep it flat and dense.

#### strategy-simulator  · M effort · 10 issues
*Today:* A clean, sensibly-structured dark backtest page (config panel, 5 aggregate KPIs, sortable per-symbol table, expandable trade log) that already consumes most tokens correctly, but reads more like a generic admin dashboard than a Bloomberg-dense terminal: emoji icons, oversized sans hero numbers, unstyled native sliders, and hand-rolled tables instead of the canonical dense components.

- **Violations:** Emoji-as-icon throughout, banned by law (lucide only, no emoji): header title '🔬 Strategy Simulator' (line 105), Run button '🔬 Running...' / '▶ Run Backtest' (line 193), exit-reason cells '🎯 T1' / '🛑 STOP' / '⌛ TIME' (line 310), empty-state '🔬' (line 330). _(high)_; Numbers not in mono with tabular-nums. KPI hero values (line 354) are text-2xl font-bold sans, no font-mono/price-display/tabular-nums. Per-symbol 'Trades' cell (line 253) is plain sans. Score-at-entry (line 302) and best/worst tickers are font-bold sans. Law: EVERY number = IBM Plex Mono + tabular-nums + slashed zero. _(high)_; Ticker symbols rendered as plain font-bold sans, not the canonical ticker treatment (uppercase mono 600, +0.04em). r.symbol (line 252), bestSymbol/worstSymbol (lines 219/221), selectedResult.symbol header (line 278). The .ticker-font class exists in index.css and is unused. _(med)_; Native range sliders (lines 150-184) are completely unstyled — default browser thumb/track (OS blue/gray), no brand-cyan accent-color, no token theming. Off-palette chrome on a core control. _(med)_; Type scale off-spec. Header text-3xl (line 105), KPI text-2xl (line 354), empty-state text-5xl/text-xl (lines 330-331) ignore the fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 + price-sm/md/lg). Micro-labels use raw text-[10px] + tracking-wider (lines 134,147,160,173,217,353) instead of the 0.05-0.08em uppercase-500 spec / metric-label token. _(med)_; Fonts in index.css are DM Sans + JetBrains Mono (lines 272-274, 431-433), not the law-mandated IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono. Numbers/words inherit the wrong families even where font-mono is applied. Foundation-level, affects the whole app but visible on this surface. _(med)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions on rows/cards/CTA (law: 150-300ms ease-out-smooth, hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale(0.98)). Table row hover is only bg-muted/20; CTA has no press/hover lift beyond the global rule. No component-level prefers-reduced-motion handling. _(low)_; Does not use existing canonical components/classes: tables are hand-rolled text-xs instead of .data-table-compact; KPIs are bespoke instead of .metric-block; cards use componentStyles.card.default (rounded-lg) rather than .bloomberg-panel with panel-header. Inconsistent with the dense terminal system. _(low)_; No data-density attribute support; spacing is hardcoded (page p-6, card p-4, py-2). Default should be compact (card pad 8-10px, row 28px); current padding skews comfortable. _(low)_; Best-vs-Worst colors the ticker glyphs themselves with trade-bullish/trade-bearish (lines 219/221). Direction encoding is legit, but the ticker is chrome — color should live on a +/- badge, not the symbol text. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 4-zone vertical stack (Config -> Aggregate KPIs -> Per-symbol table -> Trade log); it's logical. Set the page root to data-density='compact' and tighten spacing: page p-6 -> p-4, card p-4 -> p-2.5/p-3, table rows to 28px. Wrap each section in .bloomberg-panel with a .panel-header (mono uppercase 0.08em title) instead of componentStyles.card.default + ad-hoc h2. Replace the bespoke KPI grid with .metric-block tiles. Convert both <table> blocks to .data-table-compact. Header h1 drops from text-3xl to data-lg / price-display-md; empty-state from text-5xl/text-xl to a muted lucide icon + data-base copy.
- **Apply:** P1, effort M. Steps: (1) swap all 5 emoji groups for lucide imports. (2) Wrap every number in font-mono via .metric-value/.price-display-sm + tabular-nums; color via existing tokens — win-rate/expectancy/P&L keep --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish/--trade-neutral (legit money/direction), while score, active preset, selected row, CTA, 'expand' affordance, links use --brand-cyan ONLY (already correct). (3) Apply .ticker-font to symbols; move best/worst color onto +/- badges, not the glyph. (4) Convert tables to .data-table-compact and KPIs to .metric-block; frame sections with .bloomberg-panel. (5) Theme the 3 range sliders: accent-color: var(--brand-cyan) plus ::-webkit-slider-thumb cyan / track hsl(var(--border)). (6) Add data-density='compact' to page root and tighten padding. (7) Add framer-motion hover/press with reduced-motion guard. Separate foundation task (flag, not in scope here): fix index.css fonts DM Sans->IBM Plex Sans and JetBrains Mono->IBM Plex Mono (lines 272-274 / 431-433) so mono numbers are truly law-compliant app-wide.
- **Components:** Use canonical CSS classes already in index.css: .bloomberg-panel/.panel-header for section frames; .metric-block (.metric-label/.metric-value) for the 5 aggregate KPIs; .data-table-compact for both tables; .ticker-font for every symbol; .change-indicator--up/down/flat for P&L % and expectancy chips; .price-display-sm/md for entry/exit $ and hero numbers; .metric-label / micro token for all uppercase micro-labels (replacing raw text-[10px]+tracking-wider). Replace all emoji with lucide: FlaskConical (header + empty state), Play/Loader2 (Run button), Target (T1_HIT), OctagonX/ShieldAlert (STOP_HIT), Clock/Hourglass (TIME_EXPIRED).
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion: table rows get whileHover border-brighten + y:-1 (150ms, cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)); CTA whileTap scale(0.98); aggregate KPI cards animate-page-in with stagger when results arrive. All gated behind prefers-reduced-motion. No new glow/ambient effects. No 3D on this surface (the RTF gamma surface is GEX-only) — stays flat-dense. Optional 2D-only equity-curve sparkline per symbol row via .sparkline-cell.

#### terminal-trinity  · M effort · 10 issues
*Today:* A compact, dark, Bloomberg-dense list of 5 index rows (SPY/QQQ/IWM/DIA/SPXW), each an expandable accordion showing spot/NET/FLIP/MAX GEX stats and a regime pill, with a top toolbar carrying an "INDEX MODE" label and a GEX/VEX toggle; rows expand to embed the canonical GEXExpiryMatrix. It is mostly on-spec (mono numbers, tokenized surfaces, uppercase micro-labels) but leaks a few raw Tailwind palette colors and reimplements the toolbar inline instead of using the canonical HeatseekerToolbar.

- **Violations:** Raw Tailwind palette color for the FLIP value: `text-violet-400` (line 66). The codebase has a dedicated `--gex-flip` token (used correctly in olalgo.tsx) for exactly the gamma-flip line. Hardcoded palette class bypasses the token layer. _(high)_; Raw Tailwind palette color for the VEX toggle active state: `bg-violet-400/15 text-violet-400` (line 137). VEX has no token. Should map to `--gex-negative` (the purple GEX-surface token), the law-sanctioned purple for this surface. (Same flaw exists upstream in the canonical HeatseekerToolbar PillButton.) _(high)_; Raw Tailwind palette color for the fetching spinner: `text-amber-400` (line 90). Chrome/status decoration must not use raw amber; `--trade-neutral` is reserved for money/direction only, so a spinner should be neutral `text-muted-foreground`. _(med)_; Toolbar is reimplemented inline (lines 113-148) instead of using the canonical HeatseekerToolbar the file's own docstring references. Duplicates GEX/VEX pill logic and drifts (py-2/px-4 and [9px] pills vs the canonical PillButton [10px] + ring treatment). _(med)_; Accent semantics: `--gex-positive` (yellow data color for call walls) is overloaded as generic chrome — the 'INDEX MODE' page label (line 117) and 'Loading SYMBOL' text (line 102). Title/loading should be neutral muted-foreground, not a GEX data token. _(med)_; Hero spot price (line 61) renders at text-[9px] inside the micro-label cluster with only `tabular-nums` (no lining-nums/slashed-zero) and is off the law's fixed data/price scale — the most important number per row is typographically indistinguishable from its labels. _(med)_; Off-scale font sizes: `text-[8px]` regime pill (line 82) and pervasive `text-[9px]` (lines 60,73,78,126,136,144) sit below/around the law's smallest defined step (micro 9px / data-xs 11px). 8px is sub-spec; the [9px] clusters should be data-xs 11px or the micro token. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions. Law specifies 150-300ms ease-out hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98). Rows use only `transition-colors`/`transition-all duration-200` with no lift; expand/collapse is an instant DOM toggle with no height/opacity motion. _(low)_; Border opacity drift: ad-hoc `border-border/20`, `/30`, `/15` (lines 43,50,114,120,121) rather than the law's `--border` / `--border-strong` elevation ladder. _(low)_; Token-vs-law mismatch (codebase-wide, surfaced here): index.css defines `--font-sans: 'DM Sans'` and `--font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono'` but the law mandates IBM Plex Sans/Mono; `--background` is `0 0% 4%` vs law's #0a0a0a. This page inherits the wrong families — fix at the token source. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the accordion-list layout — correct for multi-index. (1) Replace the inline toolbar (lines 113-148) with the canonical HeatseekerToolbar, passing mode/onModeChange plus an `extra` slot for the 'N indices' caption; suppress the ticker-tab row via a new `showTickers={false}` prop so the page stays single-purpose. (2) Restructure each IndexRow into 3 zones: [chevron + ticker 64px] | [hero spot price + stat cluster] | [regime pill + spinner], so price reads as the row hero, not a label sibling. (3) Normalize borders to `border-border` / `border-border-strong`, dropping the /15-/30 opacity soup. NOTE: GEX-palette (yellow call-wall / purple put-wall via --gex-* tokens) is explicitly law-sanctioned on the GEX surface — keep NET green/red and the yellow MAX-gamma star; the fix is using TOKENS for them, not raw Tailwind classes. The signature R3F gamma surface already lives in the embedded GEXExpiryMatrix on expand, which is the correct single 3D moment.
- **Apply:** (1) FLIP line 66: `text-violet-400` -> `text-[var(--gex-flip)]`. (2) VEX active toggle line 137: `bg-violet-400/15 text-violet-400` -> `bg-[var(--gex-negative)]/15 text-[var(--gex-negative)]` (push same fix into HeatseekerToolbar PillButton). (3) Spinner line 90: `text-amber-400` -> `text-muted-foreground`. (4) INDEX MODE label line 117 + loading text line 102: drop `--gex-positive`, use `text-muted-foreground`. (5) Hero price line 61: bump to price-sm/data-base scale (`text-[13px] font-semibold`) and apply the existing `data-cell`/`price-display-sm` class so it carries tabular-nums + lining-nums + slashed-zero. (6) Replace `text-[8px]`/`text-[9px]` with the fixed scale: regime pill -> micro 9px, stat labels -> data-xs 11px. (7) Source-level: switch `--font-sans`/`--font-mono` to IBM Plex Sans/Mono and `--background` to #0a0a0a in index.css :root/.dark.
- **Components:** Adopt canonical HeatseekerToolbar (client/src/components/heatseeker/heatseeker-toolbar.tsx) in place of the inline toolbar. Keep the embedded GEXExpiryMatrix (already canonical). Use index.css helpers `price-display-sm`/`data-cell` for numbers and standard regime-pill conventions instead of bespoke inline classes.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion to IndexRow: hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) on `--ease-out-smooth` ~150ms, press scale(0.98); animate expand/collapse with AnimatePresence height+opacity 200-250ms instead of the instant `expanded &&` toggle. Gate all motion behind prefers-reduced-motion. No new 3D — the GEX gamma surface inside GEXExpiryMatrix is the single signature 3D moment.

Should consume var(--grade-*). _(high)_; Emoji / glyph used as icons. ✓ and ✗ in pushResult (L141,146), error box (L198), ROI-down arrows; the '> ' terminal prefix is decorative text glyph. Law: lucide icons only, no emoji. Use Check/X lucide icons and a styled ChevronRight or muted span for the prompt prefix. _(med)_; Type scale off-grid. Pervasive ad-hoc sizes text-[8px], text-[9px], text-[10px], text-[11px], text-[12px], text-[13px], text-[14px] (analyze.tsx L18,26,29; analyzer throughout) instead of the fixed tokens micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15. text-[8px] is below the micro floor and the page header mixes text-lg with text-[11px]. _(med)_; Numbers lack tabular-nums treatment. Spot/Mid/Greeks/IV (L220-240), plan boxes, ROI %, breakeven, strikes are font-mono but never get font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums lining-nums + slashed zero. The canonical .data-cell / .price-display / .metric-block classes exist for exactly this and are unused. _(med)_; Page header is mono-uppercase-bold for a words heading (analyze.tsx L14). Law: IBM Plex Sans for all words; mono is for numbers/tickers only. The H1 'Contract Analyzer' should be sans 600/700, not mono. _(med)_; Ad-hoc card chrome instead of canonical components. Both files roll their own .qe-card + border-border/30..40 + rounded-md + bg-background/bg-muted/20 panels rather than using bloomberg-panel / inset-panel / metric-block / accent-card / stat-row from index.css. Inconsistent radius (rounded vs rounded-md vs rounded-md) and border opacities (/20, /30, /40). _(low)_; Brand cyan used as decorative chrome borderline-overload: signal-row left borders, status lines, 'ROI scenarios' summary link, 'Oracle Upgrade' label, balanced tier. Cyan-as-accent/brand is permitted, but the per-signal left border + label both in cyan reads as ambient accent; acceptable but should be tightened so cyan = interactive/brand only, not every signal row. _(low)_; Custom hover not on the signature pattern. SuggestionCard uses hover:brightness-110 transition (L396) and buttons use hover:bg-color/10; law's default hover is border-brighten + translateY(-1px) at 150-300ms on --ease-out-smooth. No press scale(0.98) declared locally (relies on global button:active). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the single-column max-w-4xl flow — it suits a paste-and-grade tool. Restructure the analyzer into canonical panels: (1) input row stays compact at top; (2) wrap the Greeks summary (Spot/Mid/Delta/Theta/IV) in a bloomberg-panel with metric-block cells so each number gets mono+tabular-nums and a micro uppercase label; (3) signal rows become accent-card--cyan rows with stat-row internals; (4) final composite stays a hero row but the synthesis text is sans-serif (words) with the GradePill as the only mono element; (5) trade plan = 4x metric-block grid; (6) ROI + suggestions unchanged structurally. Convert the H1 to sans 600. Move the page-level 'Supported formats' helper card into an inset-panel.

Rewrite GradePill to map grade->token: S=var(--grade-s), A=var(--grade-a), B=var(--grade-b), C=var(--grade-c), D=var(--grade-d), F=var(--grade-f), each as 15% bg + solid text + 30% border. Push-to-Trade-Desk and Analyze buttons: cyan brand CTA via border-[var(--brand-cyan)] / text + var(--brand-cyan) hover tint (Analyze is the primary CTA, keep cyan; Push is an action, also acceptable cyan or neutral — do NOT use emerald for chrome). Apply .data-cell to every number span (Spot/Mid/Greeks/IV/plan/ROI/strike/BE) for tabular-nums + slashed zero. Swap ad-hoc text-[Npx] for the fixed scale: labels -> text-[9px]/micro uppercase 500 tracking 0.06em muted; data -> text-[11px]/data-xs; body synthesis -> text-[13px]/data-base sans. Replace ✓/✗ with lucide <Check>/<X> (h-3 w-3) colored via trade tokens; replace the '> ' string prefix with a muted <span className="text-muted-foreground/40">&gt;</span> or a lucide ChevronRight. H1: drop font-mono, use font-sans font-semibold (keep the Terminal lucide icon in brand-cyan).

GradePill should read from --grade-* tokens. Reuse the global .qe-card only where a generic container is needed, but prefer bloomberg-panel for data.
- **Motion/3D:** No 3D opportunity here (GEX surface is the only sanctioned 3D moment). Standardize micro-interactions: signal rows and suggestion cards get the canonical hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) over 150-200ms on var(--ease-out-smooth), press scale(0.98) (already global). Replace SuggestionCard's hover:brightness-110. Optionally stagger the terminal statusLines in with framer-motion opacity fade (150ms each, respect prefers-reduced-motion) to keep the streaming-terminal feel without ambient glow. Remove any reliance on .shadcn-card teal glow hover (off-law) if it bleeds in.

#### chart-database  · M effort · 9 issues

Amber here encodes branding/decoration, not money. _(high)_; Hardcoded Tailwind palette colors instead of tokens. Raw cyan: text-cyan-400 (lines 102,139,257), bg-cyan-500/10 (104,138,257), border-cyan-500/30 (257). Raw amber: bg-amber-500/10 (75,93,148,209). Raw green/red: border-green-500/30 (251), bg-red-500/10 (126,253), border-red-500/30 (253). The single chromatic accent must be --brand-cyan via hsl(var(--token)); bull/bear must use --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish tokens incl. their -bg variants, never raw tailwind hues. _(high)_; Accent (cyan) used for non-accent semantic data. 'Still Open' count and the Win-Rate Target icon are cyan (lines 102,138-139), and the TRACKING badge is cyan (257). 'Open/tracking' is a trade status, not a brand/focus/link state. Cyan is the one accent reserved for focus/active/selection/links/CTA — using it for trade status conflates accent with semantics. Open/tracking should read as muted-neutral, and amber (--trade-neutral) is the legitimate token for an unresolved/neutral money outcome. _(med)_; stat-glass elevation off-ladder: it uses backdrop-filter blur(8px) + rgba(0,0,0,0.25) translucent fill + rgba(255,255,255,0.08) border (index.css 815-820) rather than the strict 4-step solid elevation ladder (L0 bg / L1 card / L2 secondary / L3 overlay-solid) with --border/--border-strong. Glass/blur depth is not the dark-first elevation model. _(med)_; Hero numbers not on the fixed type scale: stat values use Tailwind text-2xl font-bold (lines 91,102,113,124,135) and the section heading text-xl (194), the symbol text-lg (223). Law mandates fixed micro/data-xs..lg + price-sm/md/lg for hero numbers. Project ships .price-display-sm/md/lg utilities (index.css 998-1008) that are unused here. _(med)_; Density: page is comfortable/loose, not the compact Bloomberg default. Page pad p-6 (67), stat card p-4, grid gap-4, mb-8 section gaps (70,86), filter card p-6 (146), row cards p-6 (220). Law default is compact (card pad 8-10px, base 12px) driven by data-density; no density attribute is consumed anywhere in the project (grep for data-density returns nothing). _(med)_; font-mono numbers present but missing slashed-zero / lining-nums treatment used elsewhere. Cells use 'font-mono tabular-nums' inline but not the canonical .data-cell utility (index.css 838-846) which adds lnum + slashed-zero feature settings. Ticker (line 223) uses 'font-bold font-mono' but not the .ticker-font utility (uppercase + 0.04em tracking + 600). _(low)_; Header copy uses non-scale body sizes: subtitle text-base (line 80), h1 text-2xl sm:text-3xl (78) — generic Tailwind ramp rather than the fixed terminal scale; eyebrow uses tracking-wider rather than the specified 0.05–0.08em micro-label tracking. _(low)_; No motion signature. Row cards use hover-elevate (line 220) which is fine, but there is zero framer-motion entrance/stagger on the stat row or results list, and no press scale(0.98) on interactive cards. Law calls for 150–300ms ease-out-smooth micro-interactions. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 3-zone structure (header / 5-up stat strip / filters + results list) but tighten to compact density. Reduce page wrapper to p-4 and section gaps to mb-4/mb-5; stat grid gap to var(--card-gap) (10px); stat cards to p-3; filter and result cards to p-3/p-4. Convert the results 'cards' list into a denser Bloomberg-style table-like rows (28px-ish row rhythm) keeping the 6-column md grid but with data-cell mono columns. Header: drop the amber icon chip; keep a small lucide Database icon in muted-foreground next to the title, eyebrow as proper micro-label.
- **Apply:** COLOR/TOKENS: Replace every amber chrome usage with neutral chrome — eyebrow + header/Filter/empty icons -> text-muted-foreground (Database is not a money concept). Replace raw cyan with the accent token only where it is truly accent (none here are CTAs, so prefer neutral). Map trade states to semantic tokens: WIN badge -> bg-[--trade-bullish-bg] text-[--trade-bullish] border-[--trade-bullish]/30; LOSS -> bg-[--trade-bearish-bg] text-[--trade-bearish] border token; OPEN/TRACKING -> bg-[--trade-neutral-bg] text-[--trade-neutral] (unresolved money outcome) OR muted glass-subtle, NOT cyan; EXPIRED -> muted secondary. Adopt .data-cell and .ticker-font utilities from index.css. Wire a top-level data-density='compact' attribute so spacing tokens drive density.

ICONS (the headline fix): strip ALL emoji and the ↻/��/—/↓/▲/▼ glyphs; replace with lucide: Flame (fire/hot), TrendingUp (warm), Minus (cool), TrendingDown (frozen), ShieldAlert/Octagon (red); H1 Flame + 'POSITION HEAT MAP'; KPI Flame/Snowflake; ExpandableCard 'HEAT MAP'; Best/Worst Trophy/AlertTriangle; empty-state Target; Refresh RefreshCw (spin while isFetching); table direction ArrowUp/ArrowDown. Render heat as small lucide glyph + uppercase mono micro-label colored by the heat semantic token. CLEANUP: delete the display:none dead div.
- **Components:** componentStyles.card.default / card.accentBullish / card.accentBearish / button.compact (lib/design-tokens.ts) — all on-token, keep; ExpandableCard; CacheFreshnessIndicator; EngineStatusFooter; SkeletonLoader; lucide-react (Flame, Snowflake, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Minus, Trophy, AlertTriangle, Target, RefreshCw, ArrowUp, ArrowDown, ShieldAlert). Route numbers through the codebase's mono number primitive if one exists, else apply font-mono tabular-nums directly.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion default micro-interaction to HeatTile and BestWorstCard: whileHover border-brighten + translateY(-1px), whileTap scale(0.98), 150-200ms on ease-out-smooth, gated on prefers-reduced-motion. Spin RefreshCw via animate while isFetching. No ambient glow, no springs in-terminal. No R3F here — the gamma surface is GEX-only.

#### strategy-playbooks  · M effort · 9 issues
*Today:* A loosely-spaced marketing-style dashboard: a 3-col grid of AI-strategy cards with multicolored Tailwind icon tints and gradient hover washes, then a chart + crypto gauge row, a winners showcase, photo-gradient screener tiles, and an earnings section. Reads more like a SaaS landing grid than a Bloomberg-dense terminal, and almost no numbers are mono.

- **Violations:** Numbers are not mono. strategy-card.tsx win-rate/ann-return/Sharpe render as 'text-sm font-semibold' (sans, no tabular-nums); winners-showcase.tsx totalReturn and symbol render 'text-lg font-bold'/'text-base font-bold' (sans). Law requires IBM Plex Mono + tabular-nums lining-nums + slashed zero for EVERY number, ticker, %, Sharpe. _(high)_; Hardcoded non-token decorative colors. strategy-card iconColors in page: text-blue-500, text-purple-500, text-orange-500, text-cyan-500 (Tailwind palette, used as chrome). market-gauge.tsx: text-orange-500, text-blue-500 and raw hex gradient stops #ef4444/#f97316/#eab308/#22c55e/#10b981. earnings-card.tsx: bg-emerald-500/10, bg-red-500/10. _(high)_; Semantic money colors used as chrome. --trade-bullish applied as the icon tint for 'AI Stock Picker' and --trade-neutral for 'Day Trading' icons; these encode brand/category, not money or direction. Law: bullish/bearish/neutral encode ONLY money/direction. _(high)_; Foundation token drift (global, affects this surface): --font-sans is 'DM Sans' and --font-mono is 'JetBrains Mono'/'Space Mono' in index.css, not IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono. ::selection and many helpers hardcode teal rgba(20,184,166,...) as generic chrome. _(high)_; The ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan #22d3ee) is absent. Icon container, 'View Signals' CTA, and header Sparkles icon use text-primary / bg-primary/10 (neutral). Focus/active/links/primary-CTA should be --brand-cyan. text-cyan-500 (futures icon) is the wrong cyan, used decoratively. _(med)_; Decorative gradient/glow overlays. strategy-card has 'bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/5' hover wash; screener-card uses full-card photo-style gradients + 'bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80'. Law bans ambient/decorative glow; depth is bg-step + border only. _(med)_; No data-density support and non-compact spacing. p-5 strategy cards, p-6 main, space-y-8 sections, h-36 screener tiles — far looser than Bloomberg-dense default (card pad 8-10px). No density attribute consumed anywhere in the app. _(med)_; Radius inconsistency. screener-card and Skeleton placeholders use rounded-xl; strategy-card uses Card default; law calls for --radius-card 10px (0.625rem). _(low)_; Micro-labels not to spec. 'Win Rate'/'Ann. Return'/'Sharpe' are plain text-xs text-muted-foreground (not UPPERCASE 500 tracking 0.05-0.08em). Section headers use a literal '>' glyph as chrome instead of a lucide icon / section-chrome treatment. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the vertical section stack (StrategyGrid > Market Monitor + CryptoMarketSection > WinnersShowcase > FeaturedScreeners > EarningsPrediction) but tighten to terminal density: main p-4 (not p-6), section spacing via --section-gap, card grids gap-[var(--card-gap)] (10px). Replace ad-hoc 'Heading >' rows with one canonical section-chrome header (mono uppercase 0.1em label + hairline rule) per section. Compact strategy-card to p-[10px] with the 3 perf stats as a 3-up metric-block row.
- **Apply:** NUMBERS/FONT: wrap every numeric + ticker in mono — apply 'data-cell' (font-mono tabular-nums lining-nums) to win-rate/ann-return/Sharpe in strategy-card.tsx, to totalReturn (use change-indicator--up/down) and symbol (ticker-font) in winners-showcase.tsx, symbolCount in screener-card.tsx, EPS/probability in earnings-card.tsx, gauge value in market-gauge.tsx. COLORS: delete text-blue-500/text-purple-500/text-orange-500/text-cyan-500 icon tints — strategy icons go neutral text-muted-foreground, hover -> text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]; reserve --trade-bullish/bearish/neutral strictly for % return/win values. Replace raw hex gauge stops and text-orange-500/text-blue-500 with hsl(var(--trade-*)) tokens. earnings-card: bg-emerald-500/10 -> var(--trade-bullish-bg), bg-red-500/10 -> var(--trade-bearish-bg). ACCENT: icon container bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.1)], 'View Signals' CTA + header Sparkles icon text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]. CHROME: remove from-primary/5 wash and screener photo gradients; use --card bg + --border, hover -> --border-strong. RADIUS: standardize tiles/cards/skeletons to rounded-[var(--radius-card)]. MICRO-LABELS: stat labels uppercase font-medium tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground 11px.
- **Components:** Use canonical Card on the --card/--border elevation ladder instead of bespoke gradient cards; consume the already-defined index.css utilities (bloomberg-panel, section-chrome, metric-block, change-indicator, ticker-font, data-cell) instead of inline styles; Badge for STOCKS/OPTIONS/CRYPTO tags as neutral secondary (not colored). Replace screener-card's photo-gradient hero with a flat data tile: sentiment as a single accent-card--bullish/bearish left stripe, count in mono.
- **Motion/3D:** Standardize on the law's micro-interaction: framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth, hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98). Drop the bespoke opacity-gradient hover and group-hover scale-105 on screener images. Respect prefers-reduced-motion. No new 3D — the R3F GEX surface is the only signature 3D moment; this page must not add ambient effects.

#### btc-radar  · M effort · 7 issues

- **Violations:** Third chromatic accent: GOLD = var(--brand-gold) (#f59e0b) is used as chrome/identity/decoration — the Bitcoin header icon (line 135), low-beta indicator in beta table (line 325), divergence-Z highlight (line 335), and the C-grade pill (line 348). Law allows EXACTLY ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan); amber/#f59e0b is demoted to --trade-neutral and reserved ONLY for money/direction. The Bitcoin icon and low-beta tag are decorative/categorical, not directional money — they must not use the amber accent. _(high)_; Emoji used as status icons in JS strings: '✓ Pushed …' (line 113) and '✗ Push failed' (line 115). Law: lucide icons only, no emoji. Should render a lucide Check / X (e.g. CheckCircle2 / XCircle) instead of glyphs in the result string. _(high)_; No motion signature. All hover states are plain CSS transition-colors (buttons line 147/155, table rows line 319). Law's default micro-interaction is framer-motion 150–300ms on --ease-out-smooth: hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press = scale(0.98). The page has zero framer-motion despite 8 sibling pages using it. _(med)_; Arbitrary font sizes instead of the fixed type scale. Uses text-[9px], text-[10px], text-[11px], text-2xl, text-base, text-sm throughout (lines 123,137,138,147,176,204,205,206,212,215,228,230,257,277,279,300,309,319). Law defines micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 + price-sm/md/lg tokens. (Note: these scale utilities are not yet defined in index.css, so this is partly a systemic gap, but the page should consume named classes once they exist; the hero BTC price at text-2xl should be a price-* token, not a generic Tailwind size.) _(med)_; data-density not consumed. Padding/row height are hardcoded (card p-4 line 199, p-3 line 270, rows py-2 lines 309/319). Law: compact is default and ALL spacing flows from the data-density attribute (compact/comfortable/spacious). The page ignores density entirely. _(med)_; Inconsistent border-radius vocabulary: cards use rounded-lg (lines 199,270,308), buttons rounded-md (lines 147,155,165), pills rounded / rounded (lines 233,243,352). Mixed radii without a token reference — law wants a consistent radius scale from tokens. _(low)_; Global font token mismatch (informational, not a page-level fault): --font-mono resolves to JetBrains Mono and there is no IBM Plex Sans/Mono in index.css, but the law mandates IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono. The page correctly consumes font-mono, so the fix belongs in index.css, not this file. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong existing 4-section structure (sub-header+actions, BTC state card, active signals, beta watchlist). It is already a clean Bloomberg-dense cockpit consistent with HuntShell. Changes are surgical, not structural: (1) Header row — keep the grid-cols-2/md:cols-5 BTC state card and the [1.4fr_repeat(6,1fr)] beta table grid (good). (2) Convert the pushResult banner into a token-driven inline status strip with a leading lucide icon. (3) Drive all padding/row-height off data-density rather than fixed p-4/p-3/py-2.
- **Apply:** COLOR: Remove the GOLD constant entirely. Replace each brand-gold use by intent — Bitcoin header icon (line 135) → neutral chrome: text-muted-foreground or text-foreground (it is brand identity, not money, so NOT an accent); the low-beta indicator (line 325) and divergence-Z highlight (line 335) → these ARE directional/money signals, so map them to var(--trade-neutral) (legal, since amber=neutral encodes money) and emphasize via font-weight 600 + the neutral token, not brand-gold; C-grade pill (line 348) → var(--trade-neutral). Keep BULL/BEAR = --trade-bullish/--trade-bearish (correct, money/direction). Keep B-grade pill on var(--brand-cyan) (correct single accent). Surfaces/borders already token-correct (bg-card, border-card-border, border-border/*) — leave them. ICONS: replace ✓/✗ in pushResult strings with a state enum {ok|err|null} and render a lucide CheckCircle2 (text-[var(--trade-bullish)]) or XCircle (text-[var(--trade-bearish)]) beside the message — never glyphs in the string. TYPE: swap arbitrary text-[Npx] for the fixed scale: micro-labels (the 9px UPPERCASE tracking-widest labels at 204/215/230/257/309) → a 'micro' class (UPPERCASE sans 500, tracking 0.05–0.08em, muted); data rows (11px) → data-xs; 10px secondary → data-xs; BTC hero price (text-2xl, line 205) → price-lg/price-md token. Every number already uses font-mono + tabular-nums (good) — also add slashed-zero/lining-nums via the mono utility. DENSITY: read data-density and map card pad 8–10px / row 28px in compact; spacing-only, never touch color/elevation. COMPONENTS: reuse canonical primitives — the BTCStateCard and SignalCard should be built on client/src/components/ui/card.tsx (or stat-card.tsx for the Metric tiles); the GradePill should reuse ui/badge.tsx; the action buttons should use ui/button.tsx variants (ghost for Run scan, a bullish-tinted variant for Push) instead of bespoke <button> className blobs. Keep TickerLogo (already canonical). MOTION: add framer-motion — wrap cards/rows in motion.div with whileHover={{ y: -1 }} + border-brighten and whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}, transition 150–200ms ease-out-smooth, all gated on prefers-reduced-motion. RefreshCw/Send spin/pulse states are fine. No ambient glow. 3D: none warranted here — the single R3F gamma surface belongs to GEX, not BTC radar; do not add decorative 3D.
- **Components:** Reuse: ui/card.tsx (state + signal cards), ui/stat-card.tsx (the 5 Metric tiles), ui/badge.tsx (GradePill), ui/button.tsx (Run scan / Push actions), ui/table.tsx pattern for the beta watchlist, components/hunt/cockpit/ticker-logo.tsx (keep). Mirror signal-row.tsx for the SignalCard hover/selection treatment (border-brighten + color-mix tint) since it is the established cockpit pattern.
- **Motion/3D:** framer-motion micro-interactions only: card/row hover translateY(-1px)+border-brighten, press scale(0.98), 150–200ms on --ease-out-smooth, prefers-reduced-motion respected. No decorative glow, no springs, no Aceternity. No R3F here (GEX owns the one 3D moment).

#### movers  · S effort · 7 issues

- **Violations:** Hand-rolled card chrome instead of canonical primitives. The header strip (line 91: rounded-lg border border-card-border bg-foreground/[0.03]), each gainers/losers column (line 164: rounded-lg border border-card-border bg-card p-2), and the empty/error states all rebuild card surfaces by hand. The codebase mandates QECard/QESection ('stop hand-rolling bg-zinc border rounded-lg') and these are unused here. _(med)_; Glyph-as-icon for the error state: '✗ {message}' (line 127) uses a Unicode glyph as an icon. Law says lucide only, no emoji/glyph icons. Should be a lucide XCircle/AlertTriangle. _(med)_; Raw font sizes below / off the fixed type scale. Pervasive text-[10px] (lines 92,99-103,108,168) and text-[9px] (line 193) — the scale defines micro 9px / data-xs 11px / data-sm 12px / data-base 13px. 10px is not a scale step; these should map to micro (9px) or data-xs (11px) tokens, and ideally use semantic utilities rather than arbitrary bracket values. _(low)_; Inline style={{ color: BULL/BEAR }} and color-mix in JS strings (lines 100,101,125,144,145,167,190) push token values through React inline styles instead of using token-backed classes (text-[var(--trade-bullish)]) or canonical components (QEStat tone='bull'/'bear'). Functionally token-sourced, but bypasses the class-string API and the MoverList color prop is just plumbing for these two semantic tokens. _(low)_; Direction glyphs ▲/▼ in the summary (lines 100-101). Note: this mirrors the canonical QEStat delta which also uses ▲/▼, so it is consistent with house pattern, but if the law is read strictly as 'lucide only', these are glyph icons and could be TrendingUp/TrendingDown (already imported). _(low)_; Font-family mismatch is environmental, not file-local: index.css defines --font-sans: 'DM Sans' and --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', while the design law mandates IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono. movers.tsx correctly consumes font-mono/font-sans tokens, so the page is compliant, but the underlying tokens violate the law. Flagging so it isn't fixed at the page level. _(low)_; Hover affordance on rows is border + bg only (line 181: hover:bg-foreground/[0.04] hover:border-border/20). Law's default hover signature is border-brighten + translateY(-1px); rows get no lift. Minor — dense list rows arguably shouldn't lift, but the press scale(0.98) / lift signature is absent on these primary click targets. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing structure — it is already correct and dense: QETabs (SESSION prefix) on top, a thin scan-status header strip, then a 2-up responsive grid (md:grid-cols-2) of GAINERS / LOSERS lists with TickerLogo + ticker + price + %change + sector rows. No layout overhaul needed. Refinements only: (1) wrap the header strip in QECard variant='glass' padding='sm'; (2) wrap each gainers/losers column in QESection (title='GAINERS'/'LOSERS', action={count}, variant='accent-bull'/'accent-bear') so the left-accent bar encodes direction via the accent-card system instead of a colored icon+label; (3) make the empty + 'no significant gappers' + error states QECard variant='inset'/'glass' instead of hand-rolled borders. Keep row height ~28px compact.
- **Apply:** Replace hand-rolled card divs with QECard/QESection from '@/components/ui/qe'. Use QESection variant='accent-bull' / 'accent-bear' for the two columns (drives the direction color through .accent-card--bullish/--bearish left stripe + token colors, removing the need for the `color` prop plumbing and inline style on the title). For the scan summary, swap the ▲/▼ spans for a compact QEStatStrip or inline TrendingUp/TrendingDown lucide icons (already imported) sized h-3 w-3 with text-[var(--trade-bullish)]/[var(--trade-bearish)]. Replace the '✗' error glyph with a lucide icon (XCircle, h-3.5 w-3.5) and render the error block as QECard variant='glass' with text-[var(--trade-bearish)] — drop the color-mix inline style in favor of trade-bearish-bg token. Normalize arbitrary text sizes: text-[10px]→micro/data-xs scale (9px or 11px), text-[9px]→micro (9px); prefer the existing micro-label pattern (uppercase mono 500, tracking-widest, muted) already used on line 93. Replace inline style={{color: BULL/BEAR}} on the %change/price cells with className text-[var(--trade-bullish)]/[var(--trade-bearish)] driven by direction. Numbers already use font-mono tabular-nums — keep, and add slashed-zero/lining-nums via the .data-cell utility if tightening.
- **Components:** QECard (variant glass/inset), QESection (variant accent-bull / accent-bear for the two columns), QEStatStrip/QEStat (optional, for the scan-summary up/down/total counts), QETabs (already used — no change), TickerLogo (already used — no change). Pull all from '@/components/ui/qe'.
- **Motion/3D:** Low-stakes surface — no 3D moment. Add the law's default micro-interaction to the row Links: framer-motion or CSS translateY(-1px) + border-brighten on hover (150–200ms, --ease-out-smooth) and scale(0.98) on press, respecting prefers-reduced-motion. The existing transition-colors is fine but under-delivers the house hover signature. Optionally apply the .price-tick-up/.price-tick-down flash utility (already in index.css) when a mover's %change updates on the 60s refresh — a tasteful, on-brand live-data cue. No ambient glow.
