#### olalgo  · M effort · 1 issues
*Today:* A dense, Bloomberg-style backtest dashboard: 4-card challenge tier grid, sticky section-nav pills, comparison table, weekly-diagnosis table, OlAlgoMax panel, and a best-run replay with equity sparkline + trade journal. Structurally it is one of the cleaner pages — it correctly uses the canonical QEPageShell/QEPageHeader/QESectionDivider, consumes CSS tokens throughout (zero raw hex, zero Tailwind palette colors, zero emoji), and renders every number in mono with tabular-nums. The dominant problem is purely chromatic: the entire page is themed in brand TEAL rather than the one sanctioned cyan accent, and it leaks GEX-only colors into a non-GEX surface.

- **Violations:** placeholder _(low)_
- **Layout:** Layout is sound — keep the structure: QEPageShell width=wide / sticky SectionNav / 4 sections (challenge grid, OlAlgoMax, weekly, replay). No grid or hierarchy changes needed. This is a color/token + motion refactor, not a relayout. Two small layout polish items: (1) give EquitySpark a faint --border baseline grid and a min/max axis label row already present; (2) normalize all card padding to flow from data-density rather than hardcoded p-3.
- **Apply:** 1) ACCENT SWAP — global find/replace --brand-teal -> --brand-cyan across all 29 occurrences (nav active pill bg/border/text, RunsControl active pill, ChallengeCard label+border+active ring, LOCKED VAULT card + comparison Vault column, weekly toggles, OlAlgoMax title+border+CTA, header Bot icon, TradeJournal link, both CTAs). 2) Header icon: change icon={<Bot className='text-[var(--brand-teal)]'/>} iconAccent='ai' to plain Bot (no inline color) and keep iconAccent='cyan' so the canonical QEPageHeader gradient handles it. 3) GEX-leak fix — replace every --gex-flip with --trade-neutral and the lone --gex-positive (comparison Hit-10x) with --brand-cyan or --trade-bullish per meaning (10x is an achievement metric, not money-positive -> use --brand-cyan/foreground, not a GEX color). 4) CTAs: drop hardcoded text-white -> use the Button default variant (size='sm' with no bg override) which already pairs --primary + --primary-foreground; the explicit bg-[var(--brand-teal)] override should be removed entirely so it inherits the canonical primary CTA. 5) TYPOGRAPHY — introduce/consume the fixed-scale utilities: text-[8px]->text-[9px] (micro floor), map data labels to data-xs(11)/data-sm(12)/data-base(13), hero stat numbers (BigStat value text-lg, median totals) -> price-sm/price-md classes; replace raw text-base/text-sm/text-lg with the named data/price classes. 6) Re-case micro-labels to UPPERCASE sans 500 w/ tracking 0.05–0.08em (they're currently mono uppercase); keep numbers mono. Canonical components to lean on: keep QEPageShell/QEPageHeader/QESectionDivider; consider extracting the repeated Stat/BigStat 'label + mono value + sub' block into the shared stat-card primitive if one exists in components/ui so tone->token mapping lives in one place.
- **Components:** Reuse QEPageShell, QEPageHeader (iconAccent='cyan'), QESectionDivider, Button (default primary variant, no bg override). Consolidate the four near-identical stat blocks (Stat, BigStat, the inline vault/BP cards, OlAlgoMax headline cards) into one tokenized StatCard with a tone enum mapping ONLY to {bullish,bearish,neutral,brand} -> the four sanctioned tokens, eliminating the gex-flip/gex-positive leak at the source.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion default micro-interaction to ChallengeCard, SectionNav pills, weekly/runs toggles and table rows: 150–300ms on --ease-out-smooth, hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), tap = scale(0.98), all gated on prefers-reduced-motion. Animate EquitySpark with a polyline pathLength draw-on (strokeDashoffset 0->1 over ~400ms) on mount, reduced-motion = instant. No ambient glow, no springs (landing-only). The challenge cards' active selection could use a subtle layout/border transition rather than the hard ring-2.

#### performance  · M effort · 0 issues
*Today:* A max-w-5xl analytics page: a compact header with three Select filters + export, a "Trust Score" hero (grade circle + hit-rate/avg-gain/bot-P&L) from UserPerformanceSummary, a 4-engine win-rate grid, and a collapsible "Advanced Analytics" block (6-tab TierGate with lazy charts, accordions, a data-integrity audit table, broker import). Structurally clean and reasonably dense, but visually it leaks raw Tailwind palette colors (blue/purple/cyan/emerald/red/amber-500) and a decorative cyan→purple gradient glow that break the single-accent + token-only laws.

- **Layout:** Keep the existing IA (header filters → Trust hero → engine grid → collapsible Advanced tabs → RiskDisclosure); it is sound and already compact. Tighten only: (1) make the engine grid and hero share one elevation story — hero = Card L1, engine cards = Card L1 with --border, audit/reconciliation insets = bg-secondary L2 (no colored backgrounds). (2) Replace the colored left/top semantic backgrounds on the methodology, reconciliation, and disclosure panels with neutral inset-panel (.inset-panel already exists) and reserve color strictly for the inline win/loss icons and money figures.
- **Apply:** COLOR: Remove all raw palette classes. ENGINE_CONFIG: drop per-engine hue identity — set every engine icon to text-muted-foreground with bg-secondary chip, OR if differentiation is wanted, use lucide icon shape alone (no color). Quant must NOT be blue, AI must NOT be purple. getGrade(): A/A+ → text-[var(--trade-bullish)] bg with /10 via [color:var(--trade-bullish)]; B → text-[var(--brand-cyan)] bg [color:var(--brand-cyan)]/10; C → text-[var(--trade-neutral)]; D → text-[var(--trade-neutral)] (collapse orange into neutral, no orange token exists); F → text-[var(--trade-bearish)]. Replace text-cyan-400 → text-[var(--brand-cyan)] and bg-cyan-500/10 → bg-[color:var(--brand-cyan)]/10. Replace bg-emerald-500/* , bg-red-500/* , bg-amber-500/* , border-green-500/* , border-red-500/* , border-amber-500/* , border-emerald-500/* with the matching --trade-* token via arbitrary value (e.g. border-[color:var(--trade-bullish)]/30, bg-[color:var(--trade-bullish-bg)]). SEMANTICS: change Total Ideas number from text-[var(--trade-bullish)] to text-foreground (it is a count, not money); change Database/TrendingUp icons and section headings from trade-bullish to text-muted-foreground or text-[var(--brand-cyan)] (brand chrome). GLOW: delete the `bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/5 … to-purple-500/5` div entirely; if elevation is desired use the Card border + bg-card only. RADIUS: hero grade circle rounded-2xl → rounded-[var(--radius-card)] (or rounded-lg) to match. COMPONENTS: replace both hand-rolled group-hover tooltips with the canonical Tooltip/TooltipTrigger/TooltipContent primitive; keep using Card/CardContent, Badge, Tabs, Accordion, Select (already canonical). NUMBERS: ensure every figure carries font-mono + tabular-nums (most already do via font-mono; add tabular-nums to the engine n= counts and audit actual/expected cells). MOTION: keep animate-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200 on the Advanced reveal (within 150–300ms law) but verify it respects prefers-reduced-motion; standardize card hover to the global border-brighten + translateY(-1px) (hover-elevate is already on engine cards — extend to hero/audit cards).
- **Components:** Reuse: Card/CardContent/CardHeader/CardTitle, Badge, Button, Tabs, Accordion, Select, Skeleton (all already imported and canonical). Adopt: Tooltip primitive (replace 2 hand-rolled tooltips). Consider promoting the repeated stat block (label + mono value + delta) into a shared StatTile/metric-block component — the .metric-block CSS class already exists in index.css and is unused here; use it for the hero quick-stats and engine grid to guarantee consistent mono sizing.
- **Motion/3D:** No new 3D — this surface is not the GEX gamma-surface moment. Only micro-interaction cleanup: unify card hover to border-brighten + translateY(-1px) at 150–300ms ease-out-smooth, press scale(0.98), and gate the Advanced-reveal slide on prefers-reduced-motion. Remove the decorative gradient (anti-glow). Optional: a 150ms count-up on the hero hit-rate number when filters change, reduced-motion-safe.

### P2 — legal / auth / rarely-seen  (18)

#### command  · L effort · 13 issues

- **Violations:** Pervasive raw Tailwind palette colors instead of design tokens — ~111 slate-*, 29 emerald-*, 31 red-*, 25 amber-*, 14 cyan-*, plus orange-* and purple-*. Page background is bg-slate-950, text text-slate-100, every panel uses bg-slate-900/*, borders border-slate-700/50 / border-slate-800/50. Law requires hsl(var(--token)) / named tokens only, never hardcoded palette hues. _(high)_; Semantic money/direction colors hardcoded as emerald-400 / red-400 / amber-400 (DIR_CONFIG object lines 178-182, plus VIX/PCR/flow/probability bars) instead of --trade-bullish / --trade-bearish / --trade-neutral tokens. The semantic meaning is correct (direction/money) but the values are raw, so they will not track theme or the canonical green #10b981 / red #ef4444 / amber #f59e0b. _(high)_; Accent color is raw cyan-500/cyan-400 (active index button, timeframe active, Brain/Layers/Activity icons, GEX-anchor card) rather than --brand-cyan token. Correct accent semantics, wrong source. _(high)_; Neutral/amber misuse: amber-400 used for chrome/decoration (Next-catalyst calendar icon line 392, EM Zap icon, GEX gamma-flip card, data-freshness badge, catalyst timeline header icon) — amber is reserved by law for trade-neutral money/direction only, not generic warnings or chrome accents. _(med)_; Typography family is off-spec: index.css defines --font-sans as DM Sans and --font-mono as JetBrains Mono; law mandates IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono. No IBM Plex anywhere (grep = 0). _(med)_; Numbers use font-mono but not the fixed data scale or tabular tokens — sizes are ad-hoc text-xs/text-sm/text-2xl/text-[10px]/text-[9px]/text-[11px] rather than micro/data-xs/data-sm/data-base/data-lg + price-sm/md/lg. Hero price ($spot) is text-2xl font-black font-mono (line 442) instead of a price-display-lg class; no guaranteed tabular-nums/slashed-zero on most numeric spans (page relies on bare font-mono). _(med)_; font-black used for direction label and hero price (lines 438, 442) — outside the allowed sans weight set 400/500/600/700; mono caps at 600. _(med)_; Emoji/unicode-as-icon: VIX change uses literal ▲/▼ triangles (line 357) instead of lucide ArrowUp/ArrowDown — law: lucide only, no emoji/glyph icons. (ArrowUpRight/ArrowDownRight are already imported but unused.) _(med)_; Hand-rolled panels everywhere — no use of canonical QEPanel/QEPanelHeader/QEPanelContent (components/ui/qe-panel.tsx), SignalCard (components/signal-card/), stat-card/price-card. Each of the 4 sub-panels reimplements header chrome and surfaces inline, duplicating the elevation ladder by hand with slate opacities. _(med)_; Elevation expressed via translucent slate overlays (bg-slate-900/80, /40, /30, /20, /60) rather than the 4-step solid ladder L0 background → L1 card → L2 secondary → L3 surface-overlay-solid with --border/--border-strong. Depth is opacity-hacked, not token-driven. _(med)_; Light mode effectively unsupported — page is hardcoded dark (bg-slate-950 / text-slate-100 with no dark: prefixing and no token fallback), so it will not invert under :root light. _(med)_; Inconsistent radius: page mixes rounded-md, rounded-lg, rounded, rounded-full and one rounded-xl (direction hero, line 432) and a border-2 — no use of --radius-card/--radius-button/--radius-badge ladder. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions; transitions are transition-all / transition-colors only. No border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover or scale(0.98) press on the index/timeframe buttons or panel cards (the global CSS layer covers some of this but the page opts into ad-hoc transition-all). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong existing information architecture — it is genuinely good and dense: Index Strip (SPY/QQQ/IWM + VIX + PCR + γ-regime + next-catalyst) → Timeframe bar → 8/4 split of GEX/VEX chart + Outlook panel → 3-up bottom row (Signals 4 / Catalyst Timeline 4 / Trade Setups 4) → Accuracy footer. No structural change needed. Standardize the outer container to bg-background text-foreground (drop bg-slate-950/text-slate-100). Drive the whole page with data-density='compact' so the 28px row / 8-10px pad targets are explicit rather than ad-hoc py-1.5/py-2.5. Wrap each of the 4 sub-panels in QEPanel + QEPanelHeader (title + lucide icon + right-aligned stat) + QEPanelContent so headers/surfaces stop being hand-rolled.
- **Apply:** COLOR: bg-slate-950→bg-background; text-slate-100→text-foreground; panel bg-slate-900/* → bg-card / bg-secondary / surface-overlay-solid per elevation level; border-slate-700|800/* → border-border (or border-strong for primary dividers); muted text text-slate-400|500|600 → text-muted-foreground. SEMANTIC: rewrite DIR_CONFIG to text-[--trade-bullish]/bg-[--trade-bullish-bg], text-[--trade-bearish]/..., text-[--trade-neutral]/... (or Tailwind tokens trade-bullish/bearish/neutral); recolor VIX/PCR/probability bars/flow bar/grade styles to the same trade-* tokens. ACCENT: all cyan-400|500 → brand-cyan token (text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] / the configured `cyan` token) for active index/timeframe, Brain/Layers/Activity icons, GEX-anchor card, and the countdown highlight. CHROME: demote amber chrome (calendar icon, data-freshness badge, EM Zap, section icons) to text-muted-foreground or brand-cyan — reserve amber strictly for trade-neutral. TYPE: swap --font-sans→IBM Plex Sans and --font-mono→IBM Plex Mono in index.css :root/.dark; give every numeric span the .data-cell / price-display-* utilities (already defined in index.css with tabular-nums + slashed-zero); map ad-hoc sizes to the fixed scale (text-[9px]/[10px]→micro, text-xs→data-xs, text-sm→data-sm/base, hero $spot→price-display-lg). WEIGHTS: font-black→font-bold (700). MICRO-LABELS: the many uppercase tracking-wider labels → standardize on the section-label / panel-title classes (uppercase sans 500, tracking 0.05-0.08em, muted-foreground). RADIUS: rounded-xl on the direction hero→rounded-[var(--radius-card)]; normalize buttons to --radius-button, badges to --radius-badge.

For the Signals list, adopt components/signal-card/ (SignalCard + ConfidenceGauge) for the correlation rows and alignment meter. Keep GEXVEXChart (components/gex-vex-chart.tsx) as-is; it is the canonical chart. Replace ▲/▼ with lucide ArrowUp/ArrowDown (already-imported ArrowUpRight/ArrowDownRight can serve).
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion to the index-symbol buttons and timeframe pills: whileHover { y:-1, borderColor brighten } / whileTap { scale:0.98 } at 150ms on --ease-out-smooth, gated on prefers-reduced-motion. Apply the same hover-lift to the 4 QEPanel cards via the existing .shadcn-card pattern. Keep the probability/flow/horizon bars but animate width with the smooth ease (already partially done via duration-500). No ambient glow. This page already hosts the signature 3D moment indirectly through GEXVEXChart — if that component is the R3F gamma surface, leave its lazy-load + 2D fallback untouched; do not add a second 3D element here.

#### blog  · M effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** Rainbow of hardcoded chromatic Tailwind colors throughout — CATEGORY_VISUALS gradients use from-blue-600/cyan-500/teal-400, purple-600/violet-500/pink-400, emerald-600/green-500/lime-400, orange-600/amber-500/yellow-400, red-600/rose-500/pink-400, cyan-600/blue-500/indigo-400; TopicsSection adds from-blue-500/cyan-500, green-500/emerald-500, purple-500/pink-500, orange-500/amber-500. Law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan); this introduces ~15 distinct hues. Direct, severe violation. _(high)_; Raw rgba() hex values hardcoded in CATEGORY_VISUALS.pattern radial-gradients (e.g. rgba(59,130,246,0.3), rgba(20,184,166,0.3), rgba(147,51,234,0.3), rgba(220,38,38,0.3)). Law forbids any hardcoded rgb/hex in components — must consume hsl(var(--token)). _(high)_; Accent uses literal cyan-500/cyan-400 (bg-cyan-500 active tab, text-cyan-400 hover, text-cyan-500 links, from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 icon chips) instead of the --brand-cyan / brand-cyan utility token. Even the 'correct' accent color is hardcoded to the wrong Tailwind shade (#06b6d4/cyan-500 vs token #22d3ee). _(high)_; Decorative ambient glow explicitly banned: 'blur-3xl bg-white/20 rounded-full scale-150' behind the featured icon (line 216) and 'opacity-80'/'opacity-70' full-card gradient washes. Law: NO ambient/decorative glow; depth via bg-step + border, not glow. _(high)_; Hero background decoration uses hardcoded rgba cyan/purple gradient wash (from-cyan-500/10 via-transparent to-purple-500/10) plus a hardcoded rgba(6,182,212,0.03) grid-line pattern. Purple is not an allowed accent anywhere; both bypass tokens. _(high)_; Numbers rendered in sans, not IBM Plex Mono. Read-time ('{readTime} min read', '{readTime} min'), and dates (format(...,'MMM d, yyyy')) are plain text with no font-mono / tabular-nums. Every number must be mono. _(med)_; Teal used outside its sanctioned scope. Law demotes teal (#14b8a6) to logo stripe + GEX surface ONLY; here teal-400 and rgba(20,184,166) appear in the 'education' category visual. _(med)_; Semantic trade colors misused as generic decoration in places: trade-bullish TrendingUp + trade-neutral DollarSign in the newsletter bullet list (lines 412/420) encode no money/direction — they decorate a marketing list. Conversely text-blue-400 on a BookOpen bullet is an arbitrary hue. Semantic tokens must encode ONLY money/direction. _(med)_; White-on-gradient text (text-white, text-white/90, text-white/80, bg-white/20 badge) hardcodes #fff instead of token foreground; only works because of the (illegal) colored gradient backdrops. _(med)_; Micro-label tracking uses tracking-wider (0.025em) vs law's 0.05–0.08em for UPPERCASE 500 micro-labels (lines 108, 335, 397). Font-size 10px/text-xs labels are close to scale but not on the named micro/data-xs tokens. _(low)_; Container width inconsistency: hero uses max-w-[1600px] while the body grid uses max-w-7xl — arbitrary one-off max-width literal and mismatched content rails. _(low)_; Hover relies on CSS-class hover-elevate + ad-hoc group-hover:scale-110 image zoom and group-hover:gap-2 rather than the framer-motion border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover / scale(0.98) press signature. No prefers-reduced-motion handling on the scale transforms. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing structure (hero band + category tabs, one featured 2-col card, 3-col article grid, 4-up topics, newsletter CTA) — it is sound. Unify content rail: drop max-w-[1600px], use a single max-w-7xl (or max-w-6xl) container for both hero and body so the rails align. Tighten to compact density: hero py-6, card padding via canonical Card defaults (p-4), grid gap to var(--card-gap) (10px) instead of gap-6. Replace the per-category colored 'visual' image panel with a neutral L1/L2 surface (bg-card / bg-secondary) carrying a single lucide category icon in muted foreground + a thin brand-cyan top accent rule; the differentiation between categories should come from the icon + label, not color.
- **Apply:** Delete CATEGORY_VISUALS gradient/pattern map and TopicsSection color array entirely. Replace ALL chromatic literals: text-cyan-400/cyan-500 -> text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] (or a brand-cyan utility); from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 icon chips -> bg-secondary with the icon in text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]; all from-*/via-*/to-* category gradients -> removed (neutral surfaces). Hero wash + grid pattern -> remove or replace with a single low-opacity hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.04) accent if any decoration is kept. Remove the blur-3xl bg-white/20 glow. Wrap every number (readTime, dates) in font-mono with tabular-nums lining-nums (use the data-sm/data-xs scale): e.g. <span className="font-mono text-[length:var(--data-xs)] tabular-nums">. Fix micro-labels to tracking-[0.06em] uppercase font-medium text-muted-foreground. In NewsletterCTA, make all three bullet icons neutral (text-muted-foreground) — remove trade-bullish/trade-neutral/blue-400; reserve semantic trade tokens for actual P/L only. Replace text-white/* on the (now removed) gradients with text-foreground / text-card-foreground. Card border accent for category can be a 2px top border in brand-cyan only on hover, never per-category color.
- **Components:** Use canonical <Card> (rounded-[var(--radius-card)], bg-card, border-card-border) instead of the glass-card class for in-app consistency; keep Badge/Button/Skeleton. Featured + article cards become neutral surface cards. Category tab Buttons: active = variant=default (which should map to brand-cyan primary) — remove the literal className="bg-cyan-500 text-foreground".
- **Motion/3D:** No 3D opportunity here (GEX surface is the only sanctioned 3D moment). Replace ad-hoc group-hover:scale-110 + hover-elevate with the standard framer-motion card hover (border-brighten + translateY(-1px), 150–300ms on --ease-out-smooth, press scale(0.98)); the 'Read more' arrow nudge (group-hover:gap-2) is fine but should respect prefers-reduced-motion. All transforms gated on prefers-reduced-motion.

#### blog-post  · L effort · 12 issues

- **Violations:** Rainbow accent explosion — the law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan) plus the three money-only semantics. This page uses dozens of multi-stop Tailwind gradients across the full spectrum: from-blue-600/cyan-500/teal-400, from-purple-600/violet-500/pink-400, from-emerald-600/green-500/lime-400, from-orange-600/amber-500/yellow-400, from-red-600/rose-500/pink-400, from-cyan-600/blue-500/indigo-400 (CATEGORY_VISUALS L21-52), plus per-card gradients like from-purple-500 to-violet-600, from-pink-500 to-rose-600, from-orange-500 to-red-600 throughout. None are tokens. _(high)_; Hardcoded raw rgba/hex colors in inline styles and arbitrary classes: the CATEGORY_VISUALS radial-gradient patterns use rgba(59,130,246,.3), rgba(147,51,234,.3), rgba(234,88,12,.3) etc (L25-51); the hero grid bg uses bg-[linear-gradient(rgba(6,182,212,0.03)...)] (L81, L711). Law forbids hardcoded hex/rgb in components. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors used as decoration/chrome, which the law explicitly bans. Bullish/bearish/neutral may encode ONLY money/direction. Here green/emerald gradients decorate the 'Risk Management', 'Position Sizing', 'Execute' step icons and the success CTA; the bullish/bearish tokens are also mixed with raw green-500/red-500 borders (border-green-500/20, border-red-500/30, border-green-500/border in checkbox L528) so the same concept is expressed via both token and raw palette. _(high)_; Color used for elevation/depth instead of the neutral 4-step ladder. Cards get colored tint borders (border-cyan-500/20, border-purple-500/20, border-orange-500/20, border-green-500/20) and colored background washes (bg-cyan-500/10, bg-[var(--trade-bearish)]/10, gradient card backgrounds L557). Depth must come from bg-step + --border/--border-strong, never glow or chromatic tint. _(high)_; Yellow-500 / orange-500 used as generic accent for the 'B' and 'C' signal grades and R:R labels (L174-175, L406, L352, L389 text-orange-500). These are raw palette colors, not the --trade-neutral token, and they're being used decoratively rather than to encode direction. _(med)_; Mono-font misuse: per the law EVERY number uses mono, and only numbers/tickers do. Here non-numeric strings are forced into font-mono — chart names like '4-Hour Chart'/'1-Hour Chart' (L262), timeframe labels '5-15 Minute' (L232), and the ITM/ATM/OTM badges (L358). Conversely '8 min read' is correctly mono but the rest of meta is fine. Mono should be reserved for the actual numbers. _(med)_; Typography off the fixed scale. Hero/section headings use text-2xl/3xl/xl with font-semibold/font-bold (L90, L128, L411 text-3xl), and body uses text-base/text-sm rather than the data-xs(11)/data-sm(12)/data-base(13)/data-lg(15)/price-* tokens. The 3xl R:R numbers (L411) should use a price-* hero token. font-bold is used widely though the sans scale tops at 700 for headings only. _(med)_; Primary CTA hardcodes bg-cyan-500 with text-foreground (L567) instead of the brand-cyan token / canonical primary Button variant. Cyan-500 (#06b6d4) is also the wrong cyan — brand token is #22d3ee. _(med)_; text-foreground used as icon-on-gradient fill (e.g. BookOpen/CheckCircle2 inside colored gradient chips, L88/L143/L161). On a saturated gradient the intended color is white; relying on --foreground is fragile in light mode (foreground becomes near-black) — icon would vanish. Should consume an explicit on-accent token. _(low)_; Decorative grid-overlay backgrounds (L81, L711) are ambient chrome the law discourages; combined with gradient washes they read as decorative glow. No framer-motion micro-interactions anywhere (relies on .hover-elevate only); no prefers-reduced-motion concern but also no motion signature. _(low)_; glass-card / stat-glass utility names imply glassmorphism; in dark they resolve to flat #0a0a0a + #222 border (compliant) but stat-glass still uses backdrop-filter blur(8px) and rgba washes — a leftover glass aesthetic inconsistent with the flat elevation ladder. _(low)_; Density: card padding is p-4/p-8/md:p-12 and the hero uses space-y-12 — far looser than the compact default (card pad 8-10px, row 28px). Acceptable for a marketing/long-read surface but not driven by data-density attribute. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing structure (sticky-ish hero header, max-w-4xl reading column, 4-step guide sections, default-markdown branch). Layout is fundamentally fine for a long-read; the work is almost entirely a color/token/typography re-skin, not a re-layout. Tighten the hero from p-8/md:p-12 to a calmer pad and replace space-y-12 with a token-driven section rhythm. The default-markdown branch (DefaultBlogContent prose) is already clean and only needs prose-a/prose-code remapped from --primary to --brand-cyan.
- **Apply:** 1) DELETE CATEGORY_VISUALS gradients+radial patterns; replace each category with a single neutral icon chip (bg-secondary or bg-card with --border) + an accent-card stripe in brand cyan. Category differentiation comes from the lucide icon, not from 6 different rainbow gradients. 2) Replace EVERY from-X-500 to-Y-600 gradient with: neutral chip surfaces (bg-[hsl(var(--secondary))]) for icon containers, and brand cyan (hsl(var(--brand-cyan))) for the single accent. 3) Replace raw border-cyan/purple/orange/green-500/NN with border-[hsl(var(--border))] for chrome, reserving trade-bullish/bearish/neutral CSS vars ONLY where the content is literally money/direction (Bullish Signs / Bearish Signs cards, R:R green=good, the Golden-Rule loss callout). 4) Convert bg-cyan-500/10 washes and the CTA gradient card to flat bg-card + a single accent-card--cyan stripe. 5) Fix CTA button to the primary Button variant (which already consumes brand cyan) — remove bg-cyan-500 text-foreground. 6) Replace bg-[linear-gradient(rgba(6,182,212...))] grid overlays with nothing, or with a single tokenized .graph-grid-subtle if a texture is desired. 7) Typography: map headings to the fixed scale (h1=text-lg/data-lg-ish + font-semibold, micro-labels already correct as uppercase 500 tracking-wider muted), and STRIP font-mono from non-numeric strings (chart names, timeframe labels, ITM/ATM/OTM) while KEEPING it on the R:R numbers (2:1, 3:1, 5:1+), position-sizing $ values, signal counts, step numbers, and '8 min read'. Add tabular-nums to those. 8) yellow/orange grade colors -> --trade-neutral var for B grade, and demote C/D to neutral muted rather than orange. 9) Icon-on-accent: replace text-foreground on gradient chips with an explicit on-accent fill (white in dark, or a --primary-foreground token) now that chips are flat neutral. Motion: add framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth on the 4 idea-source cards and step cards (border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover, scale(0.98) press) replacing the bare .hover-elevate; respect prefers-reduced-motion. No 3D opportunity here (not a GEX surface).
- **Components:** Replace ad-hoc colored gradient chips/cards with canonical components: use the accent-card utility (.accent-card--cyan / --bullish / --bearish — a 2px left token stripe, see index.css L900-911) instead of full gradient borders+washes for the per-category and per-step accents. Use the canonical Badge variants (default/secondary/outline) instead of bg-gradient badges. Keep Card/CardContent/CardHeader, Button, Checkbox, Skeleton as-is. For the signal-grade rows, drive the badge color from the grade token set (--grade-s/--grade-a etc already exist in index.css) rather than bg-yellow-500/bg-orange-500.
- **Motion/3D:** Add the standard micro-interaction (framer-motion 150-300ms, --ease-out-smooth, hover=border-brighten+translateY(-1px), press=scale(.98)) to the interactive cards (4 idea sources, 4 step cards, checklist rows). Checklist completion banner can fade/scale in. No ambient glow, no springs, no Aceternity. No R3F/3D — this is a content page, not the GEX surface.

#### join-beta  · M effort · 11 issues

- **Violations:** Hardcoded hex everywhere via inline-style objects: inputStyles/inputWithIconStyles use backgroundColor '#1a1a2e', border '#374151', color '#ffffff', and the access-code input overrides color to '#22d3ee'. Every form input on the page bypasses tokens entirely. Direct law violation: 'Never hardcode hex/rgb in components.' #1a1a2e is a purple-tinted navy that isn't even in the neutral grayscale elevation ladder. _(high)_; Raw Tailwind palette utilities used as chrome instead of tokens: bg-cyan-500/hover:bg-cyan-400, text-cyan-400/500, bg-cyan-500/5, from-cyan-400 to-cyan-600 (gradient logo), and pervasive neutral-300/400/500/600/800/900. Accent cyan must come from --brand-cyan; surfaces/text/borders must come from --background/--card/--secondary/--border/--muted-foreground. _(high)_; Semantic bullish green misused as chrome/decoration. emerald-500/5 blur glow, emerald-500/10 icon chip, and the step-1 'completed' indicator (bg-emerald-500/20 border text-trade-bullish). --trade-bullish encodes ONLY money/direction, never generic success/completion state. A verified-step checkmark is chrome, not P&L. _(high)_; Banned ambient/decorative glow. Two large blur orbs: success screen 'w-[600px] h-[400px] bg-emerald-500/5 blur-[120px]' and form screen 'bg-cyan-500/5 blur-[120px]'. Law: 'NO ambient/decorative glow ... depth via bg-step + border, not glow.' Also shadow-2xl shadow-black/50 and repeated shadow-lg on buttons are glow-as-depth in a dark-first surface. _(high)_; Native <input> elements with inline styles instead of the canonical Input component, and a mix of bg-[#1a1a2e] hardcoded SelectTrigger/SelectContent classes. Bypasses the design system's input token (--input), focus ring (--ring/brand-cyan), radius, and density. Focus state is 'outline: none' with no replacement ring on the text inputs — fails the focus-accent rule and is an a11y regression. _(high)_; Numbers not in IBM Plex Mono. Step indicator digits '1'/'2' are sans. The access-code input sets fontFamily: 'monospace' (generic monospace, not --font-mono / IBM Plex Mono) and lacks tabular-nums/slashed-zero. Tickers/codes/all numerics must be IBM Plex Mono with font-variant-numeric. _(med)_; text-white hardcoded for headings/labels instead of foreground tokens (--foreground / --card-foreground). Breaks the dark-first inverse for light mode and ignores tokenized text. _(med)_; Radius inconsistency vs compact density. Mix of rounded-lg / rounded-xl / rounded-2xl and oversized 80px/40px icon tiles, h-12 buttons, py-8 spacing — comfortable/landing-scale, not the terminal's compact ladder. Card uses rounded-2xl while the rest of the app cards use the tokenized card radius. _(low)_; Decorative cyan gradient hairline ('h-px bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-cyan-500/30 to-transparent') on each card top is non-token chrome decoration. _(low)_; Card primitive used inconsistently: outer wrapper is a raw <div className='bg-card border ...'> but inner uses CardHeader/CardContent/CardTitle/CardDescription. Either fully use the Card component or none — current hybrid hand-rolls the card shell. _(low)_; No motion signature. transition-all/transition-colors only; no framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth, no hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px) / press scale(0.98) on the CTA or option tiles, no prefers-reduced-motion handling. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the 2-step centered-card flow and success state; tighten to terminal density. Replace py-8/px-6 outer with compact padding, drop the max-w-lg landing feel to a focused max-w-md. Shrink logo tile and step-indicator circles (40px to ~28-32px) to match row/compact scale. Convert the raw card shell to the canonical Card component (Card + CardHeader/CardContent) so the wrapper, radius, and border come from tokens. Remove both decorative blur orbs and the cyan gradient hairlines entirely; depth comes from --card over --background plus --border.
- **Apply:** Tokens: surfaces -> bg-background (page) / bg-card (card) / bg-secondary (input + select rows); borders -> border-border, focus border-strong; text -> text-foreground (headings/labels), text-muted-foreground (sub/help). Accent: replace ALL cyan-* utilities with the brand token (e.g. bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] or the primary/brand utility) for the CTA, focus ring (ring-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]), active step indicator, and the access-code input text. Remove emerald-*: the verified-step checkmark and success icon chip become brand-cyan or neutral chrome (NOT --trade-bullish) since this is completion-state, not P&L; only keep green if you decide a 'success' check legitimately reads as money/direction (it does not here). Numbers: step digits and the access-code input -> font-mono (IBM Plex Mono) with font-variant-numeric tabular-nums + slashed zero. Replace text-white with text-foreground; replace shadow-2xl/shadow-lg/shadow-black with token border elevation (no glow).
- **Components:** Delete inputStyles/inputWithIconStyles objects and the native <input> elements. Use the canonical Input component (with the lucide icon as a leading adornment) wired through Form/FormField/FormControl/FormMessage like the rest of the form already does for selects. Use Card/CardHeader/CardTitle/CardDescription/CardContent consistently. Keep Select/Checkbox/Button primitives but strip their hardcoded bg-[#1a1a2e]/border-neutral-700 classes so they fall back to component defaults.
- **Motion/3D:** Add framer-motion micro-interactions per law: CTA and knowledge-area option tiles get hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px) and press scale(0.98) at 150-300ms on --ease-out-smooth; step transition (verify -> onboard) can crossfade/slide the card 200ms. Wrap in prefers-reduced-motion guard. No 3D here (no GEX surface); this is auth, keep it restrained.

#### login  · M effort · 10 issues
*Today:* A two-panel auth screen carrying the marketing/landing aesthetic, not the terminal one: a left branding panel with an emerald gradient + emerald grid pattern, stat blocks, and a testimonial card; a right form panel with Google OAuth, email/password, waitlist CTA, and a hidden admin code field. Light-mode-first (bg-white / gray-200 / #fafafa hardcoded everywhere) with dark: overrides bolted on, and emerald green used decoratively throughout.

- **Violations:** Decorative emerald green everywhere violates the semantic-only rule. trade-bullish #10b981 may encode ONLY money/direction, yet here emerald is chrome/decoration: left-panel gradient (from-emerald-100 / dark:from-emerald-950/50), grid pattern rgba(16,185,129,0.1) (L116-120), avatar chip bg-emerald-500/20 + initials text (L166-167), Sparkles icon + its bg-emerald-500/10 chip (L230-231). _(high)_; Pervasive hardcoded non-token colors. bg-white, border-gray-200/300, bg-gray-50, #fafafa, bg-gray-900 dark:bg-white text-black, bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/20 appear on the testimonial card, waitlist notice, Google button, divider, both Inputs, the primary Sign-in CTA, and the waitlist CTA (L161,214,228,246,256,259,277,297,324,338). Law requires hsl(var(--token)) / named tokens, never raw hex or zinc/gray/emerald/red scales. _(high)_; Canonical Button component is overridden into a raw custom button. Every Button passes a full className that replaces its variant styling with bg-white/bg-gray-900/dark:bg-white etc., so the design-system button (focus ring, elevate, shadow tokens) is bypassed. Primary CTA should be the brand-cyan primary action, not an ad-hoc black/white block. _(high)_; Inputs bypass the canonical Input token styling and kill the focus ring. Both fields override with bg-white dark:bg-card, border-gray-200, and focus:ring-0 / focus:border-gray-300 — the law mandates brand-cyan for focus/active. focus:ring-0 removes the accessible focus state entirely. _(high)_; Numbers are not on the mono data-scale. Stat values 2,500+, Multi, 24/7 use text-2xl font-mono (L147,151,155) but no tabular-nums/lining-nums/slashed-zero and an off-scale arbitrary size; they read as decorative landing stats rather than law-compliant data-xs/price-sm tokens. The © year (L178) is plain sans. _(med)_; Error/alert surfaces are hand-rolled instead of using the canonical Alert component. bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/20 with text-[var(--trade-bearish)] (L214) reinvents an alert and reuses the bearish money-color for a generic auth error (law: bearish encodes direction, not generic error — destructive token belongs here). _(med)_; Surface elevation is flat/ad-hoc, not the 4-step ladder. Cards use bg-white dark:bg-card with border-gray-200 dark:border-border; no L0->L3 progression, no --border-strong, and the right panel sits directly on bg-background with no card elevation. _(med)_; No motion signature. Static gradient/grid only; no framer-motion entrance, no hover border-brighten + translateY(-1px) on cards/CTA, no press scale(0.98), no prefers-reduced-motion handling. Only inherited CSS transitions. _(low)_; Brand accent is absent. The ONE chromatic accent (brand-cyan #22d3ee) appears nowhere — no cyan focus, link, or primary CTA. The page leans entirely on demoted emerald + neutral, so it does not read as QuantEdge. _(low)_; Logo wordmark uses font-medium sans and an h-8 raster PNG; acceptable but not aligned to the uppercase/tracking micro-label conventions used elsewhere in the terminal. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the two-panel split (hidden lg:flex left brand / right form, max-w-sm). Re-skin LEFT panel: delete the emerald gradient + emerald grid; base on bg-background (L0) with the law-sanctioned .page-atmosphere (cyan/blue radials + grid at 0.025-0.04 alpha) instead. RIGHT panel: lift the form onto an explicit --card (L1) surface, p-8, rounded-[var(--radius-card)], --border, with shadow in light / border-depth in dark, rather than sitting bare on bg-background. Demote left stat blocks and testimonial to the law data + neutral surface treatment.
- **Apply:** TOKEN MAP: bg-white->bg-card; border-gray-200/300->border-border / hover:border-border-strong; #fafafa & bg-gray-50->bg-background or bg-secondary; bg-gray-900 dark:bg-white text-black CTA->bg-primary text-primary-foreground (brand-cyan signature CTA); bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/20 text-[--trade-bearish]->destructive/10 + text-destructive. STRIP ALL EMERALD: gradient/grid->neutral or .page-atmosphere; avatar chip bg-emerald-500/20->bg-secondary w/ muted-foreground initials; Sparkles chip bg-emerald-500/10->bg-secondary, icon text-brand-cyan (brand, not money). TYPOGRAPHY: stat values (2,500+ / Multi / 24/7) and © year -> mono data scale with tabular-nums lining-nums slashed-zero (.price-display-sm or font-mono + fixed scale), not arbitrary text-2xl; micro-labels ('Traders joined','Beta tester', divider 'or continue with email') -> UPPERCASE sans 500 tracking 0.05-0.08em muted. FOCUS: remove every focus:ring-0; use brand-cyan ring. STEPS: 1) delete emerald gradient+grid -> neutral + .page-atmosphere; 2) replace all hardcoded colors per map; 3) remove color/focus overrides on Button & Input, use variants; 4) make Sign in the brand-cyan primary; 5) convert error to <Alert variant=destructive> + FormMessage->destructive; 6) numbers to mono data scale, labels to uppercase tracked sans; 7) lift form onto --card L1 with --border + shadow/border-depth; 8) add reduced-motion-aware framer-motion entrance and restore default hover/press.
- **Components:** Use canonical <Button> WITHOUT color className overrides: 'Sign in' = default variant (mapped to brand-cyan primary CTA); Google + 'Join the waitlist' = variant='outline'; admin toggle = variant='ghost' size='sm'. Use canonical <Input> (drop bg-white/border-gray/focus:ring-0 overrides) so it inherits border-input + focus-visible:ring-ring (cyan). Replace the hand-rolled error div with <Alert variant='destructive'>. Keep <Form>/<FormField>/<FormMessage> but point FormMessage at the destructive token, not trade-bearish.
- **Motion/3D:** framer-motion entrance: motion.div fade + translateY on --ease-out-smooth 150-300ms (or reuse .animate-page-in). Restore default micro-interactions by NOT overriding them: CTA + outline buttons get border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover and scale(0.98) press (already global). Inputs animate a cyan focus ring. No ambient glow, no springs (landing-only, banned in-terminal), no 3D moment here (GEX surface is the only one). Honor prefers-reduced-motion.

#### success-stories  · M effort · 10 issues

- **Violations:** Hardcoded raw-palette gradients for chrome: hero section uses bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-900/50 to-background (line 29) and CTA uses from-background to-slate-900/30 (line 197). slate-900 is not a token; depth must come from elevation ladder + border, not slate tints. _(high)_; Decorative chromatic gradient icon tiles in stat grid (lines 46,55,66,77): bg-gradient-to-br from-green-500/cyan-500/amber-500/emerald-600 with white icons and shadow-lg. Law: exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan); green/amber/emerald are semantic money colors and must NOT be used as chrome/decoration. The shadow-lg is ambient decorative glow (banned). _(high)_; Buttons hardcode bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-foreground (lines 118, 208). Should use the canonical Button primary variant driven by --brand-cyan / --primary tokens, not raw cyan-500. text-foreground on a cyan fill is also wrong CTA contrast. _(high)_; Source badges introduce off-palette accents: AI=purple-500, quant=blue-500, default=cyan-500 (lines 173-176). Purple and blue are not in the allowed accent set; the AI/quant distinction should be encoded with neutral/cyan chrome or semantic tokens, not new chromatic hues. _(high)_; Bullish direction badge mixes token + raw palette: bg-[var(--trade-bullish)]/10 text-[var(--trade-bullish)] border-green-500/30 (line 139). The border uses raw green-500 instead of the trade-bullish token; bearish similarly mixes bg-red-500/10 with the token (line 140). Should be fully tokenized. _(med)_; Empty-state icon tile uses bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-600 to-slate-700 (line 109) — hardcoded slate, off the elevation ladder. _(med)_; Numbers are font-mono tabular-nums but not using the canonical data-cell/.price-display utilities (no slashed-zero, no lining-nums feature settings) and use ad-hoc Tailwind sizes (text-2xl) instead of the fixed data/price scale (price-sm/md/lg). _(med)_; Ticker symbol (line 136) is font-mono font-bold but does not apply the .ticker-font treatment (uppercase mono 600 + 0.04em tracking) the law mandates for tickers. _(low)_; glass-card uses backdrop-blur (saturate/blur) per the utility def; banned in-terminal, though tolerable on a public marketing page. No framer-motion micro-interactions (150-300ms ease-out-smooth, translateY(-1px) hover, scale(0.98) press) — relies only on hover-elevate. prefers-reduced-motion not addressed. _(low)_; Micro-labels use text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider (lines 32,52,63, etc.) — close to spec but tracking-wider (0.05em) is at the edge of the 0.05-0.08em range and labels are sans (correct); fine. Font tokens in this repo are DM Sans/JetBrains Mono, not IBM Plex named in the law summary — a repo-wide token gap, not page-specific. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep three-section structure; flatten to elevation ladder (bg-background sections, bg-card cards, --border separators), drop all slate gradients.
- **Apply:** Remove slate/green/amber/purple/blue/cyan raw classes; tokenize via --brand-cyan, --trade-bullish/bearish, bg-card/bg-secondary. Use canonical Card + Button + Badge. Add data-cell/.ticker-font to numbers and tickers. Replace stat-glass with inset-panel.

#### terms-of-service  · M effort · 10 issues

- **Violations:** Hardcoded slate-500 chrome throughout: header dot-grid uses raw rgba(100,116,139,0.03) (lines 8); header icon tile + section icon tiles use bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-500 to-slate-600 (lines 14, 54, 159); header underline uses via-slate-500/30 (line 25). Law forbids zinc/slate/gray and raw rgba in components — chrome must use --background/--card/--secondary elevation + --border/--border-strong. _(high)_; Decorative gradient fills on icon tiles (from-slate-500/600, from-amber-500/600) plus shadow-lg create ambient glow/decoration. Law: depth via bg-step + border, NOT glow; gradients-as-decoration are banned in-terminal. Amber gradient also misuses the semantic --trade-neutral color family as pure chrome decoration. _(high)_; Semantic trade color misuse: --trade-neutral (amber, reserved for money/direction only) is applied to a legal 'Educational Platform' disclaimer (line 30) and a liability warning (line 149). These are warnings/chrome, not money or direction. Per law, trade-bullish/bearish/neutral encode ONLY money/direction. A non-trade warning should use neutral muted chrome, not the trade-neutral accent. _(high)_; amber-500 used directly as Tailwind class for the disclaimer card border/bg (border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5, line 28) and icon tile (line 31) instead of the token (--trade-neutral / --trade-neutral-bg). Even where the token IS used (text-[var(--trade-neutral)]), the surrounding card mixes raw amber classes — inconsistent and bypasses tokens. _(med)_; text-white hardcoded on all four icons (lines 15, 32, 55, 160) instead of a token (e.g. text-primary-foreground or hsl(var(--foreground))). Breaks dark-first token discipline. _(med)_; Malformed section header markup in Platform Services card: a 'Section' micro-label is wrapped in CardTitle then a second CardTitle holds the actual title (lines 75-77), while every other card uses a single CardTitle. Inconsistent, semantically wrong, and visually divergent. _(med)_; Date 'October 21, 2025' (line 20) and any numeric content render in IBM Plex Sans, not mono. Law requires EVERY number (incl. dates/time) in --font-mono with tabular-nums. Minor here since the only number is a date, but still a violation. _(low)_; Inconsistent radius: header uses rounded-xl, icon tiles mix rounded-xl (h-12, line 14) and rounded-lg (h-10, lines 31/54/159), cards default. No single radius token discipline. _(low)_; hover-elevate applied to static legal cards (lines 51, 72, 95, 113, 135, 156, 181). These are non-interactive content cards — hover lift implies clickability and adds motion with no affordance. Hover/translateY signature is for interactive elements only. _(low)_; Ad-hoc page header (lines 7-26) duplicates a pattern other pages implement inline too; no canonical PageHeader component is used, so the micro-label/title/timestamp treatment is non-standard vs the rest of the app. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the single-column max-w-4xl stacked-card document layout — appropriate for legal text. Replace the bespoke hero header (lines 7-26) with the canonical page-header pattern: micro-label (UPPERCASE sans 500, tracking 0.05-0.08em, text-muted-foreground) + 'Terms of Service' title in IBM Plex Sans 600 + 'Last updated' line with the date in mono. Drop the dot-grid background and the slate gradient underline, or rebuild the divider as a flat border-[hsl(var(--border))] hairline. Normalize all section cards to a single CardTitle (fix the double-title Platform Services card, lines 75-77). Standardize one radius (rounded-lg) on all icon tiles.
- **Apply:** Remove every slate/amber Tailwind color class and raw rgba: (1) header dot-grid bg-[linear-gradient(rgba(100,116,139...))] -> delete or use a token-based subtle bg; (2) icon tiles from-slate-500 to-slate-600 / from-amber-500 to-amber-600 -> bg-secondary border border-border, no shadow-lg; (3) header underline via-slate-500/30 -> border-t border-border; (4) text-white -> text-[hsl(var(--foreground))] or text-muted-foreground; (5) disclaimer card border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5 -> border-border bg-secondary (this is a legal notice, NOT money) and recolor CardTitle from text-[var(--trade-neutral)] to text-foreground; (6) the liability AlertTriangle row text-[var(--trade-neutral)] -> text-muted-foreground. Reserve --trade-neutral strictly for money/direction elsewhere. Wrap the date (line 20) in <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">. Remove hover-elevate from all static legal cards.
- **Components:** Reuse shared Card/CardHeader/CardTitle/CardContent (already imported). Adopt the app's canonical page-header treatment used elsewhere (success-stories/features/trading-rules share the 'uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground' micro-label idiom — extract a PageHeader if one is later created). Icons: keep lucide FileText/AlertTriangle/Shield/Scale. Icon tiles: replace gradient+shadow-lg with flat bg-secondary (L2) + border border-[hsl(var(--border))], icon colored text-muted-foreground (or text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] for a single brand accent on the FileText hero icon only).
- **Motion/3D:** None warranted. This is a static legal document — no framer-motion, no springs, no 3D. At most a single 150ms ease-out fade-in on mount, respecting prefers-reduced-motion. Remove the hover-lift entirely since cards are non-interactive.

#### academy  · M effort · 9 issues

- **Violations:** Rainbow of off-palette chromatic gradients used as decoration, violating the ONE-accent rule. Hero icon tile from-purple-500 to-blue-600 (L177); active category button bg-purple-500 text-white (L201); three Quick-Link icon tiles from-blue-500 to-emerald-500, from-emerald-500 to-green-500, from-purple-500 to-pink-500 (L219,233,247); disclaimer tile from-amber-500 to-orange-500 (L284). Law allows only brand-cyan + the 3 semantic money colors. Purple/blue/pink are not in the system at all; emerald/amber here encode nothing about money or direction. _(high)_; Purple used as the link/hover/CTA accent throughout instead of brand-cyan: text-purple-400 on title hovers (L332,403), text-purple-500 on 'Read article'/'Read' affordances (L350,412), category label text-purple-400/blue-400/orange-400 (L363,366,397). Links/active/brand must be --brand-cyan. _(high)_; Hardcoded hex/rgb colors in the hero, banned by the tokens-only rule AND the no-ambient-glow rule: decorative gradient wash from-purple-500/10 ... to-emerald-500/10 (L168) and a hardcoded rgba(147,51,234,0.03) purple grid overlay (L169). This is exactly the 'ambient/decorative glow' the law forbids in-terminal. _(high)_; Numbers are not rendered in mono with tabular-nums. Read-times ('12 min', readTime) and dates ('Jan 2, 2026', date) are plain --font-sans (L343-347, 388-391, 411). Law requires IBM Plex Mono + font-variant-numeric tabular-nums slashed-zero for EVERY number including time. _(med)_; Decorative colored glow shadow shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 on the disclaimer icon tile (L284) — violates 'NO ambient/decorative glow'. _(med)_; glass-card (backdrop-blur + saturate) is used on every card and the disclaimer (L217,231,245,282,304,373). Dark-first law builds elevation from the 4-step bg ladder + border/border-strong, not translucent blur panels; --card/--secondary surfaces are canonical. _(med)_; Inconsistent radius and ad-hoc opacity borders. Disclaimer uses rounded-xl (L282) while cards use the default; borders are border-border/50 everywhere (L167,217,231,245,282,339,410) instead of the canonical --border / --border-strong tokens. Icon tiles use rounded-lg, badge uses rounded-md — no single radius scale. _(low)_; Micro-label tracking is off-spec: tracking-wider (~0.05em is fine but applied with text-[10px]/text-xs sans) is acceptable, but the page mixes text-[10px] hardcoded sizes (L172,182,224,238,252) instead of the fixed micro/data-xs (9px/11px) scale, and hero h1 is text-base/font-semibold rather than a defined hero scale. _(low)_; Article-card category badge uses bg-[var(--surface-base)]/50 backdrop-blur-sm border-none over imagery (L385) — surface-base at 50% with blur is a one-off treatment not in the elevation ladder. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the structure (hero with category filter, 3 quick-link cards, featured article, 3-col article grid, disclaimer) — it is sound for a content/education surface. Tighten to the density system: wrap the page in data-density and reduce hero padding to compact, drop the redundant max-width swap (hero max-w-[1600px] vs body max-w-7xl — unify on one container). Replace the decorative hero background with a clean --background fill plus a single bottom border-border; no grid overlay, no gradient wash. Make the featured-card image:content split and grid gap-6 keep, but move all meta (date, read-time) into a mono data row.
- **Apply:** COLOR: delete every from-*/to-* gradient and purple/blue/pink/orange/emerald/amber utility. Icon tiles -> bg-secondary (or bg-muted) with the lucide icon in text-muted-foreground; the ONE featured/active accent -> brand cyan via text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] or a cyan token class. Active category Button: drop className override, let variant='default' carry --primary; if a colored active state is wanted use ring-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]. Link/hover affordances ('Read article', title hover, 'Read'): replace text-purple-* with text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] and group-hover:text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]. TYPOGRAPHY: wrap dates, read-times in font-mono with tabular-nums (add the .data-cell class or font-[var(--font-mono)] + tabular-nums); category labels and 'Learning Center'/'Latest Articles' kickers -> uppercase font-medium tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground at data-xs(11px)/micro(9px). SURFACE: replace glass-card with bg-card border border-border rounded-[var(--radius-card)]; the over-image badge -> bg-card/80 not surface-base/50. BORDERS: border-border (not /50), use border-strong on hover. RADIUS: standardize on rounded-[var(--radius-card)] for cards, rounded-[var(--radius-badge)] for badges; drop rounded-xl. Remove shadow-amber-500/20 and the hero gradient/grid divs entirely.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical Card/CardContent, Badge (variant=outline/secondary), Button (variant=default for active filter — its default already maps to --primary, no custom bg needed). For category labels use the established micro-label pattern (uppercase sans-500, muted). For the disclaimer use the accent-card--gold left-border utility (it reads from --brand-gold token) instead of a colored icon tile + glow.
- **Motion/3D:** Apply the canonical micro-interaction only: card hover = border-brighten (border-border -> border-strong) + translateY(-1px) over 150-200ms on --ease-out-smooth, press scale(0.98). The existing img group-hover:scale-105 over 500ms is decorative drift — shorten to ~250ms and gate behind prefers-reduced-motion, or drop it for in-terminal consistency. No springs, no glow, no Aceternity. There is no data-viz here so no 3D opportunity; this surface should stay calm and content-forward.

#### about  · M effort · 9 issues

- **Violations:** Massive hardcoded Tailwind-palette usage instead of tokens. Six engine cards use rainbow gradient icon tiles (from-blue-500, from-emerald-500, from-purple-500, from-amber-500, from-red-500, from-cyan-500 lines 18/24/30/36/42/48). Section header icon tiles repeat the same (emerald-to-teal-600 L162, purple L187, amber L216, blue L243, green L268, cyan L292). This is the single biggest violation: law allows exactly ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan) plus semantic bullish/bearish/neutral for money only — a 6-color rainbow is forbidden chrome decoration. _(high)_; Semantic trade colors used as decoration/brand, not money/direction. Hero headline 'Multi-Engine Intelligence' is text-[var(--trade-bullish)] (L132); stat values are text-[var(--trade-bullish)] (L150); Mission card has border-[var(--trade-bullish)]/50 left border (L167); trust-badge icons are text-[var(--trade-bullish)] (L277). Bullish green encodes only P/L and direction — using it for headings, generic stats, and a security card violates the semantic-color law. _(high)_; Numbers not rendered in mono. Stat values 6 / 300+ / 8000+ / 24/7 use text-3xl font-bold default sans (L150) with no font-mono / tabular-nums / slashed-zero. Timeline years '2024 Q1' etc. (L254) and footer year (L370) are also sans. Law: EVERY number is mono (--font-mono) with tabular-nums + slashed zero. _(high)_; Raw cyan-400/cyan-500/cyan-300 used directly instead of var(--brand-cyan). Hero badge border-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-400 (L127), engine card hover:border-cyan-500/30 (L194), value icons text-cyan-400 (L227), timeline badges border-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-400 (L253), timeline dot bg-cyan-500 (L256), creator photo border-cyan-500/20 (L304), creator title + links text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 (L310/324/333). Correct accent intent but bypasses the token — law says consume hsl(var(--brand-cyan)), never the raw palette name. _(med)_; text-white hardcoded on every icon inside gradient tiles (L163/188/198/217/244/269/293). Not a token; breaks light/dark inversion contract. _(med)_; CTA primary button is a multi-stop gradient bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-cyan-500 (L352). Primary CTA must be the single brand-cyan token, not a two-color gradient mixing semantic green with brand cyan. _(med)_; glass-card used heavily (12 instances). In index.css glass-card is defined three times with conflicting rules — the @layer utilities version (L1493) applies blur(16px) saturate(180%) + 0 8px 32px glow shadow. Law bans ambient/decorative glow and heavy glass in-terminal; even on a marketing page the 32px glow box-shadow is off-spec. The dark override flattens it but light mode keeps the glow. _(low)_; Micro-labels not following uppercase-tracking spec. Stat labels (L153) and section eyebrows are plain sans default casing — no UPPERCASE 500 + 0.05–0.08em tracking treatment for the label tier. _(low)_; No motion signature. Cards are glass-card (not shadcn-card) so they likely miss the global hover lift; engine cards only get bare transition-colors (L194). No framer-motion 150–300ms entrance and no canonical border-brighten + translateY(-1px) hover. Header uses backdrop-blur which the in-terminal law discourages (acceptable-ish on a public page). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing vertical section flow (hero, stats bar, mission, 6 layers, values, timeline, security, creator, CTA, footer) — structure is content-appropriate and fine. Tighten spacing toward token rhythm: --section-gap (40px) between major sections instead of space-y-16, --card-gap (10px) for grids. Add className="page-atmosphere animate-page-in" to the root div (as home/trade-desk/gex do) so public pages share one ambient treatment plus a clean opacity entrance.
- **Apply:** COLOR: delete the per-item `color` gradient field on all 6 engines and recolor every icon tile to one neutral treatment — bg-[hsl(var(--secondary))] with icon text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))], or a uniform brand-cyan-tinted tile bg-[var(--brand-cyan)]/10. Replace every from-*/to-* section-header tile the same way. Drop text-white from icons (tile is now neutral — use text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] or text-foreground). HERO: headline -> text-foreground (remove trade-bullish); optionally one cyan highlight word via text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]. STATS: values -> text-foreground, not trade-bullish. SEMANTIC GREEN: remove trade-bullish from stats, headline, mission border, trust icons; mission left border -> border-l-2 border-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]/40; trust icons -> text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] or muted-foreground (security ≠ money). RAW CYAN: swap every cyan-400/500/300 for hsl(var(--brand-cyan)); links use text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))] hover:opacity-80 instead of cyan-300. CTA: replace the gradient button with the default brand-cyan primary Button (no from-/to-). NUMBERS: wrap all stat values+suffixes, timeline years, and footer year in className="font-mono tabular-nums"; add .price-display / price-display-lg on the big stat numbers for the hero-number scale + slashed-zero. LABELS: stat labels and section eyebrows -> uppercase text-xs font-medium tracking-[0.06em] text-muted-foreground. CARDS: drop glass-card; use canonical Card default (bg-card border-border) or .bloomberg-panel to kill the glow and rely on border+elevation.
- **Components:** Use canonical shadcn Card/CardContent (remove glass-card so cards inherit bg-card/border-border and the global .shadcn-card hover lift). Use the canonical Button for the CTA (variant default = brand primary; variant outline for secondary). Use Badge with a token-driven outline (border-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]/30 text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]) for the hero pill and timeline year chips. Consider the existing .section-chrome + .section-label utilities for section headers to get consistent mono uppercase dividers instead of the colored icon-tile + h2 pattern.
- **Motion/3D:** P2 marketing page — NO new 3D and no Aceternity/spring effects (those are landing-only; this is borderline). Acceptable motion: framer-motion fade + translateY entrance on each <section>, staggered 150–300ms on --ease-out-smooth, gated on prefers-reduced-motion; standardize card hover to the law default border-brighten + translateY(-1px) by using real Card (shadcn-card) instead of glass-card. No ambient glow.

#### features  · L effort · 9 issues

Law allows EXACTLY ONE chromatic accent (brand cyan). Law: these tokens encode ONLY money/direction, never chrome or decoration. _(high)_; Raw slate hex-equivalent backgrounds bypass token system: line 66 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-950 via-slate-950/95 to-slate-900/90. Law forbids hardcoded zinc/slate/gray; must use --background / --card / --surface-overlay elevation ladder. _(high)_; Ambient/decorative glow banned in-terminal: ParticleBackground (lines 48,63-65) and line 140 bg-gradient-to-b from-cyan-500/5 hero glow, plus pervasive colored /10 icon-tile backgrounds (bg-pink-500/10, bg-purple-500/10 etc) and gradient icon tiles (from-amber-500/20 to-amber-600/10, lines 701-927). Depth must come from bg-step + border, not glow. (Mitigated slightly: this is a landing page, where Aceternity-style effects are permitted — but the colored glow tiles still violate the single-accent + no-ambient-glow rules.) _(med)_; Hero stat numbers and section counts not consistently mono+tabular-nums. Lines 158-176 stat values use font-mono but lack font-variant-numeric tabular-nums lining-nums / slashed-zero; the colored stat values (purple-400 '5', pink-400 '15+', line 162/174) also violate single-accent. Inline counts at 900/921 do add tabular-nums, showing the inconsistency. _(med)_; Typography family mismatch vs law: law mandates IBM Plex Sans/Mono, but tokens in index.css resolve --font-sans to 'DM Sans' and --font-mono to 'JetBrains Mono'. Page inherits these via font-mono/foreground, so the page itself isn't hardcoding wrong fonts, but the rendered result will not be IBM Plex. (Root-cause is in index.css, not this file.) _(low)_; Inconsistent radius/spacing: engine cards use rounded-xl p-6 (198-348), scanner/options/bot cards rounded-xl p-5 (394+), research/analytics cards rounded-lg p-5 (700+). Mixed rounded-xl vs rounded-lg and p-6/p-5 with no density attribute. Law wants one data-density-driven compact system. _(low)_; Inconsistent CTA/link active states: active nav link is text-cyan-400 (80), primary CTAs are bg-cyan-500 text-foreground (126,1026) — text-foreground on cyan-500 yields poor contrast and isn't the primary-CTA token treatment. Should be brand-cyan background with a defined on-accent foreground token. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the existing section skeleton (hero stats, Six Layers grid, Scanners, Smart Money, Options Lab, Bots, AI Learning, Research Tools, Market Coverage, Analytics, Data Intelligence, Education, Data Providers, CTA, disclaimer, footer) — the information architecture is fine. The redesign is almost entirely a color/token pass, not a structural one. Normalize all feature cards to ONE canonical card: bg-card, border border-[hsl(var(--border))], rounded-lg, p-5, hover -> border-strong + translateY(-1px). Collapse the three different paddings/radii (rounded-xl p-6, rounded-xl p-5, rounded-lg p-5) into the single compact card. Replace the per-section colored left-border accent strips with a uniform --border, OR if differentiation is desired, use the neutral elevation ladder (L1 bg-card with L2 hover) rather than hue.
- **Apply:** Global find/replace strategy: (1) Every text-cyan-400 / bg-cyan-500 / border-cyan-500/* -> token: text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))], bg-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))], border-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]. (2) Delete ALL purple/pink/blue/orange/green/amber/emerald utilities — replace decorative ones with neutral chrome (text-muted-foreground, bg-secondary, border-[hsl(var(--border))]) and reserve cyan as the single accent. (4) Line 66: replace from-slate-950... gradient with token surfaces — bg-[hsl(var(--background))] or remove and rely on the page bg. (5) Hero stat numbers (158-176): font-mono + tabular-nums lining-nums slashed-zero, color text-foreground (drop purple-400/pink-400). (6) Eyebrow micro-labels (143,186,384,461,508 etc): standardize to uppercase sans 500, tracking 0.05-0.08em, text-muted-foreground; only the eyebrow on the most important section may use brand-cyan. (7) CTA buttons: use the primary Button variant (brand-cyan bg with proper on-accent foreground token) instead of bg-cyan-500 text-foreground.

Introduce one shared FeatureCard wrapper {icon, title, subtitle, bullets} to kill the ~40 copy-pasted card blocks and guarantee token consistency. Keep lucide icons (already all lucide, no emoji — good).
- **Motion/3D:** This is a landing page, so Aceternity/Magic/spring effects are PERMITTED here (the in-terminal ban does not apply). Replace the generic ParticleBackground + ambient cyan glow with at most one tasteful landing-only hero moment. Card hover should still follow the terminal signature for consistency: framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1), border-brighten (border -> border-strong) + translateY(-1px), respecting prefers-reduced-motion. Opportunity: the Six Analysis Layers section is the natural place to tease the signature R3F GEX gamma surface (lazy-loaded, cyan-lit peaks/purple valleys) as a live preview to entice signup — high-value and on-brand since GEX is the one sanctioned 3D moment.

#### how-to  · M effort · 9 issues

- **Violations:** ENTIRE PAGE is font-mono. Every heading, subtitle, prose paragraph, list item, label, and decision question uses `font-mono`. The law restricts mono to numbers only (price/%/ticker/strike/GEX/Greeks/time); all WORDS must be IBM Plex Sans. This page is ~95% prose with almost no real numbers, so it should be almost entirely sans. This is the single biggest violation. _(high)_; Arbitrary pixel font sizes via Tailwind brackets instead of the fixed type scale. Uses text-[9px], text-[10px], text-[11px], text-[12px], text-2xl. The law mandates micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 tokenized utilities. text-[10px] body copy is below the data-xs(11) floor and the page mixes 9/10/11/12 inconsistently for similar roles. _(high)_; Emoji-equivalent / manual glyph iconography used as bullets and step markers. The Thesis Radar section uses literal `•` bullet characters (lines 77-82) and hand-typed numeric markers 1.-5. colored with brand-cyan (lines 69,73,85,89,93). Law requires lucide icons only, no emoji/glyph decoration. Numbered steps should be a styled ordered list or lucide markers, not inline cyan digits. _(med)_; `qe-card` class is undefined — it does not exist anywhere in index.css (confirmed: zero CSS definitions, used as a no-op). Cards render purely off the inline `border border-border/40 rounded-md` fallback. No canonical component is used. The app has components/ui/card.tsx and an `.accent-card` system in index.css that this page ignores. _(med)_; Cards do not follow the elevation ladder. They sit at `border-border/40` with no explicit surface step (no bg-card / L1). On the L0 page background the cards are nearly invisible (border-only at 40% opacity), failing the 4-step bg-step depth model. Borders use arbitrary /40, /30 opacity rather than --border / --border-strong. _(med)_; No framer-motion micro-interactions and incomplete hover signature. Cards/links use plain CSS `transition` with no duration/easing token, no translateY(-1px) hover lift, no scale(0.98) press. PageCard hovers only border-color; Step rows hover only bg. Law signature = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) at 150-300ms on --ease-out-smooth. _(low)_; Hardcoded/inconsistent radius — cards rounded-md, decision pills rounded, decision rows rounded: three different radii for sibling elements instead of token radii (--radius-card 10px / --radius-button 8px / --radius-badge 4px). _(low)_; Micro-labels are mono uppercase but should be UPPERCASE SANS 500 at 0.05-0.08em tracking. h1 is `font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-widest` (0.1em, out of band and wrong family); Section h2 and Step time labels share the issue. _(low)_; Brand-cyan applied via raw `text-[var(--brand-cyan)]` and `bg-[var(--brand-cyan)]/10` arbitrary-value syntax rather than a semantic utility. Color choice and usage (links/accents) are CORRECT and compliant — it only bypasses the tokenized class layer. No semantic trade colors are misused, no raw hex, no ambient glow on this page. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the strong information architecture as-is — max-w-3xl single column, 5 stacked Sections (Daily Workflow, What Each Page Does 2-col grid, How Thesis Radar Works, Decision Tree, What QuantEdge ISN'T). The structure is genuinely good; this is a typography + component-compliance refactor, not a re-layout. Tighten vertical rhythm to the density system: --section-gap-sm between sections, --card-gap (10px) in the PageCard grid, compact card padding 8-10px instead of the current mix of p-3/p-4.
- **Apply:** TYPOGRAPHY: strip font-mono from everything except real data tokens. Headings/subtitles/prose/list-items/decision questions -> IBM Plex Sans (default body). Keep font-mono ONLY on route strings (/p,/radar,/r/QCOM,/g,/trade-desk...), cron times (09:35,12:00,15:55,20:00) and counts (~120 tickers, 6 patterns, 7 signals) with tabular-nums + slashed zero; tickers/routes uppercase mono 600 +0.04em. Swap all text-[Npx]/text-2xl for scale utilities: h1 -> data-lg(15) sans 600 (or hero); section h2 -> data-sm(12) UPPERCASE SANS 500 tracking 0.06em muted; prose -> data-sm(12)/data-base(13) sans; PageCard desc + Radar bullets -> data-xs(11), raising the 10px floor; Step time label -> micro(9) UPPERCASE SANS 500 tracking 0.07em. SURFACES: card containers get bg-card + border-border at the L1 step; normalize all radii to --radius-card/--radius-badge and borders to --border/--border-strong (drop /40,/30 opacities). Convert raw text-[var(--brand-cyan)] / bg-[var(--brand-cyan)]/10 to a semantic link/accent utility (text-primary or a .link-cyan token class). Wrap the page in .animate-page-in. Kill the `•` glyphs and inline cyan digit markers.
- **Components:** Replace the undefined `qe-card` + inline-border pattern with the canonical Card from components/ui/card.tsx (bg-card, border-border, rounded per --radius-card) for Step, PageCard, the Radar explainer block, and DecisionRow containers. For the Step time-header and Radar numbered steps, use the existing `.accent-card--cyan` left-stripe utility (already in index.css) to encode the cyan accent structurally instead of coloring digits. Use a real <ol>/<ul> or a lucide marker (Circle/ChevronRight/Hash) for the 1-5 Radar steps and the bullet list — never literal glyphs.
- **Motion/3D:** No 3D here (the R3F GEX gamma surface is the only signature 3D moment and does not belong on a docs page; do NOT add page-atmosphere glow/grid — ambient glow is banned in-terminal). Motion scope = compliant micro-interactions only: framer-motion border-brighten + translateY(-1px) on Step rows and PageCards @ ~200ms --ease-out-smooth, press scale(0.98), and an optional short stagger fade-up for the PageCard grid on mount (landing-style springs banned). All gated on prefers-reduced-motion.

Hover lift (translateY(-1px)) and press scale(0.98) come only from the global CSS layer; the CTA's own hover is glow-intensify (a banned pattern) rather than border-brighten + lift. _(low)_; surface-base used for page bg (bg-[var(--surface-base)]) is acceptable but inline var() in an arbitrary-value class is non-idiomatic vs a bg-background utility; mixes token consumption styles within one file (bg-card on the panel, var() arbitrary on the page). _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the centered single-column max-w-md invite layout; it is appropriate for a low-traffic auth surface. Structure stays: brand lockup -> card(badge, title, subcopy, feature list, CTA) -> legal footer. Remove the decorative blur orb and the gradient hairline; lean on the elevation ladder (page = --background L0, card = --card L1, feature tiles = --secondary L2) with --border / --border-strong for separation instead of glow.
- **Apply:** Swap every raw class for tokens: text-neutral-400/500 -> text-muted-foreground; text-white -> text-foreground; border-neutral-800/700 -> border-border / border-[hsl(var(--border-strong))]; bg-neutral-800/50 -> bg-secondary; page bg -> bg-background (or keep bg-[hsl(var(--surface-base))] consistently). Accent: text-cyan-400 -> text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]; the badge bg/border -> hsl(var(--brand-cyan)/0.1) and /0.2; CTA -> Button primary (no shadow-cyan). Radius: card rounded-[var(--radius-card)], button rounded-[var(--radius-button)], badge rounded-[var(--radius-badge)]. Shadow: shadow-2xl shadow-black/50 -> var(--shadow-xl). Delete line 35 orb, line 56 gradient hairline, and all shadow-cyan-500/* utilities. Micro-labels ('LABS', badge text) should be uppercase sans-500 tracking 0.05-0.08em via existing utility patterns.
- **Components:** Replace the inline logo block (lines 41-50) with <QuantEdgeLogo size="lg" /> from @/components/quantedge-logo. Keep the shadcn <Button> but drop the bespoke cyan classes and use the default primary variant (or variant tied to --brand-cyan) so it inherits token theming; keep ArrowRight lucide icon. 'Exclusive Beta Access' pill should become a canonical badge (data-slot=badge) using --brand-cyan at low alpha. Feature rows can stay as simple flex rows with lucide icons in a token-bg tile.
- **Motion/3D:** Add a single framer-motion entrance: card fades/translates in over 200-250ms on --ease-out-smooth (matching .animate-page-in), respecting prefers-reduced-motion. CTA + feature tiles hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px), press scale(0.98) (already provided globally) — no glow escalation. No 3D moment here; the R3F GEX surface is the only signature 3D and does not belong on an auth page.

Eyebrow label 'GET STARTED' is correct uppercase-tracked-muted pattern but colored raw cyan. No tabular/mono concern since the form has no numbers. This is an app-wide component/token gap (button.tsx + --primary token), not unique to this page — the page correctly consumes the canonical button, so it is faithfully wrong. _(low)_; Foundation note (not page-specific): index.css ships --font-sans:'DM Sans' and --font-mono:'JetBrains Mono', not the law's IBM Plex Sans/Mono. This page has no numeric content so the mono rule is not triggered, but the typeface foundation diverges from the stated law. _(low)_

Card stays `w-full max-w-md`, canonical Card/CardHeader/CardContent. Reserve trade-bullish for money/direction only. 2) Keep all existing token usage (`bg-background`, `text-muted-foreground`, `bg-primary/10`+`text-primary` on the Mail icon is fine — neutral, not chromatic). 3) Links 'Back to Login' already use `text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground` — compliant; optionally promote the inline link to brand cyan on hover to match the law's link=cyan rule (`hover:text-[hsl(var(--brand-cyan))]`). 4) FormMessage/toast destructive paths already route through tokens — leave. 5) No hardcoded hex/zinc/slate/emerald exist on this page today — do NOT introduce any.
