#### not-found page  · S effort · 5 issues

- **Violations:** Semantic token misuse: the AlertCircle icon uses text-[var(--trade-bearish)] (#ef4444). Per the law, trade-bearish encodes ONLY money/direction (a down move). A 404 is generic chrome/error state, not a bearish trade signal — coloring it money-red pollutes the semantic palette. It should be neutral (text-muted-foreground), since this is chrome not P/L. _(med)_; Off-scale typography: the h1 uses text-2xl (24px), which is outside the fixed scale (micro 9 / data-xs 11 / data-sm 12 / data-base 13 / data-lg 15 / price-sm/md/lg). There is no 24px step for a non-hero heading; it should use a defined heading/data-lg token instead of an arbitrary Tailwind size. _(low)_; No framer-motion micro-interaction on mount. Law calls for 150–300ms ease-out-smooth entrances; the card appears with no transition. Buttons do inherit canonical hover/press shadows, so this is minor and low-traffic. Any added motion must respect prefers-reduced-motion. _(low)_; Arbitrary-value class text-[var(--trade-bearish)] reads a token (so it is NOT a raw-hex hardcode violation) but bypasses the named-utility convention; inline arbitrary token access is less maintainable than a named class. _(low)_; Foundation note (not page-specific): index.css ships DM Sans / JetBrains Mono, while the law summary names IBM Plex Sans / IBM Plex Mono. This page consumes --font-sans/--font-mono correctly, so the page is compliant; the mismatch is a global token concern to flag elsewhere. _(low)_
- **Layout:** Keep the centered single-card layout — appropriate for a terminal 404. Card max-w-md, mx-4, min-h-screen flex-center on bg-background (L0). Inside CardContent (pt-6): vertical stack — muted icon + heading row, one muted body line, then a 2-button action row. Optionally add a small mono detail line showing the attempted route (font-mono, data-sm, text-muted-foreground, tabular-nums) since routes/identifiers are mono in this system — reinforces the terminal feel without adding chrome.
- **Apply:** 1) Line 14: change icon class from text-[var(--trade-bearish)] to text-muted-foreground (neutral chrome; free the money-red token for real P/L). 2) Line 15: replace text-2xl with the on-scale heading/data-lg token (e.g. text-[length:var(--text-data-lg)] or the designated heading class), keep font-bold(=700, allowed) text-foreground. 3) Optionally wrap Card in a framer-motion.div: initial {opacity:0,y:4} -> animate {opacity:1,y:0}, 200ms var(--ease-out-smooth), gated on !prefers-reduced-motion. 4) Optionally render a mono line with the unmatched path via useLocation in font-mono tabular-nums. No other changes — surfaces/text tokens (bg-background, bg-card, text-foreground, text-muted-foreground) are already correct.
- **Components:** Reuse canonical @/components/ui/card (Card + CardContent) and @/components/ui/button — both already token-driven (bg-card, border-card-border, rounded-[var(--radius-card)]; default button = bg-primary brand-cyan CTA, outline = secondary). Keep lucide AlertCircle/Home/ArrowLeft (no emoji). Keep variant='outline' on Go Back and default (cyan primary) on Home — correct single-accent CTA hierarchy.
- **Motion/3D:** Single mount-entrance micro-interaction only (framer-motion fade + translateY, 150–300ms, --ease-out-smooth), respecting prefers-reduced-motion. NO ambient glow and NO 3D — the GEX gamma surface is the only sanctioned 3D moment and does not belong on a 404.

#### reset-password  · S effort · 5 issues

The cyan accent is entirely absent from this surface. _(med)_; Semantic color misuse: success state uses --trade-bullish (#10b981 green) for the CheckCircle badge (lines 105-106). The law's signature is framer-motion micro-interactions (150-300ms, --ease-out-smooth). The card mounts statically with no fade/translateY entrance, and the destructive 'Invalid Link' state has no visual emphasis beyond text-destructive. Minor for an auth page but a missed signature moment. _(low)_; Typography: title/description use default Card component sizing rather than the fixed data scale. ---

## 4. Rollout Waves

- **Wave 0 — Foundation.** Fonts (IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Mono), the density tokens + `DensityProvider`/`QEDensityToggle`, and the canonical primitives (`QEButton`, `QECard`, `QEBadge`, `QEInput`, `Num/Pct/Price/Ticker`, `QETable` family, `QEShell`, `QESignalCard`). ESLint guard to forbid raw `ui/*` imports outside the QE layer. Nothing visual ships until this exists.
- **Wave 1 — Golden page: `home`.** Rebuild Home to perfection on the new kit. This is the reference every other page conforms to.
- **Wave 2 — P0 core trading surfaces** (stock-detail, gex-dashboard, market-scanner, flow-heatmap, terminal-heatmap, trade-desk, gex-scanner, client/src/pages/terminal-chart.tsx, home, gex-shell).
- **Wave 3 — P1** (30 surfaces).
- **Wave 4 — P2** (18 surfaces: auth, legal, marketing).

---

## 5. Signature 3D + Animated Product Shots

**Flagship 3D — `QEGammaSurface`** (`client/src/components/gex/qe-gamma-surface.tsx`): React-Three-Fiber GEX gamma surface — strike × expiry × netGEX terrain, cyan call-wall peaks, purple put-wall valleys, orange flip-plane. Lazy-loaded, `frameloop="demand"`, 2D `GEXExpiryMatrix` fallback under reduced-motion / no-WebGL. Reuses the strike×expiry data we already widened.

**Animated product shots (landing/home):** `client/src/components/landing/product-shot.tsx` — `<video autoPlay muted loop playsInline>` wrapper for **Screen Studio** captures of the real terminal (auto-zoom on clicks) staged in **Rotato** 3D device frames; poster-image fallback under reduced-motion. Plus Spline 3D hero option for the marketing page.

### Per-page 3D / motion opportunities (the menu — we pick winners together)

- **academy** — Apply the canonical micro-interaction only: card hover = border-brighten (border-border -> border-strong) + translateY(-1px) over 150-200ms on --ease-out-smooth, press scale(0.98). The existing img group-hover:scale-105 over 500ms is decorative drift — shorten to ~250ms and gate behind prefers-reduced-motion, or drop it for in-terminal consistency. No springs, no glow, no Aceternity. There is no data-viz here so no 3D opportunity; this surface should stay calm and content-forward.
- **analyze** — No 3D opportunity here (GEX surface is the only sanctioned 3D moment). Standardize micro-interactions: signal rows and suggestion cards get the canonical hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) over 150-200ms on var(--ease-out-smooth), press scale(0.98) (already global). Replace SuggestionCard's hover:brightness-110. Optionally stagger the terminal statusLines in with framer-motion opacity fade (150ms each, respect prefers-reduced-motion) to keep the streaming-terminal feel without ambient glow. Remove any reliance on .shadcn-card teal glow hover (off-law) if it bleeds in.
- **automations** — Add framer-motion to the bot/scanner/quick-action cards: whileHover { y: -1, borderColor: brand } and whileTap { scale: 0.98 } at 150-200ms on --ease-out-smooth, all gated by prefers-reduced-motion. Animate tab-content mount with the existing animate-page-in opacity fade. No 3D moment belongs here (the single R3F gamma surface is GEX-only) — keep this surface flat and fast.
- **backtest** — Add framer-motion 150-300ms ease-out-smooth: KPI cards stagger-fade on data load (animate-page-in already exists), Analyze button press scale(0.98) (global rule covers it), card hover = border-brighten + translateY(-1px) WITHOUT the teal glow shadow. Respect prefers-reduced-motion. No 3D here — the R3F GEX surface is the only 3D moment and does not belong on this page. Optional: price-tick flash util on the Change KPI when refetched.
- **analysis** — Standardize hover to the law's signature: border-brighten + translateY(-1px) over 150-300ms on var(--ease-out-smooth), press scale(0.98). Page already uses hover-elevate + the global .shadcn-card lift — keep it but remove the dark teal glow box-shadow so hover is border-brighten only. ChevronRight reveal-on-hover is fine. No 3D opportunity here (the RTF GEX gamma surface is the one signature 3D moment, not relevant to a launcher hub). prefers-reduced-motion already handled globally.
- **about** — P2 marketing page — NO new 3D and no Aceternity/spring effects (those are landing-only; this is borderline). Acceptable motion: framer-motion fade + translateY entrance on each <section>, staggered 150–300ms on --ease-out-smooth, gated on prefers-reduced-motion; standardize card hover to the law default border-brighten + translateY(-1px) by using real Card (shadcn-card) instead of glass-card. No ambient glow.
- **command** — Add framer-motion to the index-symbol buttons and timeframe pills: whileHover { y:-1, borderColor brighten } / whileTap { scale:0.98 } at 150ms on --ease-out-smooth, gated on prefers-reduced-motion. Apply the same hover-lift to the 4 QEPanel cards via the existing .shadcn-card pattern. Keep the probability/flow/horizon bars but animate width with the smooth ease (already partially done via duration-500). No ambient glow. This page already hosts the signature 3D moment indirectly through GEXVEXChart — if that component is the R3F gamma surface, leave its lazy-load + 2D fallback untouched; do not add a second 3D element here.

All transforms gated on prefers-reduced-motion.

Animate the detail rail in with a 200ms slide/fade. No new 3D here — the gamma-surface remains the single 3D moment in the sibling GEX matrix tab; this heatmap should stay flat and fast. Make the LIVE dot use the canonical pulse and pause it under reduced-motion.
- **btc-radar** — framer-motion micro-interactions only: card/row hover translateY(-1px)+border-brighten, press scale(0.98), 150–200ms on --ease-out-smooth, prefers-reduced-motion respected. No decorative glow, no springs, no Aceternity. No R3F here (GEX owns the one 3D moment).
